Liability of Executors and Trustees FORM 270-INDEMNIFICATION FOR LIABILITY ON CONTRACT OR IN TORT 

If any executor hereof or trustee hereunder shall be or become liable or subjected to any claims on contract or in tort by reason of any act or thing done or omitted to be done in good faith in the usual course of his duties as such executor or trustee, he shall be indemnified out of my estate or property of the trust, as the case may be, for all costs and expenses incurred and payments made by him in the settlement of such claims or liabilities either before or after judgment. 

FORM 271-EXECUTORS AND TRUSTEES RELEASED FROM LIABILITY 

It is my will and I hereby direct that my said executors and trustees shall not be held responsible or liable in any event for any loss which may occur by reason of the depreciation in value of the real estate or the securities held or owned by me at the time of my decease or in real estate which they may from time to time purchase or the securities in which they may from time to time invest the proceeds of sale of real or personal estate nor for any bond given by them for money they may borrow from time to time on bond and mortgage nor for any default which may accrue on the foreclosure of any such bond and mortgage. 

FORM 272-LIABILITY OF EXECUTORS AND TRUSTEES (ANOTHER FORM) 

Said executors and trustees shall not be responsible for any waste nor for loss and depreciation nor for the acts and omissions of each other and where in their judgment it is necessary to make advances for the protection of any of the securities held by them in cases of foreclosure of mortgages or otherwise they are hereby authorized to make such advances from the capital fund held by them as an investment of the same and to pay from capital any premium which may be required to be paid in the purchase of securities. 

Miscellaneous Provisions FORM 273-EXECUTORS AND TRUSTEES MAY ACT PROFESSIONALLY 

It shall be permissible and proper for an executor or trustee hereunder to render professional or business services to the estate and receive compensation therefor. 

FORM 274-EXECUTORS MAY BORROW FROM OR SELL TO TRUSTEE 

I hereby authorize and empower the executors hereof to borrow money from or to sell property of the estate to the trustees of any trust created by me even though said trustees may be the same persons as my executors herein appointed. 

FORM 275-POWER TO TAKE POSSESSION OF PROPERTY 

(a) Immediately after my death, I authorize and empower my executors to take into their possession all personal property, or evidence thereof, and all deeds of real property then owned by me, or other evidence of such ownership, and to hold the same pending the probate of my will, hereby giving and granting unto my said executors any and every power in the premises that I am capable of conferring upon them, jointly and severally, to act to the best of their ability, and within their respective discretions, in preserving the property and assets of which I may die seized and possessed, until my will shall have been probated; and (b) Any expenses incurred by them, or by either of them, in carrying out the provisions of the next immediately preceding paragraph of my will, I hereby charge upon the general assets of my estate as expenses of administration, and I hereby release and discharge my executors, and each of them, from any and all liability or responsibility, to my said estate, or to any person, persons, or corporation, whomsoever, or whatsoever, arising out of any act, or thing, done in accordance with the authority herein granted, or intended so to be.

FORM 276-ANY LOSS OF THE ESTATE OF ANOTHER TO BE BORNE BY TESTATOR 

Inasmuch as my wife and myself are executors and trustees under the will of the late___ and I have had practically the entire management and charge of his estate, it is my will and I direct that as soon as possible after my decease an accounting be had before the proper judicial tribunal, and any sum which may be charged by such tribunal upon or against said executors and trustees by reason of any loss upon investment made either by them or by myself, be paid exclusively out of my estate. . [55]

FORM 277-REQUIRING ASSENT TO PROVISIONS OF WILL 

I hereby declare that all provisions contained in this will, in favor of my heirs and next of kin, are in each case made upon the express condition that such heir or next of kin shall, within thirty (30) days after demand by those of my executors who are not my next of kin, or within such further time as may be allowed by such executors, execute in writing and deliver to the said executors a consent to the probate of this will, as a will of both real and personal property, a waiver of the issuance and service of a citation upon them respectively, in the matter of such probate, and also a duly executed Declaration or Agreement, in such form as shall be approved by my said executors, to the effect that such heir or next of kin assents to each and every provision, condition and limitation contained in this will. In case of the failure of any heir or next of kin to comply with such condition as aforesaid, I hereby revoke all provisions made by me in this will for his or her benefit, and I give, devise and bequeath the property given to such heir or next of kin by this will to my said trustees, in trust, however, to dispose of the same as part of my residuary estate.

Now therefore, as the wife of said.. 

I do hereby consent and assent to all the provisions of said will and accept the provisions thereof made for my benefit in lieu of and instead of any and all statutory provisions or other rights, which I might have in and to the property of my husband upon his decease, and I do hereby waive all such statutory and other rights in consideration of the provisions made for me in said will.

FORM 280-INCOME TO WIFE FOR LIFE 

To pay the net income of the trust fund in convenient installments per annum to my wife during the term of her natural life at which time this trust shall terminate and the principal of the trust fund shall be paid and distributed to my certain beneficiaries hereinafter named or their issue, per stirpes, entirely discharged from this trust.

FORM 281-INCOME TO WIFE DURING HER WIDOWHOOD, WITH REQUEST AS TO MAINTENANCE OF CHILDREN 

To pay to my said wife, or permit her to receive, the income of the said trust fund during her life, if she shall so long continue my widow; and I request my said wife out of such income, to maintain and edu- [56]

cate such of my sons as shall, for the time being, be under the age of twenty-one years and such of my daughters as shall, for the time being, be under that age and unmarried, but I declare that such request shall not impose any legal obligation on my said wife, and that she shall not be required to make any accounting in respect to any income so paid to or received by her.

FORM 286—ANNUITY TO WIFE 

To pay to the testator's wife, during her lifetime an annuity in an amount exclusive of any income she may receive from other sources, which will produce a total annual income to her of $----- The testator directs the executor and trustee to set aside as soon after his death as it shall deem advisable and in the best interest of his estate, a fund which in its absolute discretion it shall consider ample to produce such revenue in order to provide the aforesaid annuity. Until such fund is set aside and the income therefrom becomes available, the said annuity shall be paid out of the testator's general estate but when the executor and trustee shall have set aside such fund there shall be no further liability upon the remainder of the testator's estate for the payment of such annuity. 

If, in any year, after such fund has been set aside, the income from the same shall be insufficient to provide such annuity, any deficiency shall be made up out of the principal of such fund and if in any year the net income derived from such fund shall be more than sufficient to provide such annuity, any surplus income shall be added to the principal of the fund and invested as hereinafter provided.

FORM 288-TRUST FOR DAUGHTER WITH POWER OF APPOINTMENT AND GIFT 

OVER To pay the net income derived from the trust estate in convenient installments per annum to my said daughter, during the term of her natural life and upon her death, to pay over and distribute the principal of the trust estate to, or to continue to hold the same in trust for the benefit of all or any one or more of the issue of my said duaghter, such estates, interest or income and in such sums, shares or proportions and subject to such provisions for maintenance and education and other provisions, limitations and restrictions, in trust or otherwise as she may deem fit by deed or last will and testament, create or appoint and in default of the exercise of such power of testamentary appointment, the trustee is directed to treat the trust estate then remaining as being composed of as many equal shares as my daughter shall have issue living at the time of her decease and one such share shall be held for the benefit of each of said children. FORM 289-TRUST FOR SON WITH POWER OF APPOINTMENT AND GIFT OVER 

The trustee shall set aside one of such equal shares for each son of the testator alive at his death and keep the same invested and pay the income derived therefrom to such son until he attains the age of__ years when the principal of such share shall be paid or transferred to him entirely discharged from this trust. 

If such son shall die before becoming entitled to receive the principal of such share, then the same shall be held by the trustee in trust for the issue of such son, or some one or more of them in such proportions and subject to such conditions and terms as he by his last will and testament may direct, provided, that if such son shall leave a widow him surviving, he may by his last will and testament direct the income from the whole or any part of such share to be paid to his widow until her death or re-marriage, whichever shall first occur. In default of the exercise of such power of testamentary appointment, or insofar as the same shall be void or shall not extend or take effect, such share shall be held by the trustee for the issue of such son who survives him in equal shares per stirpes and not per capita. If such son shall leave no issue him surviving, then subject to any provision that may be made by such son, for his widow, in accordance with the terms of this paragraph, such share shall be held by the trustee in trust for the testator's issue alive at the death of such son, in equal shares per stirpes and not per capita upon the same trust as the testator may or shall have provided regarding the others of his issue alive at the time of his death. 

FORM 290-TRUST FOR CHILDREN 

My said trustee shall divide said property into as many equal shares as there are children of mine, me surviving, and children of mine, who shall have died before me leaving issue me surviving, and shall set apart one share for each of my surviving children and one share for the issue of each of my children who [58]

shall have died before me leaving issue me surviving; each share so set apart for the issue of any such deceased child shall be paid over and delivered by my trustee to such issue, his or their heirs and assigns, for his or their own use absolutely and forever, in equal portions, per stirpes and not per capita, but no principal shall be paid to any child until he or she has attained the age of twenty-one years; the income from such share to be paid to or for such child in the meantime. Each share so set apart for any child of mine, me surviving, shall be held, invested and re-invested by my said trustee, and my said trustee shall collect and receive the interest, income and profits of each such share to the child for whose benefit the same is set apart and held in trust, until such child shall reach the age of thirty years, or shall die, whichever shall happen first, at which time the principal of the share so set apart and held in trust shall be paid over and delivered to him or her; his or their heirs, executors, administrators and assigns, for his or her own use absolutely and forever.

FORM 294—TRUST FOR CHILDREN AND ISSUE OF DECEASED CHILDREN 

In trust for all such of my children living at my death, and of the children then living of any child of mine having died in my lifetime, as being a son attain the age of twenty-one years, or being a daughter attain that age or marry under that age, if more than one, as tenants in common, in equal shares per stirpes; so that my children who shall be objects of this trust shall take in equal shares, and the children being objects of this trust of any child of mine having died in my lifetime, shall take equally between them the share which the parent would have taken had he or she survived me. [59]

FORM 295-PROVISION FOR WIVES OR HUSBANDS OF CHILDREN DYING WITHOUT ISSUE 

In event of the death of any son or daughter at the time entitled to receive income hereunder, leaving no children of such son or daughter him or her surviving, but leaving a wife or husband him or her surviving, who was in being at the time of my decease, then in such event, such wife or husband as the case may be shall be entitled to receive (here insert such portion or all of the income as may seem suitable) the income theretofore payable to said son or daughter, as the case may be, for and during the life of such wife or husband.

FORM 296-INCOME DIVIDED AMONG DESCENDANTS EQUALLY PER STIRPES 

The said income shall be divided into so many equal shares as there shall be children of mine surviving me, counting as one such in fixing the number of shares, the lawful issue of any deceased child of mine, and one of said equal shares shall be paid to each one of my children who shall survive me for and during his or her natural life, and from and after his or her death to his or her lawful issue then living, if any, for and during their respective natural lives, to be equally divided among them if more than one in number, and in equal degree, and by representation of parents if in unequal degree, and from and after their deaths respectively to their respective lawful issue then living, if any, for and during their respective natural lives, the parents share to be equally divided among them if more than one in number and in equal degree, and by representation of parents if of unequal degree, and so on to my lineal descendants in succession for and during their natural lives respectively during the continuance of the trust. 

FORM 297—GIVING TO SONS RIGHT TO DEMAND CERTAIN PERCENTAGES OF PRIN- CIPAL OF TRUST AT CERTAIN AGES: GRANTING LIMITED POWER OF TESTAMENTARY APPOINTMENT 

Whenever any son of mine, whether at the time of my death or thereafter shall have attained the age of thirty years, he shall at his option, be entitled to receive and shall, on his written request, be paid by the trustees in money or securities belonging to the share representing him a sum equal to twenty-five per cent of the whole value of the securities constituting such share; on any son of mine attaining the age of thirtyfive years he shall be entitled to receive and shall on his written request be paid by my trustee in money or securities a further sum equal to twenty-five per cent of the whole value of the securities originally constituting the share representing such son; on any son of mine attaining the age of forty years he shall be entitled to receive and shall, on his written request, be paid by my trustees in money or securities, a further sum equal to twenty-five per cent of the whole value of the securities originally constituting the share representing such son and upon such son attaining the age of forty-five years so much of the share representing such son as shall remain in the hands of the trustee shall belong to and be paid and delivered by the trustees to such son. I also empower any son of mine to dispose by last will and testament of any part of the principal sum of the share representing him which he shall not have received up to the time of his death in favor of his wife or children, or on default of children, in favor of his brother and sisters or any one of them or the issue of any one of them and upon his death the share of the son so dying or that portion of it which has not been paid to him by the trustees according to the provisions of such will and in default of such will shall belong to and be distributed among his children if he shall leave children in equal shares and if he should die without leaving children the same shall be distributed among my children other than the son so dying and their issue share and share alike, the issue of any deceased child taking the share to which the parent would have been entitled if living. 

The payments hereinbefore authorized to be made to my said sons respectively, at the several periods hereinbefore specified shall not be obligatory upon the trustees unless expressly requested in writing by my said sons, respectively, and it is my wish that in view of the benefit to my sons respectively which will be exercised by the trustees in respect to the several shares to be held in trust by them, my said sons should not require the payment to them of the said proportionate sums of the principal of their respective shares until they shall arrive at the age of forty-five years except so far as such payment may be required for capital in any business in which they may be engaged. Regardless of the ages above mentioned the trustees may advance to any son out of the share representing him a sufficient sum for the purchase and furnishing of a dwelling house for his use and occupancy charging such advance against the distribution amounts yet to be received by such son.

The above expression of my wish shall in no wise limit the power of any son of mine to exercise the option hereinbefore given to him to require payment of the principal of the share representing him to the extent, in the manner and at the times hereinbefore specified. [60]

FORM 298—TRUST FOR SON'S WIDOW 

From and after the death of my said son, the trustee shall hold the trust fund upon terms and conditions as follows: to pay the income thereof to the present or any future wife of my said son who may happen to survive him for the term of her natural life or so long as she shall remain my son's widow but without power of anticipation, and after her death, the trust fund shall be paid and distributed to the issue of my said son, share and share alike, but no principal shall be paid to any of said issue until he or she has attained the age of twenty-one years, the income from such share to be paid to or for the use of said child in the meantime.

FORM 303-LIMITATION OF INCOME UPON MARRIAGE OF WIFE 

In case my wife shall marry after my decease then upon such event she shall receive only two-thirds (3) of the net income of my trust estate and the residue shall thereafter during the continuance of my wife's lifetime be paid and distributed, share and share alike to or for the use of my children, me surviving, or their issue, per stirpes. Upon the decease of my wife the trust estate shall be paid and distributed, share and share alike to my children, me surviving, or their issue, per stirpes, but no principal shall be paid to any child or issue until he or she has attained the age of twenty-one years. The income from such share to be paid to or for the use of such child in the meantime.

From said income of such trust estate in its charge, said trustee shall pay (here designate share of income to be paid) to‒‒‒‒‒‒‒‒‒‒ for and during the term of his (or her) natural life, but only upon the following express terms and conditions. In the event only of the mental incapacity of said.. the amount payable to him (or her) hereunder may be paid to a conservator duly appointed. 

FORM 307-PROHIBITION AGAINST ALIENATION 

Notwithstanding anything hereinbefore contained, I hereby direct that the income which by the terms hereof is or shall be payable to any person shall only be so payable until such person shall by specific or general assignment, transfer, or otherwise alienate or dispose of or attempt to alienate or dispose of his or her right to such income or any part thereof or interest therein or until by reason of or in consequence of or under any attachment, sequestration, execution, writ, judgment, order or other proceeding, or by any other means whatsoever the said income or any part thereof or interest therein can no longer be personally enjoyed by him or her, or would but for the terms of this agreement belong to or become vested in or subject to the control of some other person, persons or corporation, courts or officials, and from and after the happening of any such event the right of such person to receive and be paid the said income shall cease, but the trustee, in its absolute and uncontrolled discretion may pay over to or expend for the benefit of his wife and children, or her husband and children, as the case may be, the whole or any part of such income as it may deem fit during its pleasure; the balance (if any) of such income to be paid or applied by the trustee for the benefit of such child, grandchild or grandchildren of mine as the trustee, in its absolute and uncontrolled discretion deems fit; provided always that if and so soon as (but not before) the reason and cause whereby the said person can no longer personally enjoy the said income as above mentioned, shall come to or be at an end, then in such case such income shall again be paid over to him or her as before the happening of any of the events above mentioned, until the again happening of any such event, and so often as the same shall happen the right of the said person to be paid such income shall cease and the trustee shall again deal with such income in its absolute and uncontrolled discretion and otherwise as above provided for until the reason or cause whereby such person can no longer personally enjoy the said income as above mentioned, has again come to or is at an end as above mentioned, and then shall again pay over to him or her the said income as before, and so on from time to time as the case may be when and so often as any such event may happen and the reason or cause as aforesaid has come to or is at an end as above mentioned. 

FORM 308-PROHIBITION AGAINST ALIENATION (ANOTHER FORM) 

The gifts of income hereinbefore made to my daughters and their issue shall not be alienated, disposed of, or in any manner incumbered by them, or any of them while so in possession and control of the trustee; and if any of said beneficiaries shall, at any time or times, alienate, charge or dispose of their said respective incomes, or any part thereof, or any interest therein, before the same shall have been delivered to them under the provisions of this instrument; or if, by reason of their bankruptcy or other event happening either before or after my death, said income otherwise intended for said beneficiaries or any of them shall wholly or in part cease to be enjoyed by them or any of them, as above provided, and the same, or any part thereof, or any interest therein, shall, or but for this proviso would, become vested in some other person or persons, then the trust hereinbefore expressed concerning said income shall thereupon cease and determine as to the beneficiary whose interest may be so affected, and all income otherwise hereinbefore provided for such beneficiary shall thereafter be held and distributed by the trustee during the residue of the life of such beneficiary according to the absolute discretion of the trustee, but the trustee may pay to such beneficiary, or for his or her maintenance and support, or to the husband, child or children of such beneficiary, thereafter from such income, such sums, and such sums only, as it, in its absolute discretion shall think fit and proper, using or retaining any unexpended sums for the benefit of any one or more of the beneficiaries hereunder whose interest is not so affected. [63]

FORM 309-PROHIBITION AGAINST ALIENATION (ANOTHER FORM) 

And in case any of the beneficiaries to whose use any rents, interest, income, or profits of real or personal estate are to be applied should attempt to transfer, convey or incumber the same, or if any person should by any proceeding in the law, attach or seize the same by any execution or proceeding in equity which would otherwise attach the same, then the rents and incomes shall, while such transfer, incumbrance or proceeding remain unremoved, be applied from time to time to the use of the persons who would be entitled presumptively to the property, if the beneficiary were deceased, excepting so much as is necessary for his or her support. 

FORM 310-TRUST FOR SON FOR LIFE, WITH PROVISO FOR CESSER ON ALIENA- TION OF HIS INTEREST 

In trust that my trustees or trustee shall pay the income of the trust fund to my son, or permit him to receive the same during his life, subject to the proviso that, if the said son should at the time of my death have alienated, charged, or disposed of, or if he shall, at any time or times thereafter alienate, charge or dispose of, the said income or part thereof or interest therein, or if, by reason of his bankruptcy or other event happening either before or after my death, the said income shall, wholly or in part fail or cease to be personally enjoyed by the said son, but the same or any part thereof shall, or, but for this present proviso, would belong to, or become vested in, some other person or persons, then the trust hereinbefore expressed concerning the said income shall immediately thereupon cease and determine, and the same income shall be held by my trustees or trustee during all the then residue of the life of the said son in the manner following, that is to say:

FORM 311-PROTECTIVE TRUST 

If, from any alienation or attempted alienation of any interest in or right to receive principal and/or income under a trust of my estate by a beneficiary thereunder, or if from any cause whatsoever the said principal and/or income, or any part thereof, shall, or but for this proviso would, at any time, become payable to or pass to or for the benefit of any person, other than such beneficiary, then the interest in and the right of such beneficiary to receive such principal and/or income shall cease and determine; and thereafter the said principal and/or income, or such part thereof as shall become so forfeited by such beneficiary, shall be applied as determined by the trustees in their uncontrollable discretion to the use of the wife, husband or descendants of such beneficiary or beneficiaries (or to the-- ---Society for--- --) in such manner and portions as to my said trustees shall seem best; and 

Provided further, that notwithstanding any forfeiture by any beneficiary as aforesaid, my said trustees in their uncontrollable discretion, but without any obligation so to do, may from time to time apply or direct the application of the said portion of said principal and/or income forfeited as aforesaid, or so much thereof as seems to it best, to the use of the beneficiary so forfeiting the same. 

FORM 312-DISCRETIONARY TRUST OF INCOME AFTER ALIENATION 

Upon trust during the residue of the life of the said son, or during such shorter period, continuous or discontinuous, as my trustees or trustee shall in their or his absolute discretion think fit, to pay and apply the said income or any part thereof, to and for the personal support and maintenance, or otherwise for the benefit, of the said son or his wife, child, or children, or remoter issue, for the time being, if any, whether infants or of full age, of the said son, or such one or more, exclusively of the others or other of such persons, and in such manner as my trustees or trustee, in their or his absolute discretion, shall think fit. 

FORM 313-TRUST FOR SON FOR LIFE, DETERMINABLE ON BANKRUPTCY 

In trust, that my trustees or trustee shall pay the income of the trust fund to my son, unless and until he shall have done, committed, or suffered, or shall at any time or times after my death do, commit, or suffer, any act, default or thing which would have the effect of vesting the right to receive such income, if absolutely belonging to my said son, or in any other person or persons whomsoever. 

And from and after the failure or determination, during the life of the said son, of the trust or income hereinbefore expressed in his favor, my trustees or trustee shall hold the trust fund and the future income thereof upon the trusts and for the purposes upon and for which the same would be applicable respectively under this my will if the said son were then actually dead. And from and after the death of the said son, etc. [64]

FORM 314-LIFE INTEREST DETERMINABLE ON BANKRUPTCY, ETC. 

Upon trust that if, at the time of this present trust taking effect in possession, the said. would not, by reason of any antecedent alienation or charge, or the happening of any other event, whether before or after my decease, be wholly or partially prevented from personally enjoying the life interest hereby given to him in the said trust premises, if the same were given to him absolutely, then my trustees shall pay the income of the said trust premises unto the said--- ---during his life, or until he shall become bankrupt, or shall alienate, or charge, or attempt to charge the said income, or some part thereof, or until some other event shall happen whereby, if the said income or any part thereof belonged to him absolutely, he would be wholly or partially deprived of the personal enjoyment thereof. 

FORM 315-LIFE INTEREST DETERMINABLE ON BANKRUPTCY-SHORT FORM 

And shall pay the income of the said trust premises to the said.. during his life, unless and until some event shall have happened or shall happen whereby, if the said income belonged absolutely to him, he would be deprived of the personal enjoyment thereof or of any part thereof; and afterward, during the remainder of his life, shall hold the said income upon trusts and for the purposes upon and for which the same would for the time be held if the said…. were dead.

FORM 316-SAFEGUARDING FUND AND INCOME FROM CREDITORS FOR BENEFI- CIARIES 

No title in the trust estate hereby created or in the income accruing therefrom or in its accumulation shall vest in any beneficiary, and no beneficiary shall have the right or power to transfer, assign, anticipate or encumber his or her interest in said estate or the income therefrom prior to the actual distribution thereof by said trustee to said beneficiary, and neither the income nor principal shall be liable in any manner in the possession of the trustee for the debts, contracts, or engagements of any of the beneficiaries. No beneficiary shall be permitted to appoint any agent or attorney in fact, except as may be specifically permitted by the trustee, to collect or receive such income or principal, if such agent or attorney in fact has directly or indirectly advanced any of such income or principal to such beneficiary. 

Discretionary Powers Relative to Minor's Interest

FORM 317—CARE OF MINOR'S ESTATE 

In case any beneficiaries hereunder shall be minors at the time they become entitled to any portion of my estate, I direct that during their respective minorities the executors hereof, or trustees hereunder, shall retain possession of their respective portions of my estate and invest, care for and apply the same and the income thereof through their parents or guardians, or directly after the manner of a guardian of an infant's estate, with all the powers herein granted to them as executors and/or trustees and with the same compensation for services as is allowed by law to a guardian and the receipt of such parent or guardian or evidence of such application shall constitute a complete acquittance for such payments. Provided, however, that nothing herein contained shall be construed to prevent or postpone the vesting in such minors, or to suspend the alienability or ownership of such property.

FORM 318-CARE OF MINOR'S ESTATE (ANOTHER FORM) 

Should under the provisions of this will, any part of the principal of my estate become payable to any person before he or she shall have attained the age of twenty-one years, then I direct that said part of the principal of my estate be retained by my executors in trust to collect the income therefrom and to apply the net income thereof, or as much as my said executors may deem necessary, to the education, maintenance and support of such person during his minority and to accumulate so much of such income as shall not be so applied, and upon such person attaining his majority, to pay over to him such accumulations together with the principal of his share. Should such person die before attaining his majority the principal together with accumulations, if any, *(shall be disposed of as such person shall by his last will direct, and in default of such testamentary disposition, then it) shall go to the persons then surviving, and in the proportions and in the manner provided by the then laws of the State of---. for the distribuion of his personal property had he died intestate. *Use of this phrase of no value unless minor resides in state which permits minors to make a will. [65]

If either son shall die before me or before completing his preparatory school and university or college courses, to pay out of the income of such trust fund, and if necessary out of the principal thereof, all sums necessary for the proper education in preparatory school and university or college of the survivor.

FORM 320-TRUSTEE TO PAY INCOME AT DISCRETION FOR MAINTENANCE AND EDUCATION 

Upon the death of my wife, my trustee shall forthwith and immediately pay over direct the net income derived from my residuary to, or at its option expend on behalf of my son, trust estate, in amounts and at the times which in its judgment and discretion shall be beneficial and requisite for the proper maintenance, care, education, comfort and pursuits of my said son. If my wife shall die before my son, and in the further event of the death of my son leaving lawful issue, prior to his reaching the age of twenty-five years, I then direct that my trustee shall, until such child or children become of legal age, pay the net income or any portion thereof of my residuary trust estate for the maintenance, care, education and comfort of such minor or minors as in the judgment of the trustee is reasonably required for the same during each year until the minor or minors become of legal age. The part of such income not paid over in any year shall be retained and invested, but shall be by the trustee treated as income to be drawn for the benefit of such minor or minors in any subsequent year until they become of legal age. 

FORM 321-IF DAUGHTER PREDECEASE TESTATOR SHARE TO HER ISSUE, IN TRUST, TO APPLY INCOME DURING MINORITY 

In the event that either of my said daughters shall have died before me, leaving issue me surviving, I give, devise and bequeath to such children, to be divided equally among them, when and as they respectively attain the age of twenty-one years, that share, or part, of my estate, which, had their mother survived me, would have been held under the foregoing provisions of my will in trust for her benefit during her life; and, during their respective minorities, their respective portions shall be held by my said trustees in trust to receive the rents, profits, and income thereof, and to apply the net income to their education and support, accumulating any part of the net income not required for those purposes, and paying over the accumulations to the respective beneficiaries when they become of age. 

FORM 322-PROVISION FOR MAINTENANCE AND EDUCATION OF MINORS 

The trustees shall, until any minor distributee or beneficiary becomes of legal age, pay in and about the maintenance, education, care and comfort of such minor, the whole or such part of the income of the share of such minor (and of the corpus as hereinabove provided) as in the judgment of the trustees is reasonably required for the due maintenance, education, care and comfort of such minor in each year until said minor becomes of legal age. The part of such income not paid in any year by the trustee shall be by it retained and invested, but shall be subject to be by said trustee treated as income to be drawn upon for the benefit of said minor in any subsequent year until said minor becomes of legal age. 

And the voucher of the person in whose lawful custody each such minor may be from time to time, to whom such money may be paid, shall be a sufficient voucher for the trustee. Such payments may be made without the intervention of any guardian or of any court or of any other person than such payee, but the trustee may in its discretion require such reports and take such steps as it may deem requisite to assure [66]

and enforce the due application of such money to the purposes aforesaid. Any of said income not so paid over by said trustee before such minor becomes of legal age shall upon his or her arrival at such age be paid over to him or to her.

FORM 323-ACCUMULATION FOR MINOR 

The trustees shall accumulate the net income of said trust, investing and re-investing the same until shall attain the age of twenty-one years, if (he) shall so long live. Upon (his) attaining such age said accumulated income, or the securities wherein the same shall then be invested, shall be paid over, transferred and delivered to said beneficiary; and the net income shall thereafter be paid to said beneficiary until (he) shall become entitled to the principal thereof as herein provided. The trustees may, however, in their discretion from time to time during the minority of said beneficiary pay and or apply to and for (his) support, care and education the accumulated and accruing net income, in whole or in part, in such manner and to such extent as they shall deem to be for said beneficiary's interest. 

FORM 324-PROVISION FOR ACCUMULATION 

During the minority of any of my grandchildren for whose benefit provision has been made in the foregoing six clauses of this will, the income, issues and profits received by said trustee for the benefit of said beneficiaries, respectively, shall be added to the principal held by said trustee for such beneficiaries, respectively, and be invested and re-invested, loaned and re-loaned by said trustee for the purpose of making the best available profit thereon, until such beneficiaries shall become of age, and if all of my grandchildren for whom provision has been made in the foregoing clauses of this will shall die without descendants surviving, then and in that event, all of said property so held in trust for them under said foregoing clauses of this will shall fall into and become a part of the residuum of my estate and be disposed of in accordance with the provisions of this will hereinafter set forth affecting the residuum of my estate. 

FORM 325-ACCUMULATION OF INCOME UNDER TRUST DEED WITH PROVISION FOR ACCELERATION TO COME WITHIN STATUTE 

The provisions hereof for retention and reinvestment of income or any part thereof by the trustee shall remain in full force and effect for and during the life of the donor and for a period of twenty-one years thereafter except as herein otherwise specifically provided, and in event of the minority of any beneficiary, also for and during the minority of such beneficiary, although such period may extend beyond the period of twenty-one years, and in event and only in event that any accumulation of income herein provided shall be in excess of such periods then and in each such instance the income from and after the expiration of such period on any fund held by the trustee shall be paid to or held for the use and benefit of and subject to the order of the beneficiary then entitled to receive any part of and at any future time entitled to receive, if surviving, the entire income of such fund, to the end that no such income directed to be accumulated beyond such permissible period shall remain or become undisposed of hereunder, but that the time of distribution of the entire income shall be accelerated. Nothing herein contained shall require distribution of principal accumulated from income during a period permissible by law save and except to such amount as the law applicable may require distribution thereof. 

FORM 326-SHARES OF MINOR MAY BE APPLIED, PAID TO GUARDIANS OR ACCUMU- 

LATED It is further my will that said trustees may, in their discretion, pay over any sums due to minors, under this trust, to the properly constituted guardians of said minors, or may hold such sums in their possession, investing and keeping invested the same, until the arrival of said minors respectively at majority, when the said sums, with their accumulations, shall be paid over to the person or persons entitled thereto. 

FORM 327-TRUSTEES TO RETAIN PROPERTY OF MINORS AND ACCUMULATE AT ITS DISCRETION 

If it shall happen that the beneficiary of any trust hereinbefore created to whom the trustee is directed to pay over the income of the trust fund shall be a minor, I direct that the trustee of such trust shall, during the minority of such beneficiary, instead of paying over the income to such beneficiary, apply to his or her support, maintenance and education so much of the income of the trust as such trustee shall in its absolute discretion deem proper, accumulating for the benefit of such minor the surplus income not so applied, and paying over to such beneficiary on his or her attaining the age of twenty-one years all such surplus with its accumulations. Any income thus directed to be applied to the support, maintenance and education of a minor may be either directly so applied by the trustee, or in its discretion may be paid over by it to the parent or guardian of the person of such minor, and the receipt of such parent or guardian shall be a sufficient voucher and discharge to such trustee for all payments so made by it. [67]

FORM 328-UNITED HOLDING OF MINORS' SHARES SEVERED AS EACH BECOMES OF AGE 

It is my will that the property which shall constitute the shares of my children who are minors at my death shall, at the discretion of said executors, be held and managed as a joint property during the minority of said children, and that the actual allotment or designation of the particular items or portions of my estate which shall make up the share of each minor child shall not be made, except at the discretion of said executors until, successively, as each minor child attains his or her majority; whereupon, in each case the equal part or share to be allotted to the child then becoming of age shall be separated by the designation of the particular items which said executors (or the trustees then acting as hereinafter provided), shall then select to constitute said equal part or share; a fair and just appraisement of the joint estate of said minors being made in each case as hereinbefore provided, to determine the true and proper equal share of the joint estate to be allotted to the minor then becoming of age. 

FORM 329-DISCRETIONARY TRUST TO APPLY INCOME FOR BENEFIT OF LIFE TENANT AND HIS FAMILY 

And in the event of the failure or determination during the life of the said_-_- ____of the trust lastly hereinbefore declared in his favor, my trustees shall, during the remainder of his life, pay or apply all or any part of the said income unto or for the personal support or benefit of the said. and his wife and issue (if any) and the persons or person for the time being interested in the said trust premises, under the ulterior trusts hereinafter declared, in such shares and manner as my trustees shall from time to time in their absolute discretion, think proper, and, subject to such discretionary trust or power, my trustee shall hold the said income upon the trusts upon which the same would for the time being be held if the said were then dead.

FORM 330-DISCRETION IN TRUSTEES TO APPLY SURPLUS INCOME AS THEY THINK BEST AMONG LINEAL DESCENDANTS 

In case my trustee should at any time be of the opinion that the income applicable to the use of any beneficiary, except my said wife, is more than sufficient for his or her suitable support and maintenance, I authorize and empower my said trustees, in their absolute and uncontrolled discretion, to apply so much of the surplus income as they may think best to the use of any of my lineal descendants, in such shares and proportions, whether equal or unequal, as my said trustees may, in their absolute and uncontrolled discretion, deem advisable.

FORM 331-INCOME ACCRUED AT TERMINATION OF AN ESTATE, BUT NOT PAID, TO BELONG TO HOLDER OF NEXT EVENTUAL ESTATE 

Income accrued but not due at the time of the termination of any estate hereunder shall when due and received, belong and be payable to the beneficiary entitled to the next eventual estate.

Revocation and Encroachment Powers FORM 338-TRUST FOR WIFE WITH RIGHT OF REVOCATION

Desiring at the same time to give my said wife the fullest measure of control, and to afford her both technical and experienced advice and guidance and to relieve her of the burden of details of administration and importunities to make unwise investments to which she might alone and unaided, be the more frequently and persistently subjected, I will and direct that the____ of------ 

City State as trustee, and any successor thereto, referred to herein as the corporate trustee, shall be the custodian and depositary of the trust estate, and that no transaction shall be consummated with reference to the trust estate, save upon consultation with an executive officer of such corporate trustee and, in the event that my wife shall elect to modify the powers of the corporate trustee, and enlarge her own powers as hereinafter stated, no transaction disapproved of by the corporate trustee on such consultation shall be consummated until my wife shall, in writing, have stated to the corporate trustee her reasons for carrying such transaction to consummation. Subject to such limitations my said wife may at any time and from time to time by declaration in writing by her signed and acknowledged and delivered to the corporate trustee, modify and change the powers of herself and the corporate trustee, with the same effect as if the provisions by her made were embodied herein, provided, however, that whether it be so stated in any such declaration or not, any provision thereof shall be and remain subject to subsequent modification or revocation by my said wife. 

Subject to the foregoing, the powers of the trustees hereunder shall be as follows: (Here insert the usual clauses clothing the trustee with power to properly administer estate.) 

My said wife may either for an indefinite period (but subject to revocation at her election), or for a specified period, or with reference to a transaction or transactions of a character to be by her specified, by a writing by her executed and acknowledged, delegate to the corporate trustee sole power to exercise all of the powers herein given to the trustees. My said wife may also at any time, and successively, from time to time, by an instrument in writing by her signed and acknowledged, remove the corporate trustee, provided she shall appoint in the same instrument and secure thereto the acceptance of a trust company authorized to do business in the State of___ as a successor as corporate trustee. By giving notice at least six months in advance in writing to the corporate trustee of her intention so to do, and after the expiration of such six months in writing requesting transfer to her personally and free of any of the trusts hereof, my wife shall have the power to elect to have transferred to her, and to have transferred to her by bill of sale or quit claim deed, by the corporate trustee, any part of the trust estate, real, personal or mixed, which she shall specify in such notice and request, which transfer shall be made free from and without such property being in any further measure subject to any of the trusts or terms Lereof.

Upon the death of my said wife the balance of said trust estate then remaining subject to the trusts hereof shall be disposed of in accordance with the terms hereof as the same may then exist, but as modified as herein provided by my said wife, if she shall have elected so to do, provided, however, that my said wife, if she so elects may by a last will and testament duly admitted to probate within one year after her decease, direct and appoint to what persons and on what terms and trusts the said balance of said trust estate hereunder shall be disposed of and distribution shall be made thereof in accordance with such will, except, however, that if after the execution of such will my said wife shall made a modification of the terms of the trusts hereof in conflict with such prior will, such modification shall prevail and be effective in preference to the terms of such prior will. The necessity and propriety of such withdrawals, and the amount thereof, shall be determined by the trustee and its determination shall be final; or The existence of the need of such withdrawals and the amount thereof shall be determined solely by my wife, but only after due consultation with the trustee and her determination shall be final if made after such consultation.

FORM 345-PROVISION FOR WIFE DURING LIFE, EVEN IF PRINCIPAL IS INVADED -FUNERAL FOR WIFE-REMAINDER TO SISTERS AND NIECE

If in the judgment of my said trustee, said net income is not sufficient to meet the living expenses of my said wife according to the style and fashion in which we have lived together prior to my death or should misfortune or sickness cause her expenses to increase, then and in such events my said trustee is authorized to pay my said wife, in addition to the income from my said estate, such portions of the principal as may be necessary under the circumstances. My said trustee is given the sole right to determine when payments of principal sum shall be made and the amounts of such payments. 

At the death of my said wife, my said trustee shall pay from said trust fund the funeral expenses of my said wife and such incidental indebtedness as she may leave, and which in the judgment of my said trustee shall be paid from said trust fund. FORM 346-TO PAY PROPER SUMS TO EACH CHILD FOR SUPPORT, MAINTENANCE, EDUCATION AND ADVANCEMENT 

Notwithstanding anything herein contained my trustees shall have power to pay to or apply to or for the support, maintenance and education of each of my children such sum or sums as to them may seem proper and in addition to make advances to any son or daughter on his or her expectant share of my estate for the purpose of education, settlement in life or otherwise as to them may seem proper. In case any son of mine shall die before he has received any portion of the corpus of my estate, without leaving any child or children but leaving a widow him surviving, my trustees are empowered to make such payments to her annually or otherwise as they in their absolute discretion may deem wise. 

Powers and Duties of Trustee FORM 347—FULL POWER TO TRUSTEE FOR MANAGEMENT OF ESTATE 

I hereby grant to my said executors and trustees, with respect to any and all property, whether real or personal, of which I am the owner at the time of my death, or which shall at any time constitute part of my estate, or of any trust created hereunder, the following powers: (a) To retain such property as an investment without regard to the proportion such property or property of a similar character, so held, may bear to the entire amount of my estate or any trust created hereunder. [72]

(b) To sell such property at either public or private sale for cash or on credit; to exchange such property and to grant options for the purchase thereof. (c) To invest and re-invest in any property including but not by way of limitation, bonds, notes, debentures, mortgages, certificates of deposit, common and preferred stocks, and shares or interests in investment trusts, without being limited to the class of securities in which trustees are authorized by law or any rule of court to invest trust funds. (d) To consent to and participate in any plan of reorganization, consolidation, merger, combination, or other similar plan, and to consent to any contract, lease, mortgage, purchase, sale or other action by any corporation pursuant to such plan. (e) To deposit any such property with any protective, reorganization or similar committee, to delegate discretionary power thereto, and to pay part of its expenses and compensation and any assessments levied with respect to such property. (f) To exercise all conversion, subscription, voting and other rights of whatsoever nature pertaining to any such property, and to grant proxies, discretionary or otherwise, in respect thereof. (g) To borrow money and to pledge or mortgage any such property. (h) To extend the time of payment of any obligation held by them and to compromise, settle or submit to arbitration upon such terms as to them may seem proper, or to release, any claim in favor of or against my estate or any trust created hereunder. (i) To hold the corpus of two or more trusts created hereunder in one or more consolidated funds in which the separate shares shall have undivided interests. (j) To cause any securities or other property held by them to be registered and held at their own risk in the name of a nominee. (k) To apportion stock and extraordinary dividends received, and charges incurred between income and principal, which apportionment shall fully protect my said executors and trustees with respect to any action taken or payments made in reliance thereon. (1) To satisfy any general legacy or any part thereof, including any legacy to themselves as trustees, in kind; and in the division or distribution of my estate or any trust fund, or any part thereof, including any distribution to themselves as trustees, to make partition, division or distribution of property in kind; and for any such purpose to determine the value of any such property, which determination shall be conclusive and not subject to review. (m) To collect and receive any and all sums and any and all property of whatsoever kind or nature due or owing or belonging to my estate or any trust created under this will, and to give full discharge and acquittance therefor. (n) To do all such acts, take all such proceedings, and exercise all such rights and privileges, although not hereinbefore specifically mentioned, with relation to such property as if the absolute owner thereof and in connection therewith to enter into any covenants or agreements binding my estate or any trust created hereunder.

FORM 348-GENERAL POWERS TO TRUSTEE FOR MANAGEMENT OF ESTATE— SHORT FORM 

(a) The trustee shall receive, hold and manage all the property as a trust fund, with full power to retain, sell, mortgage, improve, transfer or lease forever or a day, and to exchange all or any part of said property as though the absolute owner thereof and without order of court, and to collect, receive and recover the rents, issues, income and profits thereof. (b) The trustee is granted full power to invest and re-invest money coming into its possession in such loans, stocks, bonds, securities, and real estate as it may deem proper and suitable for the investment of trust funds without being restricted to a class of investments which a trustee is or may be permitted by law to make. (c) The trustee is further authorized and empowered to make distribution in kind upon such terms as to it may seem just; to determine whether money or property shall be treated as principal or income, and to settle and adjust all claims for or against the estate, all without order of court and as though the absolute owner thereof.

FORM 349—A COMPLETE SET OF POWERS FOR HANDLING REALTY 

I do hereby grant to and confer upon the trustees from time to time acting hereunder in addition to the powers conferred upon them hereunder, the following additional powers, authority and discretion with relation to any real property at any time held as part of any trust created hereunder, each of which powers, authority and discretion shall be deemed to apply both to said real property as a whole or to any part or parts thereof or undivided interests therein, to-wit: To sell said property at either public or private sale, [73]

. at such times and upon such terms and conditions as to credit and all other matters, as they may deem proper; to exchange, or grant options to purchase said property; to lease said property for such term or terms, whether five years or more or less than five years, and whether or not any such term may extend beyond the period of any trust created under this will, without application to any court, and with such options to the lessee of renewal or purchase and such covenants with respect to any improvements erected or which may be erected upon said property, and upon such other terms and conditions, as said trustees may deem proper; to mortgage said property for the purpose of taking up any mortgage to which any of said property may at any time be subject, or of repairing or improving any of said property or of paying any assessments or other charges thereon, or for any other purpose connected with the management of said property or with the administration of any trust created under this will, and to make, execute and deliver good and sufficient bonds and mortgages upon any of said property to secure the same, upon such terms as to time of payment of principal and rate of interest, and upon such other terms and conditions, as said trustees may deem proper; to pay off and liquidate any mortgage or mortgages to which such real property may at any time be subject and to use any other property, real or personal constituting part of the principal of any trust created under this will for that purpose; to partition said property wherever a division or distribution is required to be made under the terms of this will, and for such purpose to determine the value of said property and of any part or parts thereof, such determination to be conclusive upon all persons interested in the trust, and not to be subject to review; to enter into agreements of partition with any person or persons or representatives of any such person or persons with whom they may own any interest in such real property in common or jointly, and to make, execute and deliver and to accept deeds partitioning such property, and to pay any sums necessary for equality of partition from any other principal funds then in their hands as trustees; to make repairs and alterations and improvements on and to said property and to determine whether the cost of such repairs and improvements shall be chargeable wholly to the principal or to the income of any trust, or shall be chargeable partly to principal and partly to income and to apportion such charges between principal and income; to appoint agents to act in their behalf, to delegate to such agents discretionary power and to compensate such agents from the income of such real property or from any other trust property which the trustees may from time to time have in their hands; to effect insurance upon said property; to procure insurance against public liability or such other risks arising from the ownership of said property as may be insurable to them individually, and to charge the premiums thereon against either principal or income of any trust created under this will, as the trustees may determine; to pay all taxes, assessments, water rates and other expenses incidental to the administration of said property; to compromise, adjust and settle or to release, upon such terms as to them may seem proper, any claim or claims which they may at any time have against any person or persons, firm or firms or corporations, growing out of or relating to contracts made in reference to, or injuries done upon, said property, and if any person, firm or corporation shall at any time assert any claim or claims against the trustees or against any part of the property held by them as trustees, to compromise, adjust and settle any such claim or claims upon such terms as to them may seem proper; and generally to exercise, as in their absolute judgment shall seem advisable for the benefit of the said trust, with respect to any and all property which may at any time be in their hands or under their control as trustees thereunder, all rights, powers and privileges of every name and nature which might or could be exercised by one owning such property absolutely and in his own right. 

The foregoing powers which are hereinabove granted to and conferred upon the said trustees shall survive the vesting of title to said property in any remainderman or remaindermen and may be exercised over the property in part or as a whole for the purpose of better administering any trust which may continue as to a portion of said property, or for effecting any distribution of principal directed by any trust created under this will.

FORM 351-POWERS AS TO MANAGEMENT OF ESTATE 

The trustee hereunder, its successor and successors, in trust shall have full power and authority to receive, take, collect, receipt for and hold said property to it hereby given in trust, and to compromise, adjust and settle all claims to or against such property, at such amounts and upon such terms of payment as it deems best, and to invest and re-invest said trust estate, convert real estate into personalty; and personalty into real estate; to sell, or for such time as it deems for the best interests of the estate to retain unsold, any stocks, bonds or securities; to make alterations of, repairs upon, additions to, and to erect improvements upon real estate and to sell, join in or make partition of any real estate, to lease or otherwise dispose of real estate, and to create or grant easements with respect to real estate, and to make or vacate plats or sub-divisions of real estate (in any case the purchaser from the trustee shall not be bound to see to the application of the purchase money), and to make leases of any real estate for such term as the trustee may deem advisable, the trustee being given express power to make a lease for a term that may extend beyond the term of the trusts hereof, if it deems such course advisable. 

FORM 352–FULL POWERS TO TRUSTEE TO SELL TRUST PERSONAL PROPERTY AND WITH CONSENT OF BENEFICIARIES TO SELL REALTY 

I hereby authorize and empower the trustee for the time being acting under this will, to sell and convey such parts of the trust premises held by it as the interest of the trust or the advantages of those interested seem to require, the advisability of selling any personal property held hereunder to be left to the absolute and uncontrolled discretion of the trustee, but in the case of real estate the trustee hereunder shall, before making any sale of any real property held by it in trust, report the matter of such sale in writing to the beneficiaries at the time entitled to receive income hereunder, who are of lawful age and not adjudged incompetent or of unsound mind, which report shall be sent by mail, addressed to the last address registered by such beneficiary with the trustee, and if the trustee secures the consent in writing of a majority in interest of such beneficiaries, or no one of such adult beneficiaries shall deliver objections in writing to the trustee to the proposed sale within ten days after such report is so mailed, the trustee may make such proposed sale, but as to the grantee or grantees any sale shall be valid and effectual without evidence of such assent of record or otherwise.

FORM 353-TO IMPROVE REAL ESTATE 

In case any of the lands devised or appointed for life shall need to be improved by the erection or repairing or altering of buildings, or the payment of assessments for permanent or durable improvements, the personal property held for the same life tenant or party entitled, may be applied by the trustees thereof, toward such improvement as an investment thereof. And furthermore, all taxes, ordinary repairs and ordinary charges upon the lands and property given for the benefit of my daughters respectively, either in this will or by the settlements on their marriage, and other deeds above referred to, shall be paid out of the income of the estates and property held in trust for them respectively. Also premiums of insurance against fire, so far as the life tenants or the trustees may desire such insurance, but the insurance monies in case of loss shall be applied to replacing or rebuilding what is damaged or destroyed. 

FORM 354-RECOMMENDATION THAT REAL ESTATE SHOULD REMAIN UNSOLD 

I declare it to be my earnest request and recommendation (but not so as to interfere with the legal effect and operation of the trust for sale herein contained, or otherwise to be legally binding or restrictive), that under no circumstances shall any part of my real estate be sold during the lifetime of my said wife, or during the minority of any of my children, or for the period of________years and that such real estate be not afterward sold unless or until, in the judgment and discretion of the said trustees or trustee, it shall be requisite for the purpose of administering my estate, or desirable or expedient with a view to the interest and advantages of the persons entitled to or interested in my estate, that such sale should take place. 1 [75]

FORM 355-SECURITIES BELONGING TO TESTATOR TO FORM A PART OF TRUST FUNDS, WITH POWER TO CHANGE 

It is my will that all stocks, bonds and other securities belonging to me shall form part of the trusts created by my will at such value as may be placed upon the same by my executors and trustees, but they may sell and dispose of the same, or any of them whenever in their discretion they think proper. And I expressly direct that my executors and trustees are not to be responsible or liable for or charged with any loss or depreciation that may arise from the sale or retention of such securities, or any other securities forming part of any of the trusts created hereby. And I empower them to make investments and re-investments of trust monies in such other securities besides those recognized by law as they may think proper. 

FORM 356-POWER OF TRUSTEE TO POSTPONE SALE AND CONVERSION OF REAL AND PERSONAL ESTATE 

Provided always, and I hereby declare, that it shall be lawful for the said trustees and the survivor of them, their successors or successor, or the trustees or trustee for the time being, to defer and postpone the sale, conversion and collection of the whole or any part or parts of my said real and personal estate, respectively, so long as such trustees or trustee shall, in their or his discretion, deem proper, but my real estate shall, for the purpose of transmission, be impressed with the quality of personalty from the time of my death; and I empower my said trustees and trustee, during such interval or postponement, to manage and let or cultivate my real and leasehold estates, and to make any outlay such trustees and trustee may consider proper for improvements, repairs, premiums on policies, or otherwise, for the benefit or in respect of my real or personal estate.

FORM 357-TO SELL; LEASE FOR 21 YEARS; POWER EXTENDED TO SUCCESSOR TRUSTEES 

It shall be lawful for the said trustees and the survivor of them, their and his, successors or successor, and they are hereby expressly granted full power and authority, to grant, bargain, sell and assign, transfer and convey, all, or any, of the estate hereinbefore transferred to them in trust, or any part or parts, thereof, and to execute good and valid releases and conveyances thereof in fee simple, or of any less, or other, estate therein, to the purchaser or purchasers, thereof, grantee, or grantees, thereof, and to re-invest the proceeds thereof as the same shall be received. That it shall, and may, be lawful for the said trustees and the survivor of them, their, and his, successors, or successor, to lease, for terms not exceeding twenty-one (21) years, any real estate that may be held by them, and to execute good and valid leases thereof. 

FORM 358-EQUITABLE CONVERSION OF REAL ESTATE IF PART OF TRUST 

Should any real estate be decreed to be a part of this trust, I direct my trustee to sell such real estate and to convert it into money as soon as such conversion can be made without sacrifice, and thereafter to keep such money invested only in personalty during the term of this trust. 

FORM 359-CONVERSION OF LANDS IN OTHER JURISDICTIONS WHERE WILL WOULD BE OTHERWISE INVALID 

If at the time of my decease I shall be the owner of any lands, tenements or hereditaments situate, lying and being in any other state or country than the State of -----aforesaid, and the laws of such other State or Country shall be such that any of the provisions of this instrument shall or might be in conflict therewith, or would be to any extent made ineffective or inoperative thereby, then it is my will and I direct that my executors and residuary trustees shall have the power and shall proceed forthwith to sell such lands and convert them into money or other personal property, and the proceeds of such sale shall be applied as the property sold and converted was directed to be; and I hereby give and devise all such lands, tenements and hereditaments to them for the purpose of such sale and conversion, and they shall hold all and singular the proceeds of such sale and conversion as a part of my estate upon the trusts and for the purposes in this will directed; and they shall, except when otherwise provided, make all divisions and apportionments of property, real or personal, and all appraisals of value that may become necessary in the setting apart of the several trust funds hereby provided and in making the distributions herein directed to be made; and in making such divsions and apportionments and appraisals of value their judgment shall be final, and all other parties in interest are directed to accept the divisions so made by them. I authorize them accordingly without sale or conversion, to make use of any of my real estate or of the securities in which any of my personal estate may be left invested by me, in the discharge and payment of legacies given by this will, or in setting out any of the funds herein directed to be set out and given to the several trustees hereby appointed; and undivided interests in real estate may be set over in payment of bequests in the discretion of my executors and residuary trustees. [76]

FORM 360-DISCRETIONARY POWER TO EXECUTORS AND TRUSTEES TO CONVERT 

I authorize and empower my executors and trustees hereinafter named to sell and convey and convert into money in their discretion, at such time or times and upon such terms as they shall think best, all or any part of my estate, real or personal except such parts thereof as are specifically devised or bequeathed by this will or declared herein not to be included in the power of sale hereby given and to execute sufficient deeds of conveyance to said real estate.

FORM 361-CERTAIN REALTY TO BE DEEMED PERSONALTY; TIME OF SALE OF REALTY DISCRETIONARY WITH EXECUTORS OR SURVIVOR

I leave to the discretion of the said executors of this my will or of such of them as shall act, and the survivors and survivor of them the choice of the particular time for making sales from time to time of the real property to which this article of my will is or shall be applicable. 

FORM 362-TRUSTEES TO HOLD AND INVEST PROCEEDS OF REAL AND PERSONAL ESTATE 

And I do hereby declare that the said trustees and the survivor of them, their successors or successor, or the trustees or trustee for the time being, shall, out of the monies arising from the sale of the said real estate hereinbefore devised in trust for sale, and from the calling in, sale and conversion into monies of such parts of the said personal estate lastly hereinbefore bequeathed as shall not consist of money, and, out of the ready money of which I shall be possessed at my death, pay my funeral and testamentary expenses, and debts and legacies (other than specific legacies) bequeathed by this my will, or any codicil hereto, and shall invest the residue of the same monies in the names or name of them, the said trustees or trustee for the time being, in funds and securities for the time being suitable or authorized for the investment of trust monies, and may at any time vary or transpose such stocks, funds or securities into or for others of any nature hereby authorized, at their or his discretion. 

FORM 363-POWER TO RETAIN INVESTMENTS 

Provided always, and I hereby declare, that the trustees or trustee for the time being of this my will may retain any shares or stock in any public company, or any bonds of any such company, or of any public body whatsoever, or any investments whatsoever, of which I may be possessed at the time of my death; and that the property so retained, and the annual income thereof, shall be held upon the trusts, and with and subject to the powers, provisions and declarations herein declared and contained; but nothing herein contained shall authorize the said trustee or trustees to make any investment of any description other than those hereinbefore expressly authorized.

FORM 364-POWER TO RETAIN INVESTMENTS (ANOTHER FORM) 

To retain the stocks, bonds, or other investments, if any, which are respectively received by the trustee from my executors, although not of the character authorized by the laws of_________ for trust investments, or to dispose of, or change, any, or all, of such investments, or any other securities, which may, at any time, form part of the trust estate, and to invest and re-invest the proceeds thereof, or any other moneys, in such stocks, bonds, or other investments, as may be deemed safe and for the best interest of said respective trusts, although not of the character authorized by the laws of---. __for trust investments.

FORM 365-RETENTION OF SECURITIES 

I, suggest to the trustes under my will that such shares in the capital stock of that company as I shall own at the time of my death and as they or my executors shall not be bound to sell under any agreement made by me in my lifetime be retained by the trustee a permanent investment unless from a change of circumstances a sale thereof in the opinion of the trustees for the time being may become desirable in which case of course such a sale should be made. But I direct that any agreement made by me in my lifetime for a sale of any shares in that company be faithfully carried out. But the proceeds of any sale of any of said shares whether made in pursuance of any agreement made by me in my lifetime or otherwise shall stand in the place of the shares so sold and be invested by the trustees for the time being in other property but to be held upon the same trust for the same uses and purposes and for the benefit of the same persons and with the same power and provisions as the shares sold were held under this will or would have been held thereunder if not sold. [77]

FORM 366—RETENTION OF SPECIFIC SECURITIES 

It is my opinion, and I have for a long time considered this matter, that it would be wise for my executors and trustees to retain substantially all the stock of the---‒‒‒ __of_. --that I may own, at the time of my decease, and not to sell the same, during the continuation of the trusts under this, my will; but that whenever they shall deem it advisable to sell the said stock they should do so, if possible, in one block. 

Notwithstanding the opinion I have thus expressed, I leave it to the discretion of my executors and trustees to do as their own judgment, from time to time, may direct as I do not wish to have the opinion I have above expressed to be considered as a restriction upon their absolute freedom in dealing with the said stock as they shall deem to be best.

FORM 367-RETAINING AND CONTINUING SECURITIES (1) BETTER CLASS OF SECURITIES 

While I do not wish to control or embarrass the discretion of my executors and residuary trustees, it is my desire that they shall retain for my estate the better class of securities, including mortgage, railroad or other corporate stocks or bonds, and other securities in which they may find any part of my estate invested at my death, and that in selling or converting any securities they shall in the first instance dispose of such as in their judgment shall seem to be of the less substantial and enduring value for the purpose of investment; and in making up the several trust funds which under the provisions of this will they are to set up and transfer to the trustees appointed herein of the said several trust funds, they shall use for that purpose so far as possible, except as in this will in any case otherwise directed, the securities of the better class aforesaid, which may compose a part of my estate. 

(2) RETENTION OPTIONAL 

And they may keep invested all or any part of my estate that at the time of my decease may be invested in realty or personalty, or in stocks, bonds, or securities, and from time to time invest all or any part of my estate, in stocks, bonds, or securities; they shall not be obligated to convert into cash, nor to change the character or identity of any stocks, bonds and securities belonging to my estate at the time of my decease, or at any time thereafter.

FORM 368-POWER TO CONTINUE INVESTMENTS; FIDUCIARIES ABSOLVED FROM DUTY TO CONVERT TO “LEGALS" AND FROM LIABILITY FOR SHRINKAGE 

I authorize and empower my executors to hold and continue in their discretion, so long as they may think proper, any investments of my personal estate existing at the time of my death, and, upon their discharge as such executors, to transfer, assign and deliver such investments, so held and continued by them, to my trustees. I likewise authorize and empower my trustees to hold, and continue in their discretion, so long as they may think proper, any investments of my personal estate, existing at the time of my death, that may be transferred, assigned and delivered to them by my executors, as herein provided. My intent in giving my executors and trustees the authority and power herein conferred upon them, is that they shall be absolved and discharged from any legal duty of converting my personal estate into money, or from disposing of such investments by reason of their not being investments authorized to be made by trustees under the laws of the State of___ and I expressly declare that my executors and trustees shall not be liable for any shrinkage in value by reason of their not selling the investments so held and continued by them.
