FORM 369-AUTHORITY TO CONTINUE INVESTMENTS 

I authorize my executors and trustees to retain, either permanently or temporarily, or for such period as to them may seem expedient, any investment made or property held by me, of whatever nature, and I direct that they shall not be held liable for any loss resulting to my estate by reason of their retaining any such investment or property or for any error of judgment in this respect. 

At the risk of my estate and without responsibility to my executors and trustees, I authorize them to continue and in their discretion to turn over as part of the shares of my estate hereinbefore given, devised and bequeathed, in the erection of the trusts herein created, any stock, bonds, or other securities in which at the time of my death any portion of my estate shall be invested. 

At the risk of my estate, and without responsibility to my executor and trustee he may continue to hold any stock, bonds or other securities in which at the time of my death any portion of my estate shall be invested, although the same shall not be of the character authorized by the laws of the State of---for trust investment, and likewise to have full power and authority to dispose of, call in and change any [ 78 ]

and all investments, and to invest and re-invest in any stock, bonds or other securities which in his discretion may be deemed safe and for the best interest of my estate, and said executor or trustee shall not be restricted to securities of the character authorized by the laws of the State of for trust investment.

FORM 3692-POWER TO LEASE BEYOND DURATION OF THE TRUST 

I give to the trustee of each trust hereby created full power and authority to sell, for cash or on credit, or partly for cash and partly on credit, at public or private sale, at such times and places and in such manner and upon such terms and conditions as it shall deem expedient, or to lease or rent for such terms of years, whether or not such term or terms shall extend beyond the duration of the trust, or otherwise to dispose of, in such manner and upon such terms and conditions as it shall deem expedient, any and all real estate at any time constituting a part of such trust, and to execute and deliver any and all deeds, leases or other instruments necessary or proper for the accomplishment of such purpose. 

FORM 370-POWER TO CONTINUE MORTGAGES 

And I also declare that any mortgage of real estate which I shall hold at my death may, in the discretion of the said trustees or trustee, be from time to time continued or renewed upon such terms as to the rate of interest, duration and time and mode of repayments of the loan and in all other respects, as shall be approved by the said trustees or trustee. 

FORM 371—POWER FOR THE TRUSTEES TO RAISE MONEY BY MORTGAGE 

Provided also, and I declare, that it shall be lawful for the said trustees or trustee at any time or times, by mortgage of any of the said real estate included in the general devise hereinbefore contained, to raise any sums or sum of money which they or he shall think necessary or expedient for the payment of funeral and testamentary expenses, debts and legacies, or any part thereof, or for any of the other purposes of this my will, or the execution of any of the trusts and powers herein contained, and any such mortgage may contain such powers of sale or other powers and provisions as the said trustees or trustee may think fit. But no person advancing any money on any such mortgage or charge as aforesaid shall be bound or concerned to see that the money proposed to be raised is actually wanted for the purposes aforesaid, or otherwise as to the propriety or expediency of such money being raised. And, I further declare that the said trustees and trustee shall stand possessed of the surplus of the monies to be raised by any such mortgage as aforesaid, which shall remain after satisfying the purposes for which the same shall have been raised, upon and for the trusts and purposes herein declared concerning the residuary monies. 

FORM 372-POWER TO SELL OR MORTGAGE 

I hereby empower my said executors and trustees and such of them as shall qualify and undertake the execution of this, my will, and the survivor or survivors of them and their successors in their discretion to sell and dispose of any of my personal estate and also to negotiate and obtain a loan or loans on the real estate to be improved by the alteration of existing buildings or the erection of new buildings or upon such real estate so purchased including the part or share now held by me for such time and for such terms and for such rates of interest not exceeding the legal rate as they shall deem advantageous and to secure the repayment of such loan or loans by executing and delivering to the loaner a bond or bonds or other evidence of indebtedness binding my estate and to give good and sufficient indenture or indentures of mortgage. The expense of procuring such loan or loans shall be charged against the income of my estate. 

FORM 373-POWERS AS TO MORTGAGES AND OTHER INCUMBRANCES 

In event that from time to time any of the trust property shall be subject to any lien or incumbrance, the trustee shall pay the interest accruing and payable on the debt or obligation secured by such lien, out of any income from the property subject to such lien, or if the same be insufficient out of any other income from the particular trust fund in which such property is held. In event that any such obligation secured by any such lien matures and becomes payable and in the judgment of the trustee it is advisable or necessary to pay the principal of such obligation, the trustee is expressly authorized to pay the same from funds held by it as principal or from funds realized by it by sale of capital assets, such funds or assets so to be applied shall be first those in the same principal trust fund as the property subject to such lien and second, if such be not sufficient or not available, then by way of loan or investment to be made from any other trust fund held by the trustee hereunder. In event of the discharge of any such lien, such discharge shall as between [79]

any beneficiaries entitled to income or to remainders or reversions, be held and considered as investment of the amount of lien paid from such other funds so applied to discharge the same, and all and every equity between beneficiaries hereunder shall be held to be adjusted on the basis of such investment and distribution of income from the property so discharged from such lien as provided with respect to the income of such fund. The trustee shall not in exercising discretion as to such discharge of any lien be held to consider whether the property subject to such lien is property in which it would otherwise invest or be entitled to invest the trust estate hereunder. Except as specifically provided herein no lien or incumbrance created or existing on any property when and as received by the trustee hereunder shall be held or considered as subject to discharge by the application thereon of any other funds, personal or real, of the trust estate save only by way of investment as above provided. 

FORM 374-LIMITED POWER TO TRUSTEE TO BORROW MONEY FOR BUSINESS 

The trustee hereunder shall have full authority in its own name as trustee, or under such name or names as it may choose so to do, in the course of the conduct of any business herein authorized to be conducted or continued by the trustee, from time to time to borrow money and endorse and secure the discount of negotiable paper by it received in the course of the business by it conducted, or continued in such amounts and upon such terms and for such period as it may deem requisite, provided that any such loan or discount shall be made repayable only out of and as a charge against that part of the trust estate in the charge of said trustee, which it is hereunder authorized to employ as capital in the conduct of such business and any such part of said trust estate may be pledged or mortgaged to secure payment of the money so borrowed; but the aggregate amount of such loans together with the amount of capital employed in said business at the time of my decease including in the amount of capital employed the amount of liability upon endorsement of discounted paper. Neither the trustee nor any other part of said trust estate than that expressly chargeable as above provided shall be liable for repayment of any money so borrowed. 

FORM 375-POWER TO BORROW MONEY GENERALLY 

The trustee is hereby authorized in its name as trustee to borrow money and engage and bind by mortgage, deed of trust, or pledge as the case may be, all or any part of the property and estate in its charge hereunder, for the repayment of the same; providing that the trustee shall not individually be liable to any extent for the repayment of such money. Any money so borrowed shall become part of the principal of the trust estate, but the trustee is hereby authorized to set apart and hold and apply for the repayment thereof, a sufficient amount of income of all of the property in its hands, and may also to the extent that principal has been increased by the borrowing of such money, sell property in its control as trustee to the amount necessary to repay at maturity the money so borrowed with interest, but the course of the trustee as to such means of providing for payment shall be determined by it, and at its discretion. 

FORM 376-POWER TO BORROW MONEY ON SECURITY BASIS 

The trustee is hereby authorized in its name as trustee, to borrow money, when and to the extent it may deem such course advisable to improve or conserve any property in the trust estate, but such money shall be borrowed only on the security of and only on condition that it shall be repayable from the property so to be conserved or improved, as the value thereof stands after application of the money so borrowed, which money shall not be a charge against the trustee as such or individually, or against any other property in its hands. Any money so borrowed shall become part of the principal of the trust estate, but the trustee is hereby authorized to set apart and hold and apply for the repayment thereof, a sufficient amount of income of all of the property in its hands, and may also to the extent that principal has been increased by the borrowing of such money, sell property in its control as trustee to the amount necessary to repay at maturity the money so borrowed with interest, but the course of the trustee as to such means of providing for payment shall be determined by it, and at its discretion.

FORM 379-CLAUSE AUTHORIZING EXECUTORS AND TRUSTEES TO INVEST IN SPECIFIED SECURITIES 

I authorize and empower my executors and trustees to invest and re-invest the principal, or any part of the principal, of any trust fund created by this, my last will and testament, and any income accumulated during the minority of any beneficiary of any such trust fund, in first mortgages on improved real estate situate within the City of---. -; in bonds of the United States of America, or of the State of_____ and in bonds, or stock, of the City of‒‒‒‒ ; and in first mortgage bonds, or stock, of any railroad company that, continuously for a period of five (5) years, has paid dividends of not less than five (5%) per cent per annum, or in mortgage bonds of any railroad company, if the payment of the principal and interest thereof is guaranteed by any other railroad company that similarly has paid such amount of dividends for such period of time.

FORM 380-SPECIFYING GENERALLY SECURITIES IN WHICH TRUSTEES MAY INVEST 

My said trustees are hereby authorized and empowered to invest and keep invested all my personal estate to be received and held by them in trust under this my will, as well as the proceeds of any trust, real estate when sold by them, in the following manner, that is to say: 

1. For any manner in which savings banks or trustees may, at the time, lawfully invest any of their funds, except in mortgages on unimproved real estate. 

2. In bonds secured by first mortgages on improved real estate in the City of‒‒‒‒‒‒‒‒‒to the extent of not more than five per cent (5%) of said trust estate in any single one of said bonds and mortgages, provided however, that the total investments made by my trustees in such bonds and mortgages shall not exceed one-fourth (4) of the net value of my said trust estate. 

3. In bonds and stocks of the United States, or of the several States of the United States, which have never made default in the payment of principal or interest upon their stocks or bonds within ten years immediately preceding the making of such investment; provided that in no case shall more than twenty-five per cent of the said trust estate be invested in such securities collectively nor more than ten per cent of the said trust estate be invested in any one class or kind of such securities. 

4. In mortgage bonds of railroad corporations in the United States of America which shall have regularly paid the fixed or obligatory interest upon all of their outstanding bonded indebtedness, for a period of five consecutive years immediately preceding the making of such investment, but not more than twentyfive per cent of said trust estate shall be invested in such securities collectively, nor more than five per cent of said trust estate be invested in any one class or kind of such securities. 

5. In bonds of any city in the United States of America having a population over 50,000, to the extent of not more than five per cent of said trust estate in bonds of any one of said cities. 

FORM 381-INVESTMENTS IN BONDS OF UNITED STATES, CERTAIN STATES, AND CITIES

general form in which I may leave the same and with the power to change and vary such investments from time to time; and it is my will and direction that all investments and any trusts and securities in any trusts hereunder created and in my estate be issued, recorded and held in the names of all the executors and trustees for the time being such.

FORM 383-INVESTMENTS IN CORPORATE STOCKS AND BONDS; FINANCIAL INSTI- TUTIONS, STREET RAILWAY SECURITIES, AND PACKING HOUSES

FORM 384-CLAUSE LIMITING EXECUTORS AND TRUSTEES TO INVESTMENT IN LEGAL SECURITIES ONLY 

I give to my executors and trustees power and authority to invest and re-invest any funds coming into their possession, under this, my last will and testament, in such securities only as shall be legal for the investment of trust funds under the laws of the State of. that shall be in force and effect at the time when such investment, or investments, are to be made.

FORM 385-LIMITING TRUSTEE'S POWERS OF INVESTMENT 

I desire and intend to relieve my trustees, subject to the limitations and conditions herein contained, of and from all restrictions placed by law on investments which may be made by trustees; provided that no loan shall be made to any individual, firm, or corporation, unless the same shall be secured to the satisfaction of my executors, or of my trustees, by first mortgage, or mortgages, upon improved real estate, or by securities listed upon the New York Stock Exchange; and provided, further, that no investments shall be made in securities that are not, at the time legal investments for savings banks in the State of___ without the consent, or approval, of all my then trustees to the making of such investments. 

FORM 386-INVESTMENT IN CERTAIN SECURITIES WITHOUT LIABILITY FOR LOSS 

In making investments or re-investments my said trustees, or the surviving trustees or trustee, as the case may be, is expressly authorized to select not only investments of a character permissible by law for the investment of trust funds but also to purchase and hold shares of stocks in banking institutions and trust companies incorporated and doing business in the State of. and to purchase and hold improved, productive, unencumbered real estate situated within the city of. all without leave and approval of any court. Any of said securities or property as my said trustees, or surviving trustees or trustee, as the case may be, shall purchase within the authority hereby given are hereby exonerated from any and all liability, loss or responsibility for the consequences of their acts as a result thereof. 

FORM 387-INVESTMENTS: ONE-HALF IN REAL AND ONE-HALF IN PERSONAL PROPERTY 

It is my will and I direct that investments be made with reference to the security of the trust fund rather than the rate of interest or income to be derived from it, and that where real and personal property have been given in trust, a proper proportion be maintained between them. It has been my general intention to keep at least half of my property in real estate and the rest in personal property; but in this particular my trustees are to exercise their own discretion and act in each case as may under the circumstances seem best to them.

FORM 388-NON-LEGALS 

I will and direct that the trustees or surviving trustee at the time of the several trusts created or existing by or under any of the provisions of this my will, shall be, and are or is, hereby authorized to continue to hold as investments of such trusts respectively for so long as they or he, in their or his discretion, may see fit, any and all stocks, bonds, securities or items of property, real or personal, belonging to me at my death, which may be transferred or conveyed by the executors of this my will to such trusts respectively, although the same may not be of the character permissible for trust investments by the general rules of law, and [82]

likewise in respect of both said trusts respectively, shall have full power and authority to dispose of, call in and change any or all investments of such trust estates respectively, and to sell and convey any and all real estate which may at any time belong to such trusts respectively.

FORM 390-INVESTMENTS 

I authorize my executors to invest the funds of my estate in first mortgages upon improved real estate in the city of‒‒‒‒‒‒ no loan, however, to exceed two-thirds of the value of the mortgaged premises as appraised by my executors or by any person or corporation engaged in the business of appraising real estate, or in the bonds of the United States or of any State, City or County which shall not, during a period of‒‒‒‒‒‒‒‒years prior to such investment, have defaulted in the payment of either the principal or interest of any of its bonded obligations, also in the mortgage bonds of any railroad that shall for a period of at least three years prior to such investment, have paid interest on its next junior mortgage, or dividends upon any of its stock.

FORM 391-FULL POWER AND DIRECTION TO MAKE INVESTMENTS 

To invest and re-invest money coming into its possession, subject to any limitations or conditions hereinafter specifically imposed in such loans, stocks, bonds, securities and real estate as it may deem proper or suitable for the investment of trust funds, without being restricted to a class of investments which a trustee is or may hereafter be permitted by law to make. The trustee may purchase for investment purposes, securities, real estate and other property from its bond, mortgage or other departments, paying therefor not more than the price at which said securities, real estate or other property are offered for sale to the general public.

FORM 392-TRUSTEE IS AUTHORIZED AND EMPOWERED TO HOLD AND INVEST IN NON-LEGALS 

I authorize and empower my trustees to retain any investments or securities held by me at the time of my death as investments for my estate, notwithstanding that they may not be in the form of trustee investments. I further authorize them to invest any funds in such bonds and other stocks or other investments as they may consider satisfactory, notwithstanding that such investments are not such as trustees would be authorized to invest in under the laws of the State of‒‒‒‒‒‒ My trustees shall not be held responsible for any loss that may occur to my estate through a bona fide exercising of their discretion, either as to realization or investment.

FORM 393-NEW INVESTMENTS 

(After specifying character of investments continue:) Except as hereinbefore specified, I hereby direct that all new investments made by my executors or trustees shall be in such domestic securities and property as shall in law be deemed suitable for the investment of trust funds unless they shall be approved in writing by (a majority of) such of the adult beneficiaries as shall have attained the age of thirty years and, if made with such approval, the executors or trustees shall not be responsible for any loss or deterioration resulting from the making or retention of such investments.

FORM 394 OMNIBUS POWER CLAUSE 

I give my executor and trustee full power to sell real or personal property at public or private sale for cash or upon credit, upon such terms as my executor may deem sufficient, to continue present investments; to invest and re-invest in such property or securities as my executor shall deem adequate and safe, free from limitations imposed by law; to exchange, pledge or mortgage any property of my estate; to deposit any securities with voting trustees or for any other purpose; to exercise any and all rights appurtenant to any securities accrued or accruing, and to use the principal of my estate for that purpose if deemed advisable; to distribute the whole or any part of my estate in kind by undivided shares or otherwise in the absolute discretion of the executor, both as to propriety of distribution and execution thereof; to compromise any and all claims in favor of the estate or against it. [83]

FORM 395—DISCRETIONARY POWERS LIMITED TO ORIGINAL TRUSTEES 

When my said original trustees shall both have ceased to act hereunder as trustees, I direct that the discretionary powers given to my original trustees, as to investments, shall be terminated, and that the trust company that shall thereafter act as trustee shall be limited, in making new investments, to such investments as are permitted to be made by trustees under the laws of the State of---. ; but said trust company shall be authorized to retain such investments as are transmitted to it by the former trustees, at the time it commences to act as trustee. 

FORM 396-COMBINING MANY POWERS 

The trustee, in addition to and not in modification or limitation of all common law statutory authority, shall have power with regard to both real and personal property in the trust, and any part of such property thereof; to mortgage, to lease with or without option to purchase; to sell, in whole or in part at public or private sale, without approval of any court therefor obtained, and without necessity upon any person dealing with the trustee to see to the application of any money or other property delivered to it; to exchange property for other property; to invest and re-invest in securities or properties although not of a kind or in an amount which ordinarily denominated “legal investments for trustees"; to keep any or all securities or other property in kind at values determined by it; to decide with a power of attorney for their transfer attached or in its own name without disclosing its fiduciary capacity; to determine what receipts or expenditures shall be charged or credited to income and what to principal; to make distributions or divisions of principal hereunder in property in kind at values determined by it; to decide whether or not to make deductions from income for depreciation, obsolescence, or amortization and in what amounts; to pay, compromise or contest any claim or other matter directly or indirectly affecting this trust; to employ counsel for any of the above or other purposes and generally to do all things in relation to the trust fund which the Donor could do if living and this trust had not been executed. All such divisions and decisions made by the trustee in good faith shall be conclusive on all parties at interest. 

FORM 397-POWER TO VARY INVESTMENTS 

And may from time to time with such consent or at such discretion as aforesaid, vary, alter and transpose all or any of the stocks, funds, shares and securities into or for other stocks, funds, charges or securities of the same or like nature. 

FORM 398-GENERAL DIRECTION AS TO CONSENT TO SALES AND INVESTMENTS 

Provided always, and I declare, that during the lifetime of my said wife, no sale, conversion, investment or variation of the investment of any part of my residuary real or personal estate in which she shall for the time being be interested, shall be made without her previous consent in writing; and that after death of my said wife, no sale, conversion, investment or variation of investment of any part of my said residuary estate, which shall have been appropriated to answer the whole or any part of the share hereinafter given to my trustees or trustee in trust for any of my children and his or her issue, shall be made during the life of the child entitled to the income of such share, if of full age, without his or her previous consent in writing. 

FORM 399-POWER TO REGISTER SECURITY IN NAME OF TRUSTEE 

I, also, authorize and empower the trustee, in its sole discretion, to cause the securities that may, from time to time, comprise the trust funds, or any part thereof, to be registered in its name as trustee hereunder, or in its own name, or in the name of its nominee, or to take and keep the same unregistered, and to retain them, or any part thereof, in such condition that they will pass by delivery. 

FORM 400-REGISTRATION OF SECURITIES 

The trustee shall not be required to cause stocks or registered bonds belonging to the trust to be registered in its name as trustee hereunder, but it may cause suck stocks or registered bonds to be registered in its own name without describing the trust or in the name of its nominee, but such registration shall neither increase nor decrease the liability of the trustee. 

FORM 401-DISSOLUTION, MERGER OR CONSOLIDATION OF CORPORATE TRUSTEE 

If at any time the corporate existence of such trust powers of the corporate trustee of a trust created hereunder shall terminate or its charter shall be forfeited, and, if a new corporation with trust powers shall [84]

be organized pursuant to then existing law, for the purpose of continuing the business of the trustee, or if at any time such trustee shall become merged in or consolidated with any other corporation, having corporate power to administer trusts, such new or other corporation shall become the trustee of such trust and it or any other successor corporate trustee, however appointed, shall be vested with all and the same rights, titles, estates, interests, powers, discretions, conveyance, as if originally named herein. 

FORM 402-POWER TO ENTER REORGANIZATIONS 

I authorize my executors in case of default in the payment of the interest or principal of any obligations issued by a corporation, securities of which are held by them as a part of my estate, to deposit such securities subject to the terms of any protective or reorganization agreement that may be entered into in relation thereto and to accept any new or substituted securities in place of the defaulted securities and to make any payments necessary to enable them to obtain such new or substituted securities; and I also authorize them to accept at any time in place of any securities held by them any new or substituted securities issued for the purpose of taking up the securities so held by them; and in case any right to subscribe for new securities of any corporation shall devolve upon them by reason of their holding any securities of such corporation, then I authorize them either to exercise such right and acquire such new securities or to sell such rights as they, in their discretion may deem best. Should any company or corporation in which the testator or her estate may hold shares or other interest increase its capital, the trustee is authorized to subscribe for and take up the proportion of such increased capital to which as holder of shares or other interest in such company or corporation it may be entitled, and to pay for the same out of the moneys of the testator's estate, or in the alternative, to sell its rights of such allotment. The trustee is further authorized to join in any plan for the reconstruction or reorganization of any such company or corporation, or the amalgamation of any such company or corporation with any other company or corporation, or for the sale of the assets of any such company or corporation, or any part thereof, and the trustee may in pursuance of any such plan, accept any shares or securities in lieu of or in exchange for the shares or other interest held by the testator's estate in such company or corporation. 

FORM 403-REORGANIZATION POWERS (ANOTHER FORM) 

I further authorize my said executors and trustees in their discretion to vote, in person or by proxy, upon all stocks held by them, to unite with other owners of similar property in carrying out any plan for the reorganization of any corporation or company whose securities form a portion of my estate or of any trust fund under this will, to exchange the securities of any corporation for others issued by the same or by any other corporation upon such terms as the said trustees shall deem proper; to assent to the consolidation or merger of any corporation whose securities are held by them with any other corporation, to the lease by such corporation of its property or to the lease by any other corporation of its property to such corporation, and upon any such consolidation, merger, lease or similar arrangement to exchange the securities held by them for other securities issued in connection with such arrangement; to pay all such assessments, expenses and sums of money as they may deem expedient for the protection of their interests as holders of the stocks, bonds or other securities of any corporation or company, and generally to exercise in respect to all securities held by them all the same rights and powers as are or may be lawfully exercised by persons owning similar property in their own right.

FORM 404-POWER TO ENTER REORGANIZATION AGREEMENTS (SHORT FORM) 

To join in any reorganization or protective plan approved by it, should any stocks, bonds and other securities held hereunder be involved in a reorganization, and to deposit said stocks, bonds and other securities subject to the terms of any protective or reorganization agreement approved by it, and to pay from the principal of the trust any moneys required to be paid, according to such plan and without applying to any court for permission so to do.

FORM 405-AUTHORIZING EXECUTORS AND TRUSTEES TO EXERCISE OPTIONS, PRIVILEGES AND RIGHTS TO SUBSCRIBE TO SECURITIES 

I authorize and empower my executors and the personal trustees appointed by me, or the survivor of them, but not their successors in the trusts hereby created, provided all my executors and personal trustees for the time being shall agree, or consent thereto, to exercise for the benefit of my estate, or any and all options upon, or privileges, rights, to subscribe to the stock, bonds, or other securities, or obligations, of any company of, or in, which I may be a creditor, stockholder, or bondholder, or otherwise interested at the time of my death, or of any company, or companies, which may be, or become, the successor to, or carry on the business in whole, or in part, of any such company, or companies, in which I may be a creditor, stockholder, or bondholder, or otherwise interested at the time of my death; and I authorize and empower [85]

my executors and personal trustees, provided all my executors and personal trustees for the time being shall agree, or consent thereto, to exercise such options, privileges, or rights to subscribe in favor of my estate, and to take and receive, either by purchase, exchange, or otherwise, and to pay or provide for, or procure the payment of the same out of, or by means of, the other securities or property constituting my estate, as fully and to the same extent as I could if living, and I authorize and empower my executors and my trustees for the time being, provided all my executors and trustees for the time being shall consent thereto, to advance moneys for the payment of any of such bonds, stocks, or other securities, or to provide, in their discretion, for the payment of the same by the sale, pledge, or mortgage of any part of my estate, or to borrow such sums of money, and to secure the same by the mortgage, or pledge, of any such portion of my estate as, in their judgment, may be necessary, or expedient, to enable them to acquire such bonds, stocks, or securities, for the benefit of my estate.

I direct that my personal trustees shall have the right, provided all my personal trustees for the time being agree, or consent thereto, to exercise any such options, privileges, or rights, and all my trustees for the time being shall have the right and power to advance such moneys, or borrow, or pledge, as herein provided, without applying to any court for any order, or decree, concerning the same. 

FORM 406-POWER TO COMPOUND DEBTS, ETC. 

And I hereby authorize and empower the trustees or trustee for the time being of this my will, in their or his absolute discretion, to allow, pay, compromise, compound, enforce, waive, accept or give real or personal security, for, or give time without taking any security for, or refer to arbitration, any debts or demands whatsoever which may be owing from or to be made upon or against or in behalf of, my estate, whether the same be legally enforcible or not, and upon such evidence as they or he shall think sufficient. 

FORM 407-APPLICATION OF TRUST FUNDS 

I declare that purchasers and other persons who shall pay any trust moneys to my said trustees or either of them, or to my executors or either of them, shall be exempt from all responsibility in respect to the application of the same, and from the necessity of inquiring into the regularity, and validity or propriety of any sale made, or purported to be made, under trusts or powers contained in this will, provided the same appear upon their face to be regular.

Definition of, and Apportionment of Gains and Loss to, Principal and Income 

FORM 408——INCOME AND PRINCIPAL DEFINED 

The trustee is authorized and empowered to determine at its exclusive discretion, whether money or property coming into its possession, under the terms of this last will and testament shall be treated as principal or income and to charge or apportion expenses or losses to principal or income according as it may deem just and equitable.

FORM 409-PROVISION IN RESPECT TO SINKING FUND AND STOCK DIVIDEND 

It is my intention, and I direct that, wherever the principal of any trust fund is invested in securities bought, or taken, at a premium, the life tenant, or tenant for years, shall receive the whole net income from the trust fund (except stock dividends), and that no part of the income of any trust fund shall be taken from any life tenant, or tenant for years, to make good to the remaindermen the premium paid in making the investment, and no such premium shall be regarded as in the nature of any dividends from principal, which the remaindermen are entitled to have repaid, and it shall be no part of the duty of the trustees to create any sinking fund by setting aside any of the yearly interest on such securities to offset any depreciation in value caused by the approach of the date of maturity, or otherwise. 

FORM 410-FACE AMOUNT OF INTEREST OR DIVIDENDS TREATED AS INCOME 

The trustee shall in all cases of the earning of interest or dividends by any securities by it held in trust, whether received by it at the inception of the trust or thereafter by it purchased or acquired, consider the face or actual amount of interest or dividends paid from time to time on such securities as income, which, subject only to deductions for expenses of the trustee (to be by it apportioned as it shall deem just and proper) shall be payable to the beneficiary at the time entitled to receive income of the principal of such sum. 

There shall be no amortization or charge or credit therefor as against income, on account of any premiums or discount at which securities are purchased, and the person or persons entitled to the reversion of any such [86]

securities shall only be entitled to have the same or the value thereof at the time such reversion becomes effective. Any benefit of advance in value or discount or loss of premium on original investment in securities shall accrue to or fall upon the reversioner; provided however, that in no case shall the trustee be held liable to any beneficiary for or be called to account for any loss or gain by reason of the amount of principal payment made to purchase any security, being more or less than the value or amount of principal secured as the proceeds thereof when turned over or realized upon by the trustee. 

FORM 411—ALL DIVIDENDS AND INTEREST TO BE TREATED AS INCOME (SHORTER FORM) 

I direct my trustees to apply the income of all securities at any time held by them to the use of the beneficiary for whom they were held, irrespective of the price paid for them, or of their market value at any time, it being my intention that no part of such income shall be applied as a sinking fund to offset any loss of the premium upon, or market value of, any such securities.

FORM 412-PREMIUM AND DISCOUNT NOT TO BE AMORTIZED 

In making distribution and division of said estate, it is my intention to impose the loss, decrease and diminution of value and loss of premium upon property, assets, investments and securities, whether the same were owned by me, or shall be acquired by the trustees, upon those who shall share in the final distribution of my estate, or upon the remaindermen, and not upon the beneficiaries of the said trusts herein set forth, or the cestuis que trustent, and the trustees shall not reserve any portion of the income to make good against the wearing away and wasting of premium, nor of principal, nor the amount paid for premium, nor principal, either by me, or said executors and trustees; nor shall there be any amortization; nor shall they set aside, nor hold, any part of income to make good, or to preserve, or to maintain, any wasting, or diminishing, or decreasing, assets.

FORM 413-PREMIUM AND DISCOUNT TO BE FIGURED EACH YEAR 

In all instances where the trustee shall have purchased securities at a price greater or less than the par value thereof, the trustee shall in computing the income payable to the beneficiaries from income derived from such securities, pay out of such income only the interest earned by the actual investment, which shall be taken for the first year at the amount computed on the actual investment for such year at that rate which, upon the amount actually invested, the actually received amount of income thereon represents upon such price, and the difference between such interest earned and the interest actually received, shall, if an excess be credited to principal, or if a deficiency be debited to principal, and in each succeeding year a similar computation shall be made, the principal account being taken as the price paid each year, which computation shall be so made that the principal or book value of each such security will at the maturity thereof, equal the par value thereof.

FORM 414-PREMIUMS TO BE CHARGED AND DISCOUNTS TO BE CREDITED TO PRINCIPAL 

The trustee herein is hereby directed to charge all premiums on investments, and to credit all discounts on investments against and to principal, and not against or to income, and no person entitled to the remainder or reversion of any fund shall have any claim against the trustee on account thereof, save to the then principal of the securities it is invested in at the time such right accrues. 

FORM 415-CASH DIVIDENDS AS INCOME-PROFITS AS PRINCIPAL 

Extraordinary cash dividends which shall be received by my trustees hereunder shall be considered by them as wholly income and shall be distributed accordingly, but any dividends other than those paid in cash shall be considered as part of and added to the corpus of the trust estate. In the case of securities taken or purchased for the trust at a premium, my trustees shall not be required to set aside any part of the income thereof as a sinking fund to retire or absorb such premium, nor shall they be required to set aside any part of the income from the said trust estate to absorb any loss that may be incurred from the subsequent sale of securities at less than the purchase price, and my trustees shall further treat as a part of the principal of the trust estate any increase that may be derived from the sale of securities over the purchase price, and also any increase that may be derived from the subsequent redemption or payment at maturity of securities taken or purchased at less than the redemption price or at less than par. [ 87 ]

FORM 416-STOCK DIVIDENDS AS INCOME, SINKING FUND FOR PREMIUMS OR DEPRECIATION NOT REQUIRED 

I authorize and direct my executors and trustees to treat all stock dividends declared upon stocks constituting part of my residuary estate as income, or profits, payable to the beneficiary thereof for the time being, and that said executors, as such or as trustees, shall not be required to reserve, or set apart, from income any sinking fund, or funds, to meet diminishing premiums, or depreciation, upon any securities or investments, belonging to my estate, or to any trust hereby created. 

FORM 417-DIRECTION THAT STOCK DIVIDEND BE PRINCIPAL 

I direct and declare that any dividend payable in the stock of the corporation declaring or authorizing the same, and declared and authorized hereinafter, in respect of any stock composing, in whole or in part, the principal of the trust herein provided for, shall be and become in whole principal, and not income, of the said trust.

FORM 418-FORMULA FOR DETERMINING PREMIUM AND DISCOUNT 

In all instances where the trustee shall have purchased securities at a price greater or less than the par value thereof, the trustee shall in computing and paying over to any beneficiary the income payable to such beneficiary upon such securities, proceed as follows:

The trustee shall divide the amount of the premium or discount, as the case may be, by the number of years intervening between the time of purchase and the time of maturity of such security, and shall pay the beneficiary entitled to income where the security is, purchased at a premium, the actual interest or return on the security less the pro rata part of such premium for the period represented thereby, and where the security was purchased at a discount shall pay the beneficiary the full income actually collected, and credit in addition each year an amount equal to the pro rata part of the discount for such year, which shall be paid to the beneficiary only when such amount shall from time to time amount to and be paid in a divisible share of such security at par, or when out of sale of such security the beneficiary may be paid such proportion of the face of the credit as it then stands, as the price obtained for the security bears to par. And in each such instance the trustee is authorized in its judgment to settle and adjust the amounts paid to beneficiaries as to duly apportion such premiums or discounts, and it is expressly provided that neither any beneficiary nor the trustee shall ever be called on to account for any money paid or to be paid solely because any security is not actually paid at par at maturity, but the person or persons interested in the same at the time of maturity shall be taken hereunder as bearing any loss occurring by reason of non-payment in full at maturity. 

FORM 419—APPORTIONING COST OF IMPROVEMENTS 

Upon the making of any addition to, alteration of, or improvement upon any real estate, which the trustee shall certify is a permanent improvement and not merely current repairs (the latter to be paid for out of current earnings) and upon the payment of any special assessment or tax levied or assessed for and in respect of the benefit of any permanent or fixed improvement to any real estate, held in trust, the trustee shall, acting through its administrative officer of highest rank in its trust department, make an estimate and appraisal with respect to such improvement, as follows: First, it shall estimate the probable effective life of such improvement; Second, it shall make a computation of the probable value of the same as at the time of the termination of each interest hereunder in the income derived from such real estate, estimating the same as to duration of lives on the basis of the Wigglesworth tables, and on the rate of net income at the 'time of such improvement, but prior to the making thereof earned on such property (or in event such real estate is vacant at the average rate of income of all income producing securities, other than real estate) then held in trust; Third, upon the basis of the foregoing, the trustee shall pro rate and apportion the cost and amount paid for such improvement as a charge against each interest then or thereafter existing against or in the income or principal of such property. The share or proportion of each interest so computed, which is an interest in income, shall be paid, however, as follows: The total cost of the improvement shall be taken as an investment of capital, and the share or proportion payable by each interest entitled to income shall 

FORM 420-APPORTIONING COST OF IMPROVEMENTS (ANOTHER FORM) 

It is intended that as far as may be possible without impairing the resources from which the trustee must meet obligations for interest and for money borrowed, if any, that in each case where income is payable to a beneficiary, the full net income be paid to such beneficiary, without deduction for any depreciation of improvements or any charge for the use of principal in making such improvements as the trustee shall certify are permanent improvements (as distinguished from current repairs and alterations the cost of which [88]

shall be charged against current income), save and except as follows: First, the amount used in making such permanent improvements shall be considered as constituting part of the principal of the share on which any such beneficiary is entitled to income, and if the money used is from part of the estate the income on which such beneficiary is not entitled to have, the same shall be considered as money borrowed by the trustee to make such improvement, both with respect of interest and principal; Second, the trustee shall have full power to make such provision by sinking fund to be derived from apportionment of income or otherwise, as it may deem best, to provide for repayment of money borrowed to make improvements and to pay interest thereon. Provided also, however, that in any case where the trustee shall improve property, it shall not deduct from the income payable to a person entitled thereto for a term of years or for life, any part of the principal cost of such improvement, which on computation made by the trustee, shall represent the value thereof to the person or persons entitled to such property after expiration of the right of such person so entitled to income, but such value the trustee shall pay out of principal or leave in force, with interest thereon, as a charge on principal, and without diminution of income payable to the prior beneficiary by reason thereof.

FORM 421-APPORTIONING COST OF IMPROVEMENTS (SHORT FORM) 

The trustee shall for and during each year of the computed period of such interest deduct from the gross income payable from such property to any person having an interest in the income, an amount equal to the total amount for such improvement charged to such interest divided by the number of years and fraction, if any, of the computed period of such interest. 

FORM 422-CHARGES FOR CARRYING UNPRODUCTIVE REAL ESTATE 

All costs and expenses in any calendar year of carrying, repairing and maintaining real estate in the trust, may in the trustee's discretion be charged against the principal of the trust estate to such extent, if any, as such costs and expenses exceed the income derived in such year from the real estate 'upon which they were incurred. The trustee shall reimburse the principal in whole or in part therefor by charges to the subsequent income from such real estate in such amounts and at such intervals as they shall determine to be just and expedient.

FORM 423—LIFE TENANTS IN EVENT OF TERMINATION OF PRIOR ESTATE TO BE CHARGED WITH DEDUCTIONS FROM INCOME 

In the event an interest shall terminate before the expiration of the period computed for its existence, the next succeeding interest, if one for years or a life, shall be first charged with deductions from income for the unexpired part of the computed term of the prior interest at the amount per annum such prior interest would have been charged with if it had continued, and when such unexpired term is ended, the succeeding interest shall thenceforth be charged on the basis of the amount and period computed as above, with respect to it. When the property shall, however, come to pass to those taking the fee or principalabsolutely, such remainderman or reversioner shall not be entitled against any prior interest to make or collect any charge with respect to any unexpired part of the computed terms of such prior interest, but all unpaid or undischarged burden computed as aforesaid to be paid by any prior interests, shall be borne by the remainderman or reversioner as a diminution of capital, and as arising from acceleration of the computed period of determination of prior interests.

FORM 424 DEFINITION AS TO STOCK DIVIDENDS 

All stock dividends, payable to the trust estate including the sale value of any subscription rights, or the right to exercise subscription rights, shall be considered as income and so received and accounted for by the trustee hereunder. In all cases where subscription rights accrue, and the right is not sold, but is in the judgment of the trustee exercised and stock taken by the trustee, it shall make an appraisal of the cash value of the stock so acquired over and in excess of the price paid by the trustee therefor, and (adjusting fractional shares in cash) stock acquired to the cash value of such excess, shall be set apart and considered and disposed of as a stock dividend is hereunder to be disposed of. 

FORM 425-DIVIDENDS CONSIDERED INCREMENT 

No stock dividends, or rights to subscribe for stock whether availed of, or sold by the trustee, shall be considered as income, but the same shall be considered as an increment in value of principal and be added to principal account of the fund in which the stock is held upon which such dividend or right accrued after deduction therefrom or a charge against the same of all income taxes payable with respect thereto. [89]

Estate Distributions

FORM 426-DISTRIBUTION IN KIND 

The trustee in making distribution of principal hereunder, may do so in money, securities or other property and the judgment of the trustee as to what shall constitute a just and proper division or apportionment among beneficiaries shall be binding and conclusive on all parties. 

FORM 427—DISTRIBUTION IN KIND (ANOTHER FORM) 

The trustee may make any distribution directed hereunder in kind, according to its discretion, but any securities or other property so distributed in kind shall be taken at their fair market value, or if same have not a market value, then at such value as the trustee shall consider proper. 

FORM 428-METHOD OF DISTRIBUTION; TRUSTEE TO MAKE APPRAISAL; TRUSTEE TO DISTRIBUTE IN KIND; POWER OF BENEFICIARY TO ENFORCE PROMPT DISTRIBUTION 

In making any distribution hereunder, the trustee (and its successor and successors in trust) shall be and it is and they are hereby expressly empowered to proceed to set apart and designate the property, whether real or personal, or both, to be transferred to each or any distributee, as follows: Said trustee shall make an appraisal at what it deems are fair market values, of all property out of which such distribution is to be made, and shall set apart for each or any distributee such parcels and lots of property, either real or personal, or both, as in its opinion shall be to the best interest of all concerned, making up at such appraised values the aggregate to be distributed to each, and transfer and convey in full satisfaction and discharge of all and every right or claim of each distributee hereunder the property so set apart as the share of such distributee. Such partition or division by the trustee shall be conclusive and until such division is made by or on behalf of the trustee, no legal title shall vest in the distributee in any such property. Provided, that on failure of such trustee, however, duly to make such division within ten days of the time distribution is herein provided to be made, the interest of such distributee shall be taken to vest as an undivided interest and such distributee may proceed to secure the same to be set apart by such legal remedy as he or she may select.

FORM 429-TRUSTEE EMPOWERED TO ALLOT TRUST PREMISES, DETERMINE VALUES 

My trustee shall have power at any time or times to allot any part or parts of the trust premises in the actual state of investment thereof for the time being or in money in or towards satisfaction of any share of the trust premises, and conclusively to determine in such manner as my trustee shall think fit the value of the trust premises or any part or parts thereof for the purpose of such allotment and to sell the same or any part thereof for the purpose of allotting the proceeds in money and to make such conveyances and transfers of any property so allotted or sold as may be proper and every such allotment shall take effect from such time or times and be made in such manner as my trustees may think proper. 

FORM 430-LEGACIES PAID IN SECURITIES INSTEAD OF CASH, LARGEST FIRST 

I should much prefer (although I do not require) that legacies shall be paid in securities instead of cash, and I authorize my executors, in their sole discretion to pay either all or any legacy or legacies in this way, and to pay all or any part of any legacy in securities rather than in cash. So far as they can do so, I suggest but do not require, that they permit legatees to make selection of the securities to be taken, at the market value, the choice to be exercised in the order of the sizes of the legacies, that is to say: the largest is to have the first choice and so on down. This, however, I leave entirely to the discretion of my executors.

FORM 431-DISTRIBUTION TO BE IN SPECIE RATHER THAN BY CONVERSION INTO MONEY; EXECUTORS AND TRUSTEES, HOWEVER, TO HAVE DISCRE- TION TO DECIDE WHAT IS TO BE DISTRIBUTED BY SPECIE AND WHAT BY CONVERSION; “NON-LEGALS” MAY BE ALLOTTED TO DISTRIBUTIVE SHARES 

In making such division of my residuary estate into---- --parts, I hereby authorize and empower my said executors and trustees to divide in specie any shares of stocks, bonds, scrip, or other securities, of any corporation, which I may own at the time of my death, allotting to each part an equal amount of [90]

such stocks, bonds, scrip or other securities as far as practicable; my preference being that there shall be such a division in specie rather than the conversion thereof into money for the purposes of such division, except as to such stocks, bonds, scrip, or other securities as my executors and trustees may in their judgment deem it best to sell; and I direct that their judgment as to what shall be divided in specie, and what shall be sold, shall be final and conclusive on all parties interested in my estate. 

For the same purpose I hereby authorize my said executors and trustees to allot such parts, or any of them, any stocks, bonds, scrip or other securities that I may own at the time of my death, and whether as to those of the parts which are bequeathed in trust, they are securities in which a trustee is authorized to invest by law or not; and the judgment of my executors as to the propriety of such allotment and the relative value of any securities so allotted shall be final and conclusive on all parties interested in my estate.

FORM 432-RIGHT OF MAJORITY OF EXECUTORS TO VALUE ESTATE AND DISTRI- BUTE IN KIND 

To divide my residuary estate in kind, or partly in kind, among the persons entitled thereto, my executors, acting by a majority of them, to have the right to value my estate as of the date of such distribution in kind, and to determine what property shall be delivered in kind to each of the residuary legatees; provided, however, that, so far as may be advisable, unless another arrangement shall be agreed to by the residuary legatees, etc. (Here insert method best adaptable to needs of beneficiaries and condition of estate.) 

FORM 433-DISTRIBUTION IN KIND BY EXECUTRIX AND EXECUTOR 

And I do expressly absolve and release said executrix and executor from any and all duty and obligation to sell, convert, collect, or otherwise realize on my property, assets or securities which I may own at the time of my death, and do declare that a transfer and delivery in kind of any property or securities received, acquired or invested in by said executrix and executor, or both, shall be full and complete protection to them and a performance of their duties hereunder. 

FORM 434-APPRAISAL OF EXECUTORS AND TRUSTEES FINAL 

In dividing my residuary estate, or any trust fund held under this will, into shares, my executors or my said trustees, as the case may be, may, in their discretion allot to any share any specific security or securities, or other property belonging to my estate or to such trust fund, real or personal, or any undivided interest therein, and to that end they may value or appraise the property belonging to my estate or to such trust fund, and the judgment of my said executors or my said trustees, as the case may be, as to the propriety of such allotment and the value for the purposes of such allotment of any securities or property so allotted, shall be final and conclusive upon all persons interested in my estate. In making such division they shall not be bound by the market value of any security or other property, but shall form their own judgment of value, having regard to the income-producing power of the investment, its safety and permanence and such other factors as they may deem material upon the question of value. 

FORM 435-MAXIMUM TERM OF TRUST 

(To provide against the possibility of the trust being held illegal because it might, by its terms, extend beyond the limit provided under any statutory or common law rule against perpetuities, this form is advisable if distribution of principal is beyond the lives of persons in being.) 

The trust hereby created shall in no event continue for a period longer than the lives of all of herein named, and the survivor of all of them, and twenty-one years thereafter, at the end of which time distribution shall be made to the persons then receiving the income and in the manner herein provided, irrespective of their then attained ages.

FORM 436-TRUSTS IN PERPETUITY 

Name of Bank or Trust Company

I give and bequeath to my trustee hereinafter named (or to The_ as trustee) the sum of_. --Dollars, to be held by it as a trust fund invested and re-invested, and to pay the net income therefrom to_. and... ----corporation; such income to be received and used by said corporation in perpetuity in carrying out its purposes of-- 119 [91]

FORM 437-POWER TO TRUSTEE TO HOLD REAL AND PERSONAL PROPERTY AS A UNIT WITH CERTIFICATES OF INTEREST 

I hereby authorize and direct that the trustee hereunder shall from and after my death, hold, retain, manage and control the following property, or so much thereof as shall be retained undisposed of by said trustee after carrying into effect the other provisions of my will, viz: (Here insert description of property) in trust upon the following terms, trusts and conditions: 

First. Said trustee shall hold such trust under the name or designation "Estate of------ " and is expressly authorized by such name, by itself as trustee to conduct any action, to make contracts, deeds, leases and conduct all transactions hereby authorized, provided said trustee may in its discretion and wherever required or in its judgment expedient, so conduct any action, or make any contract, deed or lease in its name as trustee.

Second. Said trustee shall receive, control and hold such property with powers as follows: 

The trustee shall have power to sell or lease upon such terms and for such consideration as it may deem proper from time to time, the real or personal estate to it hereby devised in trust, and any and all property hereafter acquired by it under and subject to such trust, and in such lots and parcels as may seem to be expedient and may execute all deeds, leases, or other instruments necessary or proper to carry into effect the terms of this instrument. Such leases may be for a term of years beyond the possible life of the trust hereby created. The trustee shall have power to encumber the real estate hereby devised by mortgage or deed of trust, and to renew or extend any mortgage or deed of trust at my death or thereafter maturing, and which is a lien on any of such property. (The above may be varied or enlarged: reference to other trustee forms will afford suggestions.) 

Third. Said trustee shall issue to each of the beneficiaries herein below designated, certificates of interest in said trust property to set aside under said trust of the "Estate of-- " in words and figures as follows:

as trustee under the will of___ hereby certifies and declares

It is an express condition of this certificate that no interest, whether undivided or otherwise, shall be held or considered to be vested in any holder of such certificate in or to any real estate or interest in real estate, or any personal property held by the trustee in trust as aforesaid, but the sole and exclusive right of the holder of such certificate shall be held to be one for an accounting, as of personal property, for money which shall be the proceeds or avails of the property held by such trustee in trust, and be subject to distribution or payment by such trustee in accordance with and subject to the terms of the trust by said will set forth and established, and to distribution on termination of said trust, as in and by said will provided.

in trust for and during a period of (not to exceed 21 years)____years [92]

and shall from the net income derived therefrom, deduct all of its expenses and compensations to said trustee at the rate of_-_-_-_-per centum per annum upon the amount of all gross income by it received, and at the rate of_____ --per centum per annum upon all principal amounts by it collected and reduced to cash and re-invested (the renewal of any existing loan to be considered as a collection and re-investment, but no collection upon any investment to be so considered which shall involve collection and re-investment within a period of one year of a sum within such year theretofore invested). The trustee shall be authorized to employ brokers, attorneys and agents in and about the sale or leasing of property and collection of rents or income or principal and to pay, as part of its expenses, usual and reasonable charges to the persons so employed.

After deduction of such expenses and compensation, said trustee shall next deduct from such income such amount as it shall deem requisite and necessary to set aside for depreciation, for repairs and as reserve against losses, and all balance of such income shall be by said trustee divided annually, or at such semiannual periods as said trustee may deem advisable, in addition to annual distributions, and paid pro rata upon each share to the several and respective holders of said certificates, according to the number of shares designated in the certificates held by each.

Fifth. In the event, and only in the event of there being available for distribution to holders of such certificates, an amount exceeding an annual return for any one period of one year in excess of ten (10) per cent upon the value of the principal of said estate in the hands of said trustee at the beginning of such year, upon values to be appraised by said trustee, said trustee may be authorized by the holders of a majority in amount of said certificates to purchase certificates outstanding as follows: Upon written request of such majority of such holders delivered to the trustee within thirty days of and prior to the yearly period of distribution (the date thereof to be by said trustee fixed for at least two years in advance from time to time) that bids be solicited for the surrender to the trustee of certificates issued hereunder, the trustee shall mail to each holder of any such certificates at the last address of such holder of such certificate registered with the trustee, a written notice that at a date therein fixed, to be not less than twenty days after the date of mailing such notice, the trustee will receive bids for the surrender to and cancellation by the trustee of certificates issued hereunder, such bids to be submitted at the price for each single proportional share hereunder represented by a certificate, at which the owner is willing to surrender and cancel all interest or claim thereunder, and that to the amount then available as aforesaid, to be mentioned in such notice, the trustee will purchase at the lowest prices offered, such number of such shares as may be acquired with the funds available, each bidder to agree to surrender at the price named by him, all or any part of the number of shares of interest by him or her owned, but the trustee to apportion the number of shares by it taken pro rata on all equal bids, so far as the acquirement of only even, and non-fractional shares may permit. And the trustee on giving such notice shall be entitled to so acquire such shares and on giving notice of its application of the funds available to the cancellation of the shares by it designated to be selected as aforesaid; all right, title and interest to such shares shall terminate and the only interest remaining in the holder of the certificate therefor shall be to receive the money so applied and so to be paid and received for surrender and cancellation thereof.

Sixth. No holder of any such certificate shall have any voice with respect to the determination of the terms or price of any sale, lease, mortage, or other transaction of the trustee, save and except as herein expressly provided, nor shall any such holder have any interest whatever in the real or personal estate by and under this trust devised and bequeathed, but the interest of each owner of any such certificate shall extend only to such of the income and proceeds of such property as may under the terms hereof be distributable from time to time, to the holders of such certificates, and no holder of any such certificate shall by any instrument or agreement have any right or power to anticipate or assign or transfer any right, title and interest in or to or under any future distribution; but as and when any distribution is made, only the then owners hereunder of the shares hereunder shall be entitled to receive the same. 

Seventh. No widow, heir-at-law or devisee of any owner of such certificate shall have any right of dower or right of homestead or right of inheritance or any other real property right, as distinguished from personal property right, statutory or otherwise, in or to any of the real estate at any time in trust hereunder, No administrator or executor, heir or legatee shall be entitled to transfer of any certificate save upon due satisfaction of or provision for payment of all and every inheritance or succession tax payable with respect thereto.

Eighth. The relation of the respective owners from time to time of said certificates for shares, shall be simply and only that of co-beneficiaries hereunder, entitled solely to distribution as and when made hereunder, and no partnership or relation of principal and agent or any joint interest in any property is intended to be or shall be held to be hereunder established as between such holders. 

Ninth. The trustee shall not be liable for any losses in money, in market value of property, or otherwise in the conduct of the trust hereunder, except such losses as shall directly result from a willful breach upon its part, and the trustee shall not be liable for the error, mistake or default of an attorney or agent by it employed in the exercise of ordinary care and discretion. The discretion of the trustee when duly exercised by its officers, or by its executive committee of its Board of Directors with regard to any matter regarding [93]

which the trustee is given discretion hereunder, shall be final and absolute, and shall not be subject to review by any court, but shall have the full effect of the exercise of a full and unconditional power of appointment by the trustee.

Tenth. The trustee hereunder may, as to all duties, powers and title as to any part of such trust, resign at any time upon giving notice thirty days prior to the date when its resignation shall become effective, to all holders of certificates issued hereunder and then outstanding, but only by mailing such notice to such last address of each such holder as may be registered with such trustee, or upon removal of the trustee by order or decree of any court or upon such resignation and upon such notice being given, the majority in amount of such certificate holders may before or within--------days after the time specified in such notice, when the resignation becomes effective, by a writing by them executed and acknowledged, appoint a new trustee, and when such written appointment is recorded in the 

County of____ Name of Recording Office State of_____ 

as deeds then be recorded, such trustee therein named shall may in all respects be vested with all title, powers, rights and discretion herein vested in the original trustee, and successive resignations may so be made.

Eleventh. At the end of the period hereinabove stated for the term of this trust, the trustee shall make distribution pro rata, each share outstanding being entitled to one equal share of all property in its hands hereunder, to the holders of certificates issued hereunder then outstanding and thereupon the said trust shall be terminated and said trustee thereby discharged. In the event that any holder of any certificate shall not be found, or shall refuse to accept or receive distribution as to him tendered by the trustee, the trustee shall, only as to such holder or holders respectively and severally, and only with respect to the property tendered to or which the trustee has set apart for conveyance to such holder or holders, continue as trustee to hold title to and manage such property for and on behalf of such holder or holders, severally and not jointly, but only so long as may be necessary to settle and determine the trust with respect thereto by conveyance to the beneficiary.

FORM 450RESIGNATION OF CO-TRUSTEE 

In case at any time, upon the death, resignation, failure or inability of a co-trustee to act hereunder, there then being no person theretofore appointed as such co-trustee who is willing and competent to act hereunder, the trustee hereunder shall give mailed written notice to each beneficiary at the time entitled to receive income hereunder, who shall be of the age of at least twenty-one (21) years, setting forth such vacancy and the right of such beneficiaries within the time and in the manner here provided to choose a successor co-trustee; and thereupon such beneficiaries may within thirty (30) days after the mailing of such written notice, by written instrument signed by at least a majority of such beneficiaries and lodged with the trustee hereunder select a successor co-trustee to fill such vacancy. In event of the failure of such beneficiaries to so select a successor co-trustee within said time, then the Board of Directors of The__‒‒‒ Company shall within thirty (30) days after such failure, by resolution duly adopted by at least a majority vote, select a disinterested competent person to act as such successor co-trustee. And a successor co-trustee chosen and appointed by either of the foregoing methods shall, upon acceptance of said office, have all the powers, duties and discretions of the co-trustees hereunder. 

FORM 451-DISSOLUTION, MERGER OR CONSOLIDATION OF CORPORATE TRUSTEE 

If at any time the corporate existence or trust powers of the corporate trustee of a trust created hereunder shall terminate, or its charter shall be forfeited, and, if a new corporation with trust powers shall be organized pursuant to then existing law, for the purpose of continuing the business of the trustee, or if at any time such trustee shall become merged in or consolidated with any other corporation, having corporate power to administer trusts, such new or other corporation shall become the trustee of such trust and it or any other successor corporate trustee, however appointed, shall be vested with all and the same rights, titles, estates, interests, powers, discretions, duties, privileges and elections, without the necessity of any further act or conveyance, as if originally named herein.

FORM 457-POWER OF MAJORITY OF EXECUTORS TO REMOVE TRUSTEE AND APPOINT NEW TRUSTEE; NOT TO APPOINT THEMSELVES; WAIVER OF SECURITY 

I authorize and empower the persons hereinafter named as executors and executrix of this will, or a majority of such of them as shall from time to time be living, by an instrument in writing executed under their hands and seals and acknowledged in like manner as a deed to be recorded, to revoke the appointment of trustee of any or all of the trusts in and by this will created, and to appoint any person or persons, resident or non-resident of this State, including any foreign or domestic corporation having power by its charter to execute trusts, to be trustee of any of said trusts in respect to which such power of revocation shall be exercised and from time to time to revoke such appointments, and to appoint again a new trustee or trustees. 

But the power to appoint new trustees hereby given shall not be construed to authorize the appointment of any of the persons named in this will as executors or executrix to be such new trustee and any trustee or trustees, appointed as herein provided, shall have and possess all and singular the same powers, rights, privileges, elections and discretions, and shall be subject to all and singular the same duties and obligations as are in and by this will conferred upon the original trustee of such trusts respectively. And no trustee to be appointed in pursuance of the power herein given shall be required to give any security as such trustee. 

FORM 458-RESIGNATION OR DEATH OF TRUSTEE 

In event any of the trustees appointed under this my will shall die before the said trusts be fully executed or desire to renounce the trust or become disqualified from acting as such trustee, I authorize and empower the surviving or remaining trustees or trustee should they deem it advisable and for the best interest of my estate by suitable instrument, executed by them jointly if there be more than one, and duly acknowledged, to appoint a suitable person to become a trustee in the place of him or her so dying, renouncing or becoming disqualified and said newly appointed trustee shall succeed to all the rights and obligations and be clothed with the same powers as if originally named for that purpose in this my will.

FORM 460-DURING ABSENCE IN FOREIGN COUNTRY 

Either trustee or executor under my will may appoint from time to time to act for him while he is absent from the United States of America at any time, any other trustee or executor under my will and by a written power of attorney confer upon such attorney or appointee all such powers and authority including any and all discretionary powers as the person or constituent executing such power of attorney could have possessed or exercised as trustee or executor if personally present and acting or confer upon such attorney or appointee any less powers and all powers and authority so conferred shall be valid and effectual to all intents and purposes as if done in person by the person or constituent executing said power of attorney.

FORM 461-—POWER TO DELEGATE DUTIES 

I authorize my executors and any of my trustees to delegate to any other of them any trust, duty, or power, conferred upon my executors and trustees under my will, or any codicil, or codicils, thereto. In case of the absence, or inability to act, of any one of my executors, or trustees, I authorize and empower the other executor, or trustee, or the other two trustees, except as otherwise herein specifically provided, to act and conduct the business of my estate in all matters covered, or provided for, by my will.

Examples of Complete Wills SIMPLE WILL NAMING WIFE EXECUTRIX 

I, of the City of 

County of and State of_ 

do make, publish and declare this my Last Will and Testament hereby revoking any and all wills by me heretofore made.

ITEM I. I direct that all my just debts, funeral expenses and the cost of administering my estate be paid as soon after my decease as may be found convenient. ITEM II. All the rest and residue of my estate, which I may own or have the right to dispose of at the time of my decease, I give, devise and bequeath to my wife absolutely and forever. 

ITEM III. I hereby nominate and appoint my wife, the executrix of this my Last Will and Testament with full power to sell, lease, mortgage, hypothecate, extend indebtedness or in any other way to deal with and dispose of the whole or any part of my estate, without order or leave of court and to execute any deeds or other documents necessary to carry out any of the foregoing purposes; to compound, compromise and to settle and adjust any and all claims or debts for or against my estate as fully as I could do if living and I request that she be not required to give any bond or security for the performance of her duties as such executrix. 

In Witness Whereof, I have hereunto set my hand to this my Last Will and Testament this.. day of..
