SIMPLE WILL LEAVING EVERYTHING TO WIFE WITH EXPLANATION WHY; AND APPOINTING CORPORATE FIDUCIARY EXECUTOR 

I, a resident of__ being of sound mind and memory, do make, publish and declare this my last will and testament, hereby revoking any and all Wills by me heretofore made. 

ITEM I. I direct that all my just debts and funeral expenses be paid as soon after my decease as may be found convenient. *ITEM II. (a) I give, devise and bequeath all of my property, real, personal and mixed, of whatever nature and kind and wheresoever situate, which I may own or have the right to dispose of at the time of my decease to my beloved wife, to have and to hold the same to her own uses absolutely and forever. (If there are or may be children, add the following): (b) Having every confidence that my wife will use any property that I may leave for the best interest of any children we may have, as well as herself, I purposely make no provision for any such child, whether now living or born hereafter, either during my lifetime or after my decease. 

ITEM III. I hereby nominate and appoint the__ 

its successor or successors as the executor of this my Last Will and Testament with full power and authority at its discretion to do any and all things necessary and proper for the complete administration of my estate, including the power to sell at public or private sale and without order of court any real or personal property belonging to my estate. 

The executor is authorized and empowered to execute, acknowledge and deliver any deeds or other instruments necessary or desirable in order to carry out any of the foregoing purposes and to compound, compromise or otherwise to settle or adjust any and all charges, debts and demands whatsoever against or in favor of my estate, as fully as I could do if living. 

In Witness Whereof, I have hereunto set my hand to this my Last Will and Testament this---day of...

Signed, Published and Declared by the said. to be his last will and testament in our presence, who at his request and in his presence and in the presence of each other, we believing him to be of sound and disposing mind and memory, have hereunto subscribed our names as witnesses. 

--residing at.. *(For another way of expressing this same idea see Form No. 7.) [106]

CONDITIONAL WILL County of___ 

I, of the City of---- ___and State of_-do make, publish and declare this instrument as and for my Last Will and Testament, hereby revoking all former Wills by me made.

ITEM I. I direct that all my just debts, last illness, funeral and administrative expenses be paid as soon after my decease as may be conveniently done. 

ITEM II. I give and bequeath to my wife, all of my wearing apparel, jewelry, watches, books, furniture, silverware, household goods and other household effects and any automobiles I may own at the time of my decease, the same to be hers absolutely; but if my said wife does not survive me, then I give and bequeath said property to my children, share and share alike. 

ITEM III. I give and bequeath to each of my children me surviving, or to the issue per stirpes of any child who predeceases me, the sum of $-. 

If any such child or issue of a deceased child be a minor at the time of my decease, the sum bequeathed to him or her shall be held by----of___ of.. in trust nevertheless, to pay to or for such child such sum or sums out of the income or principal of his or her bequest as said trustee may deem necessary or proper to provide for the suitable education of such child until he or she shall attain the age of twenty-one (21) years, at which time any unexpended portion of such bequest shall be paid to said child and the trust as to such one shall thereupon cease and determine.

ITEM IV. All the rest, residue and remainder of my estate, real, personal or mixed, wherever situate, which I may own or have the right to dispose of at the time of my decease, I give, devise and bequeath to my wife, --, absolutely and forever. 

ITEM V. In event my wife predeceases me or her death takes place within thirty days after my own,

I give, devise and bequeath all of the property mentioned in the next preceding item of this Will to in trust nevertheless, subject to terms and conditions as follows: _of___ (111 (Here set forth Powers of Trustee and clauses relating to disposition of the estate as may best conform to desires of testator.) 

ITEM VI. I further declare, anything to the contrary notwithstanding, that my wife, is fully authorized and empowered at her option and in her uncontrolled discretion to determine whether or not this will shall be Probated as my Last Will and Testament or whether in the alternative I shall die intestate in respect to any and all property of which I may die seized; and this Will is made by me strictly subject to the foregoing conditions. 

ITEM VII. I hereby nominate and appoint my wife, the executrix of this my Last

Will and Testament and in event of her death, resignation, incapacity or refusal to act, I nominate and appoint of the city of--its corporate successor or successors, Executors hereof. All references to my executrix herein shall be applicable to my executor. 

I authorize and empower my executrix to sell at public or private sale and without order of court, or to retain unsold all or any part of my estate, without liability for any loss or depreciation by reason of any such sale or retention; to lease, mortgage, hypothecate, exchange, manage, improve, control and in any other way to use and deal with all or any part of my estate as though the absolute owner thereof; to execute, acknowledge and deliver all conveyances and instruments in writing necessary to carry out any of the foregoing purposes and to compromise, settle and adjust any and all claims for or against my estate as fully as I could do if living. I direct that no bond or other underwriting be required of my executrix in the administration of my estate.

In Witness Whereof, I have set my hand to this my Last Will and Testament this A. D. 193__. -9 ----day of

JOINT WILL 

We, and State of---- __and_ residents of the City of---.

County of___ 

being of sound mind and memory, do hereby make, publish and declare this our last will and testament, hereby revoking all wills by us heretofore made: 

ITEM I. It is our will that all our just debts, funeral expenses and costs of administration be paid out of the principal of our estates as soon after our death as may be found convenient. 

ITEM II. We give, devise and bequeath all of the residue and remainder of our estates of whatever nature or kind and wheresoever situate, which we may own or have the right to dispose of at the time of our decease, absolutely and forever each of us unto the other, meaning thereby that the survivor of us shall be the owner of all that either of us possess. 

ITEM III. In the event of simultaneous death, or because of a common accident or calamity, or under any circumstances causing doubt as to which of us survives the other, then we hereby give, devise and bequeath our entire remaining estates to the... .__of__ in trust nevertheless, for the uses and purposes as follows:

County of... in trust nevertheless for the uses and purposes as follows: 

State of____ POWERS AND DUTIES OF TRUSTEE 

1. To receive, hold and manage all the property of the trust estate, and to collect, receive and recover all the rents, issues and profits thereof. 

2. To sell and exchange or for such purpose as it may deem for the best interests of the trust estate to retain unsold any real estate, stocks, bonds and other property belonging to the same without liability for loss or depreciation in value by reason of any such sale or retention as the case may be. 

3. To make alterations of, repairs upon, additions to, and to erect improvements upon real estate, and to sell, join in, or make plats and subdivisions thereof, and to make or join in leases of real estate upon such terms as the Trustee may deem advisable, the Trustee being given express power to execute leases for a term or terms that may extend beyond the duration of the trusts hereof. 

4. To extend indebtedness, make advances and borrow money on such terms and conditions at any time or times, for such purpose or purposes as it may deem necessary or proper for the improvement or preservation of the trust estate. For the payment of such advances with interest and the payment of its charges, commissions and necessary expenses the Trustee shall be given a first and prior lien upon the trust estate and for money borrowed or advanced may issue its promissory note or notes as Trustee and secure the repayment thereof by hypothecating, mortgaging, or otherwise incumbering all or any part of said trust estate. 

5. To exercise or dispose of all rights in respect to any stocks, bonds or other securities belonging to the trust estate and to join by pledge or otherwise in any plan of reorganization or other means of protecting or dealing with such stocks, bonds or other securities as it may deem best and as it would have the power to do if the absolute owner thereof. 

6. To determine whether money or property coming into its possession shall be treated as principal or income and to charge or apportion gains, expenses and losses to principal or income according as it may deem just and equitable. 

7. To employ suitable agents and attorneys and to pay them reasonable compensation and expenses. 

The Trustee shall not be liable for any neglect, omission or wrong doing of such agents or attorneys, provided reasonable care shall have been exercised in their selection, nor save for its own neglect or willful default, for any loss or liability it may incur by reason of any of its acts or commissions as such Trustee. 

8. To invest and reinvest the funds, securities and real estate at any time constituting the trust estate in such loans, stocks, bonds or other securities and real estate as it may deem proper for the investment of trust funds, without being restricted to a class of investments which a Trustee is or may hereafter be permitted by law to make. 

9. Any division or distribution of the trust estate required under the provisions of this my last will and testament may be made by the Trustee in kind, that is to say, in real estate, stocks, bonds, mortgages or other property belonging to the trust estate, but if so distributed the same shall be taken at their fair market value, and if no market value is obtainable, then at such value as the Trustee shall consider just and equitable. 

10. Any corporation having trust powers, whether a State or National institution, into which the Trustee may be converted or merged or with which it may be consolidated or to which it may sell or transfer its business or assets, as a whole or substantially as a whole, or any corporation, resultnig from such merger, conversion, consolidation, sale or transfer to which the Trustee shall be a party, shall without any affirmative act, stipulation or agreement of the parties hereto, become the successor of the trustee hereunder and shall succeed to and be vested with all of the title of the Trustee in and to the whole of the trust estate, and shall be vested with all of the powers, discretions, immunities and privileges, and shall be charged with all of the duties and obligations, with like effect as if such merged or resulting or substituted corporation were herein named as original Trustee. 

11. In the event the Trustee shall resign, become disqualified, cease to act as such Trustee or its doors be closed as a banking or financial institution for voluntray liquidation or by operation of law, my wife during her lifetime, or a majority of the adult beneficiaries hereunder, after her decease, are hereby authorized and empowered at her or their discretion by an instrument in writing, duly executed and acknowledged, to select as successor Trustee any bank or trust company duly incorporated under the laws of the 

State of authorized to perform the duties as such Trustee and having its principal place of business in the City of and the bank or trust company so selected is hereby appointed Trustee hereunder with all the powers, duties and obligations herein conferred upon my original Trustee. -19 [110]

DISPOSITION OF TRUST ESTATE 

The Trustee is authorized and empowered after deducting its commissions and proper and necessary expenses in connection with the administration of the trust estate to pay and apply the net income and the principal thereof in the manner and upon the terms and conditions as follows: in convenient 1. To pay the net income derived from the trust estate to my wife, installments per annum during the term of her natural life and in addition thereto the Trustee is directed to pay to her from the principal of the trust estate such sum or sums at any time or times as it shall deem necessary or proper to provide for her suitable support, comfort and maintenance or for the education of any of my children that may be dependent upon her, should her income from this or from other sources, prove in the judgment of the Trustee inadequate for such purposes. 

2. Subject to the foregoing provisions and after my wife's decease, or in event she shall not survive me, the trust estate shall be divided into four (4) equal shares and one such share shall be held for the benefit of each of my children as follows: --> 6111 Any lawful child of mine born hereafter either during my lifetime or after my decease shall be treated as an equal shareholder with my other children in the foregoing division. 

3. Until any such child shall attain the age of twenty-one (21) years the Trustee is authorized and empowered to pay to or for the benefit of such one from the principal or income of his or her share such sum or sums as it shall deem necessary or proper to provide for the suitable support, comfort and maintenance and education of such child, and if and when such child shall attain said age of twenty-one (21) years the Trustee shall thereupon pay to such one the entire net income derived from the share held for such child in convenient installments per annuum until he or she shall attain the age of thirty (30) years, and if and when such child shall attain said age of thirty (30) years the Trustee is directed to distribute to him or her the principal of his or her share of the trust estate and the trust as to such share shall thereupon cease and determine. 

4. In event of the decease of any of my children before distribution to such one shall have been completed the share of such one or the part thereof remaining in the hands of the Trustee shall be held for the issue of such child or some one or more of them in such proportions and subject to such terms and conditions as such child may direct by his or her last will and testament, provided, however, that if any such child shall leave a surviving spouse alive at the time of my decease he or she may by last will and testament direct the net income from the whole or any part of such share to be paid to such spouse until his or her death or re-marriage, whichever shall first occur. In default of the exercise of such power of testamentary disposition or in so far as the same shall be void, shall not extend, or take effect, such share or portion thereof then remaining in the hands of the Trustee shall vest in and be paid and distributed to the surviving issue of such child in equal shares per stirpes, and in default of issue the same shall be equally added to the share held or intended to be held for my remaining children, their testamentary appointees, or issue as the case may be, and in event there be no such children, testamentary appointees, or issue then surviving, the trust estate shall be paid and distributed to my next of kin under the laws and statutes of the State of____ 

relating to descent and distribution in force and effect at the time of my decease.

5. If the issue of any child of mine or other person named as beneficiary herein becomes entitled to a share in the trust estate before attaining the age of twenty-one (21) years the share of such person shall be held and kept invested by the Trustee and the income and principal, or so much thereof as the Trustee in its absolute discretion considers necessary or advisable shall be used for the benefit of such person until he or she attains the age of twenty-one (21) years. The Trustee may make any payments for any person under the age of twenty-one (21) years to the parent or guardian of such person, whose receipt shall be a sufficient discharge to the Trustee. 

6. Notwithstanding anything hereinbefore contained, I will and direct that the income which by the terms hereof is or shall be payable to any person shall only be so payable until such person shall by specific or general assignment, transfer, or otherwise alienate or dispose of or attempt to alienate or dispose of his or her right to such income or any part thereof or interest therein or until by reason of or in consequence of or under any attachment, sequestration, execution, writ, judgment, order or other proceeding, or by any other means whatsoever, the said income or any part thereof or interest therein can no longer be personally enjoyed by him or her, or would but for the terms of this will belong to or become vested in or subject to the control of some other person, persons or corporation, courts or officials, and from and after the happening of any such event the right of such person to receive and be paid the said income shall cease, but the Trustee, in its absolute and uncontrolled discretion may pay over to or expend for the benefit of his wife and children, or her husband and children, as the case may be, the whole or any part of such income as it may think fit during its pleasure; the balance (if any) of such income to be paid or applied by the 

Trustee for the benefit of such child, grandchild or grandchildren of mine as the Trustee, in its absolute and uncontrolled discretion sees fit; provided always that if and so soon as (but not before) the reason and cause whereby the said person can no longer personally enjoy the said income as above mentioned, shall come to or be at an end, then in such case such income shall again be paid over to him or her as before [111]

the happening of any of the events above mentioned, until the again happening of any such event, and so often as the same shall happen the right of the said person to be paid such income shall cease and the Trustee shall again deal with such income in its absolute and uncontrolled discretion and otherwise as above provided for until the reason or cause whereby such person can no longer personally enjoy the said income as above mentioned, has again come to or is at an end as above mentioned, and then shall again pay over to him or her the said income as before, and so on from time to time as the case may be when and so often as any such event may happen and the reason or cause as aforesaid has come to or is at an end as above mentioned.

WILL CREATING TRUST FOR WIFE WITH BROAD POWERS, SIMPLY STATED PREAMBLE: 

I, Edward A. Jones, of Cleveland, Ohio, do make, publish and declare this instrument as and for my last will and testament, hereby revoking all former wills and codicils by me made. 

DEBTS: 

ITEM I. I direct that all my just debts and funeral expenses shall be paid first out of the principal of my estate as soon after my decease as the same can be done conveniently. 

NOTE: Inquiry should be made as to whether testator desires to have a monument erected, as the executor will have no authority to do so unless given in the will. 

HOUSEHOLD GOODS, ETC.: 

ITEM II. If my wife, Mary A. Jones, survives me, I give and bequeath to her all of my wearing apparel, jewelry, watches, books, pictures, silverware, household goods and other household effects and also any automobiles owned by me at the time of my death, the same to be hers absolutely; but if my said wife does not survive me, then I give and bequeath the property in this item mentioned share and share alike to my children. 

NOTE: At this point other legacies, if any, to relatives, servants, charities, etc., should be set forth.

DEVISE OF RESIDUE: 

ITEM III. All the rest, residue and remainder of my estate, of every kind, nature and description, real and personal, wherever the same may be situated and whenever by me acquired, and including any property which I may have the right to dispose of by will, I give, devise and bequeath to The Union Trust Company, [112]

of Cleveland, Ohio, or any corporate successor thereof, to be held by it and its corporate successors in trust, nevertheless, for the uses and purposes hereinafter set forth. 

NOTE: If it is known that the testator has been given a power of appointment to dispose by will of the property of another, a specific reference should be made to the instrument creating such power.

POWERS OF TRUSTEE: 

With respect to the trust estate and any parts or shares into which it may be divided, said trustee shall have full power and authority to do all things that an absolute owner might do, and among other things shall handle, manage, control, lease for any term irrespective of the duration of the trust, bargain, sell, transfer, convey, allot, invest and reinvest the same or any part thereof upon such terms, under such conditions and in such manner as it in its discretion shall deem for the best interest of the trust estate, all limitations and restrictions as to the investment of trust funds now or hereafter enacted or prescribed being hereby expressly waived.

NOTE: The foregoing gives to the trustee very broad powers of investment, which may not be desired in all cases. It is to be observed that the statutes of Ohio give to trustees powers of investment which are quite limited, and it would seem unwise to confine the trustee to strictly statutory powers. The testator may specifically set forth the nature of investments to be made, or may forbid investments of a certain nature. However, in view of the rapidly changing forms of modern investments, it would seem advisable to give the trustee considerable latitude rather than to unduly limit his powers.

A provision may be inserted naming some person or persons whose approval the trustee is to secure before making any changes in investments.

It is frequently advisable to provide that the executor and trustee may retain without liability for depreciation, the investments (or certain of them) of the testator, in order that the trustee shall not be obliged to dispose of investments which are not of a trust character at an undue sacrifice. 

Special provision should also be made for the retention and handling of stock in a close corporation in which the testator may have been actively interested. 

FURTHER POWERS: 

In the performance of its duties said trustee is authorized and empowered to comply with all legal requirements as to the execution of deeds, leases, or other writings, documents and formalities, without the order of any court. No person dealing with said trustee shall be bound to inquire concerning the validity of any act of the trustee or be liable for the application of any money or other consideration paid or loaned by such person to such trustee. 

Said trustee shall have full power and authority to determine whether money or other property coming into its possesison shall be treated as income or principal, and its decision with respect thereto shall be binding and conclusive upon the beneficiaries hereunder. 

NOTE: Cases frequently arise where it is difficult to determine as between life tenants and remaindermen what is properly income and principal. In order to avoid the delay and expense of court proceedings, the trustee should be given the right to make the determination. The trustee cannot of course be arbitrary in its decision, but should be placed in position to deal fairly with the interested parties in doubtful cases in order to effectually carry out the testator's plan of distribution.

In the care, management and preservation of the trust estate said trustee shall also have full power and authority to apportion gains, losses and expenses, including its reasonable compensation, to income and/or principal as it shall deem equitable. 

NOTE: There will be occasions when extraordinary expenditures will be required for the proper protection and management of real estate or other assets comprising the trust estate, and the truste should be given authority to allocate such expenses to income and principal. 

TRUSTEE'S BOND: 

I request that my said trustee act in such capacity without bond.

INCOME TO WIFE: 

If my wife, Mary A. Jones, survives me, my trustee shall pay to her quarterly in each year, or oftener in its discretion, the net income derived from the trust estate, so long as she shall live. In the event that at any time said net income so paid to my said wife shall not in the sole judgment of the trustee be sufficient for her care, support and maintenance, and the care, support and education of our children, taking into consideration such other means of support and sources of income as she and our children shall then have, my trustee shall have full right, power and authority to pay to her from time to time such amounts from the [113]

principal of the trust estate as will in its judgment be sufficient (together with said net income and such other means of support and income as my said wife and children shall have), to provide for the aforesaid purposes.

NOTE: It may be desired to make the foregoing provision for the use of principal somewhat broader rather than to provide only for the care, support and maintenance of the widow. Sometimes the testator will wish to make a different provision in the event of the re-marriage of his widow by reducing the amount of income to be received by her and giving a definite amount of income to the children during the widow's life. In all cases it should be remembered that the widow may decline to take under the will and elect to take her share at law, so that the provisions made for her should be sufficiently ample that she will be satisfied with them. 

DIVISION OF TRUST ESTATE UPON WIFE'S DEATH: 

Upon the death of the survivor of my said wife and myself, the trust estate shall be divided into as many equal shares as there are then living children of mine, counting as one such then living child the then living heirs of the body of any child of mine who shall have died prior to such time leaving heirs of the body. My trustee shall pay over and distribute one of such equal shares to the then living heirs of the body of any such deceased child per stirpes, and shall retain in trust one such equal share for each of my then living children.

NOTE: Sometimes the testator desires to continue the trust for the benefit of his grandchildren. 

In an attempt to do this there is danger of violating the so-called rule against perpetuities, prohibiting the postponement of the vesting of an estate beyond the period of a life or lives in being and 21 years thereafter.

DISTRIBUTION OF INCOME AND PRINCIPAL TO SONS: 

With respect to the share of the trust estate held for the benefit of any son of mine, my trustee shall pay over quarterly in each year, or oftener in its discretion, to such son, or to his duly appointed guardian, the net income derived from his share or the undistributed portion thereof. When such son shall have attained the age of thirty years, or at the time of the division of the trust estate into shares if such son shall already have attained that age or more, one-half of his share of the trust estate shall be paid over and distributed to him; and when such son shall have attained the age of forty years, or at the time of the division of the trust estate into shares if he shall already have attained that age or more, the entire remainder of his share shall be paid over and distributed to him. 

NOTE: It will of course depend upon the testator's wishes in view of the circumstances known to him as to the proper ages at which to make distribution of principal to his sons. 

DISTRIBUTION OF INCOME AND PRINCIPAL TO DAUGHTERS: 

With respect to the share of the trust estate held for the benefit of any daughter of mine, my trustee shall pay over quarterly in each year, or oftener in its discretion, to such daughter, or to her duly appointed guardian, the net income derived from her share, so long as she shall live. 

NOTE: The reason for the testator providing that his daughters' shares should be held in trust for their entire lives, was that if they should receive the principal they would not be in position to properly manage investments, and also that the principal might be subjected to the risks of their husbands' business enterprises in cases of emergency. This testator felt that he should insure an income to his daughters regardless of other circumstances. 

TRUSTEE MAY USE PRINCIPAL FOR CHILDREN: 

If at any time after the division of the trust estate into shares the net income so paid to any child of mine or to its duly appointed guardian shall not in the sole judgment of my trustee be sufficient for the care, support and education of such child, taking into consideration such other means of support and sources of income as such child shall then have, my trustee shall have full right, power and authority to pay to or for the use of such child from time to time such amounts from the principal of its share of the trust estate as will in my trustee's judgment be sufficient, together with said net income and such other means of support and income as such child may have, to provide for the aforesaid purposes. 

NOTE: Some testators will consider it advisable to make a provision enabling the trustee to turn over to his children certain amounts of principal for the purpose of engaging in business. 

DISTRIBUTION OF SHARE OF CHILD DYING BEFORE TERMINATION OF TRUST: 

Upon the death of any son of mine before receiving full distribution of his share of the trust estate, or upon the death of any daughter of mine, the portion of the trust estate then held for the benefit of such son or daughter shall be paid over and distributed by my trustee to the then living heirs of the body per stirpes of such son or daughter, if any such heirs there shall be, or in default of such heirs then in equal shares to my other then living children, counting and treating as one such then living child the then living [114]

heirs of the body of any child of mine who shall have died prior to such time leaving such heirs of the body, which heirs shall take per stirpes one such equal share; but any portion of such deceased child's share to which any such other then living child of mine whose own share of the trust estate is then held in trust would be entitled, shall be added to the principal of the share of the trust estate held for such other child and be treated and disposed of as though such added portion has originally constituted a part of the principal of such other child's share. 

NOTE: In this case the testator's children were not given the right to dispose of by will any portions of their shares which were not actually received by them. It is submitted that it would be a good plan in most cases to permit the beneficiaries to make such testamentary disposition, as they will be in better position to decide upon a proper distribution in view of circumstances which will be known to them, but not to the testator at the time his will is made. 

FINAL DISTRIBUTION IF THERE SHALL BE FAILURE OF ISSUE 

If at the time for final distribution of the trust estate or any part thereof there shall not be living any child of mine or any heir of the body of any child of mine, then the trust estate or such part thereof shall thereupon be paid over and distributed by my trustee to my then living heirs at law in accordance with the laws of the State of Ohio then in effect with respect to the descent and distribution of the personal property of an intestate.

NOTE: The foregoing provision enables the trustee to make a distribution to then living persons if there shall be a failure of issue, and eliminates the necessity of successive administrations. 

SPENDTHRIFT CLAUSE: 

It is the intent hereof that the principal and income of the trust estate shall be inalienable by any of the beneficiaries hereunder and shall not be subject to the possible claims of creditors of any of them. 

Accordingly, if any of said beneficiaries shall at any time alienate, charge, dispose of or encumber the net income or principal payable to him or her, or any part thereof, or any interest therein, before the same shall have been paid to him or her under the provisions hereof, or if by reason of bankruptcy or other event such net income or principal shall wholly or in part cease to be personally enjoyed by such one as herein provided and said net income and/or principal, or any part thereof, or any interest therein, would but for this proviso become vested in some other person or persons, then the trust herein expressed concerning the net income and/or principal payable to such one shall thereupon terminate as to him or her and all such net income and/or principal otherwise hereinabove directed to be paid to such one shall thereafter be held and distributed by my trustee during the remainder of his or her life according to its absolute discretion, and the trustee shall thereafter pay to such one for his or her care and support such amounts only from time to time as in its discretion it shall deem fit and proper, and any surplus net income not so used shall be added to the principal from which derived and such principal shall upon the death of such one be disposed of as has hereinabove been provided for the disposition thereof upon his or her death. 

NOTE: It appears to be generally understood that the so-called spendthrift clause is valid in Ohio. 

AFTERBORN CHILDREN: 

At the present time I have two children, namely Ruth B. Jones, born December 15, 1915, and Margaret 

A. Jones, born July 13, 1923, but the words "child" and "children" as used herein shall be held and construed to include not only the aforesaid two children but also any child or children hereafter born to me; and this provision for afterborn children shall be held and construed to be a provision within the contemplation of the statutes of the State of Ohio, and such afterborn children shall receive no part of my estate save and except the portions provided for them in this will. 

NOTE: It is necessary that provisions be made with respect to afterborn children in view of statutory provisions in Ohio. Notwithstanding this provision, the testator should be counselled to give renewed consideration to his will when a child is born. 

DISTRIBUTION IN KIND: 

In making any division or distribution of the trust estate, or any part thereof, my said trustee may make the same in cash or in other property belonging to the trust estate, or partly in cash and partly in such other property, as to it shall seem wise, but if such other property is used whether wholly or in part, the same shall be taken at its fair market value, or if it shall not have a market value then at such value as shall be fixed and determined by my said trustee. 

PROVISIONS IN LIEU OF WIFE'S RIGHTS: 

ITEM IV. The provisions herein made for my said wife, Mary A. Jones, are intended to be in lieu of her dower interest in my real estate and distributive share of my personal property, as well as her claim to a year's support and any and all other rights, interests, and claims which she might otherwise have as my widow in, to or against my estate. [115]

APPOINTMENT AND POWERS OF EXECUTOR: 

ITEM V. I hereby nominate and appoint The Union Trust Company, of Cleveland, Ohio, or any corporate successor thereof, executor of this my last will and testament, giving to my said executor full right. power and authority to bargain, sell, convey, transfer, deed, compromise, settle, adjust, manage and deal with any and all of my property and all obligations or claims against my estate upon such terms and under such conditions as to my said executor shall seem best for the proper settlement of my estate, without the order of any court; and in the execution of said duties and powers said executor shall have the power to comply with all legal requirements as to the execution of deeds and all other writings, documents or formalities, without the order of any court. 

NOTE: A further power which might well be given to the executor is to renew notes and extend indebtedness of the testator. 

I desire that my said executor shall serve in such capacity without bond. 

NOTE: In some cases it may be advisable that an individual act as co-executor or co-trustee with a trust company.

In Witness Whereof, I hereunto set my hand at Cleveland, Ohio, this------day of--- 19____. 

The foregoing instrument, consisting of------sheets, was at the date thereof signed, published and declared by the said Edward A. Jones as and for his last will and testament in the presence of us, and we at his request and in his presence and in the presence of each other have hereunto subscribed our names as attesting witnesses, at Cleveland, Ohio, this_-_-_-_day of---- 

_residing at_ residing at.. --residing at---- NOTE: The testator and witnesses should identify each sheet by signature or initials, to prevent substitution.

It is advisable that the attorney who prepares the will act as one of the witnesses, in order that he may testify if there should be a contest of the will. It has been held in Ohio that if the testator procures the attorney to act as a witness, the statute preventing an attorney from testifying does not apply. (The above form was used by J. C. Royon, Vice President of The Union Trust Company, Cleveland, Ohio, in discussing the subject of trust wills before a session of the Cleveland Bar Association at the Hotel 

Allerton on December 20, 1928.)

WILL CREATING ANNUITY FOR CRIPPLED CHILD, AND TRUST FOR WIFE 

I, of____. being of sound mind and memory, do make, publish and declare this my Last Will and Testament, hereby revoking any and all Wills by me heretofore made. 

ITEM I. I direct that all my just debts and funeral expenses be paid as soon after my decease as may be found convenient. 

ITEM II. I give and bequeath to my beloved wife, to be her absolute property, all of my personal effects such as jewelry, household goods and all other articles of domestic and personal use including any automobile or automobiles which I may own or have the right to dispose of at the time of my decease.

ITEM III. All the rest, residue and remainder of my estate, real, personal and mixed of every kind and nature and wheresoever situate, which I may own or have the right to dispose of at the time of my decease, I give, devise and bequeath to-in trust nevertheless, for the following uses and purposes: 

1. To receive, hold and manage all of the property of the trust estate and to collect, recover and receive the rents, issues, income and profits thereof and to invest and reinvest the funds of the trust estate and other securities and property in such loans, stocks, bonds, securities and real estate as it may deem proper or suitable for the investment of trust funds without being restricted to a class of investments which a trustee is or may hereafter be permitted by law to make; to sell or for such time as it deems for the best interests of the trust estate, to retain unsold any stocks, bonds, other securities or real estate, without liability for any loss or depreciation resulting therefrom; to make alterations of, repairs upon, additions to and to erect improvements upon real estate, and to sell, join in or make partition of any real estate; to lease or otherwise dispose of the same and to make or vacate plats or subdivisions thereof and to make or join in leases of real estate upon such terms as the trustee may deem advisable, the trustee being given express power to make or join in the making of leases for a term that may extend beyond the term of the trusts [116]

hereof and after deducting the commission of the trustee and its proper and necessary expenses in connection with the administration of the trust estate to pay and apply the income and the principal thereof in the manner and upon the terms and conditions as hereinafter provided. 

2. The Trustee is directed to pay to my son, annuity in an amount which will produce a total income of---. 

during the term of his natural life an 

I direct my Trustee to set aside as soon after my decease as may be found convenient and in the best interests of my estate, but in any event not later than____ ---years after my decease, a fund which in its absolute discretion it considers ample to produce revenues that will sustain such annuity, and after said fund has been so set aside the trust estate then remaining shall be exonerated from any liability to make up any deficiency of income or impairment of principal which may arise through the operation of said annuity fund.

If in any year after said annuity fund has been set aside the income thereof shall be more than sufficient to provide said annuity, such surplus income shall be added to the principal of said fund and invested as part thereof, and if in any year the net income of said fund shall not be sufficient to provide said annuity, any deficiency shall be made up out of the principal of said fund. -1) 

Until my son, shall attain the age of twenty-one (21) years the Trustee is directed to pay to or for his benefit the whole or such proportion of said annuity as the Trustee shall consider necessary and proper in order to provide for his adequate support, comfort, maintenance and education. When he shall attain the age of twenty-one (21) years the Trustee shall thereupon pay to him the whole of said annuity in quarterly installments for the term of his natural life, and upon his death the said annuity fund then remaining shall fall into and become a part of my residuary estate. 

3. The net income derived from the trust estate after the establishment of the foregoing annuity fund shall be paid to my wife in quarterly installments or oftener per annum during the term of her natural life, and in addition thereto the Trustee is directed to pay to her such sum or sums and at such time or times from the principal of the trust estate as it may deem necessary or proper in order to provide for her suitable support, comfort and maintenance and for the education of any of her children that may be dependent upon her should her income from this and from other sources prove in the judgment of the 

Trustee inadequate for such purposes. 

4. Subject to the foregoing provisions and after the death of my wife or in event she does not survive me, the trust estate shall be treated as composed of as many equal shares as there should be lawful children of mine then surviving, counting as one such share the issue then surviving of any lawful child who predeceases me per stirpes and not per capita. All references to a child or children hereinafter in this will contained shall include the surviving issue of any lawful child of mine who shall predecease me. 

Until any such child shall attain the age of twenty-one (21) years, the Trustee is authorized and empowered to pay to or for the beenfit of such one from the income or principal of the share held for such one, such sum or sums as it shall deem necessary or proper to provide for the suitable support, education and maintenance of such child and after any such child shall attain the age of twenty-one (21) years, the trustee shall thereupon pay to such one the entire net income derived from the share held for such child in quarterly installments or oftener until such child shall attain the age of thirty (30) years, when the trustee is directed to pay and distribute to such child if and when such one shall attain the age of thirty (30) years, one-third (%) of the share held for such one, paying income upon the balance of the share retained by the trustee as hereinabove provided and if and when such child shall attain the age of forty (40) years, the trustee shall pay and distribute to such one attaining said age, the balance of the share then held for the benefit of such one and the trust estate as to such share shall thereupon cease and determine. 

5. If any such child shall die before becoming entitled to receive the principal of his or her share, then such share shall be held by my trustee for the issue of such child or some one or more of them, in such proportions and subject to such terms and conditions as such child may direct by his or her Last Will and Testament, provided, however, that if any such child shall leave a wife or husband, him or her surviving, and such spouse was alive at the time of my decease, he or she may by Last Will and Testament direct the income from the whole or any part of such share to be paid to such wife, or husband until his or her death or re-marriage whichever shall first occur. In default of the exercise of such power of testamentary disposition or insofar as the same shall be void, shall not extend or take effect, such share shall vest in and be paid and distributed to the issue of such child who shall survive him or her in equal shares per stirpes and in default of issue, such share or portion thereof then remaining shall be equally added to the shares held or intended to be held for the remaining children, their testamentary appointees or issuue as the case may be. 

6. My trustee may make any division of my estate or pay any share or interest therein either wholly or in part in the assets forming my estate at the time of my death or at the time of such division or payment, and I expressly will and declare that my trustee shall in its absolute discretion fix the value of my estate or any part thereof for the purpose of making any such division or payment and the decision of my trustee shall be final and binding upon all persons concerned. [117]

7. I give to my trustee, full power and authority to apportion gains, losses and expenses to principal or income as in their uncontrolled discretion they shall deem equitable and their decision shall be final and binding upon all persons concerned. But I declare that all stock dividends shall be treated as principal and not as income. 

8. Should any company or corporation in which I or my estate may hold shares or other interest, increase its capital, I authorize my trustee to subscribe for and take up the proportion of such increased capital to which as holder of shares or other interest in such company or corporation it may be entitled and to pay for the same out of the moneys of my estate or in the alternative to sell its rights to such allotment; and I further authorize my trustee if in its opinion it would be in the interests of my estate so to do, to subscribe for and purchase additional shares in any such company or corporation. I authorize my trustee to join in any plan for the reconstruction or reorganization of any such company or corporation or the amalgamation of any such company or corporation with any other company or corporation or for the sale of the assets of any such company or corporation or any part thereof and my trustee may in pursuance of any such plan accept any shares or securities in lieu of or in exchange for the shares or other interest held by my estate in such company or corporation. I further authorize my trustee if in its discretion it considers it in the best interests of my estate so to do, to enter into any pooling or other agreement in connection with any interest in such company or corporation and in case of sale thereof to give any options it may consider advisable. 

9. If, at the time of my death, I am liable as endorser, guarantor, surety or otherwise for any liability of any company or person or persons, I authorize and empower my trustee to renew from time to time in its discretion, the bills, notes, guarantees, or other securities or contracts evidencing such liability and for that purpose to enter into new bills, notes or contracts for and on behalf of my estate. My intention in conferring upon my trustee the powers and discretion by this clause conferred is to give it such powers and authorities as will enable it to assist in the gradual liquidation of the liabilities which I may be under in order that the companies or persons for whom I may be liable as aforesaid may not be unduly embarrassed. 

10. If from any alienation or attempted alienation of any interest in or right to receive principal and/or income under a trust of my estate by a beneficiary hereunder, or if from any cause whatsoever, the said principal and/or income, or any part thereof, shall, or but for this proviso would, at any time, become payable to or pass to or for the benefit of any person other than such beneficiary, then the interest in and the right of such beneficiary to receive such principal and/or income shall cease and determine; and thereafter the said principal and/or income, or such part thereof as shall become so forfeited by such beneficiary, shall be applied as determined by the trustees in their uncontrollable discretion to the use of the wife, husband or descendants of such beneficiary or beneficiaries, in such manner and portions as to my said trustee shall seem best; and provided further, that notwithstanding any forfeiture by a beneficiary as aforesaid, my said trustee in its uncontrollable discretion, but without any obligation so to do, may from time to time apply or direct the application of the said portion of said principal and/or income forfeited as aforesaid, or so much thereof as seems to it best, to the use of the beneficiary so forfeiting the same. 

ITEM IV. I will and declare that if any person who may be entitled or become entitled to any benefit under this my Will shall instigate or cause to be commenced any litigation in connection with any of the provisions of this my Will, the benefit to which such person would have been entitled shall forthwith cease, and I thereupon revoke the benefit given to such person by this my Will and direct that such benefit shall be added to the shares of the residuary beneficiaries of this my Will in the same proportions as they share in the residue of my estate, excepting always therefrom the said person instigating or commencing such litigation as aforesaid. 

ITEM V. I direct my executor to pay all inheritance, estates, succession and legacy taxes to which my estate or the transfer of any property hereunder may be subject and to charge such taxes as a part of the expense of administration.

ITEM VI. I do hereby declare that the foregoing provisions in favor of my wife are intended to be in lieu of any and all rights of dower, distributive share, year's allowance or other interest or right in my estate.

ITEM VII. I hereby nominate and appoint..

POWERS AND DUTIES OF TRUSTEE 

1. The Trustee hereunder shall have full power and authority to receive, hold and manage all the property of the trust estate and to collect, receive and recover all the rents, issues, income and profits thereof and to invest and reinvest the funds, securities and other property of the trust estate in such loans, stocks, bonds, securities and real estate as it may deem suitable or proper for the investment of trust funds, without being restricted to a class of investments which a trustee is or may hereafter be permitted by law to make. 

2. To sell upon such terms, times of payment and security, as the Trustee may deem best or to retain, unsold, all or any part of the stocks, bonds, securities and real estate, at any time constituting the trust estate, without liability for their depreciation in value or loss by reason of such sale or retention. [119]

3. To exercise or dispose of all rights in respect to any stocks, bonds or other securities belonging to the trust estate and to join by pledge or otherwise in any plan of reorganization or other means of protecting or dealing with such stocks, bonds or other securities as it may deem best and as it would have power to do if the absolute owner thereof. 

4. To make alterations of, repairs upon, additions to and to erect improvements upon real estate; to create or grant easements in respect to real estate and to join in or vacate plats or subdivisions thereof and to convert real estate into personalty and personalty into realty and to lease forever or a day, all or any part of the real estate at any time constituting the trust estate, even though such lease may extend beyond the term of the trusts hereof. 

5. To make advances or borrow money, to renew notes and to extend indebtedness at any time or times for such purposse as it may deem desirable or proper for the improvement, protection or preservation of the trust estate. For the payment of any such advances, with interest, the Trustee shall have a lien upon the trust estate and for such sums so borrowed, may issue its promissory notes as Trustee and secure the repayment thereof by mortgaging or pledging all or any part of the trust estate. 

6. To determine whether money or property coming into its possession shall be treated as principal or income and to charge or apportion gains, expenses and losses to principal or income according as it may deem just and equitable. 

7. To employ suitable agents and attorneys and to pay them reasonable compensation and expenses. 

The Trustee shall not be liable for any neglect, omission or wrong doing of such agents or attorneys, provided reasonable care shall have been exercised in their selection, nor, save for its own neglect or willful default, for any loss or liability it may incur by reason of any of its acts or commissions as such Trustee.

In event my wife desires to sell or dispose of the whole or any part of said premises at any time, then upon her written request the trustees are directed to sell and dispose of the same upon such terms and con- [122]

ditions as may be acceptable to my said wife. The moneys, evidence of indebtedness or other avails resulting from such sale to be held in trust, subject to sale, exchange, investment or reinvestment, upon terms and conditions as are hereinafter provided in this my last will and testament, and my trustees are directed to pay the rents, issues, income or profits thereof to my said wife thereafter in convenient installments per annum for the term of her natural life. Should my wife after the sale of said premises herein described, desire to acquire and purchase any real property in fee simple to be used by her as a residence, the trustees, upon her written request, shall purchase and acquire the same, taking title to said premises in their name as trustees, and permit my wife to use and occupy the same for the term of her natural life, subject to the same terms and conditions as are hereinabove provided for her life use and occupancy of said original premises. It is understood, however, and I hereby direct, that the price which may or shall be paid for said premises shall not in any event be in excess of the amount realized from the sale and disposition of said original premises.

Upon the death of my wife, or in event she does not survive me, the premises hereinabove described or in case of prior sale the avails thereof then remaining in the hands of my trustees shall be added to and become a part of my residuary estate, and be divided and distributed to the persons and at times as provided in the next item of this my last will and testament. 

ITEM V. I give, devise and bequeath all the rest, residue and remainder of my estate wherever situate, which I may own or have the right to dispose of at the time of my decease to my son, my daughter, --, and my brother-in-law, .following terms and conditions: in trust nevertheless, subject to the 

1. To pay to my wife, in convenient installments per annum during the term of her natural life the net income derived from the trust estate, and upon the unanimous written consent of my trustees to pay to her in addition thereto out of the principal of said trust estate, such sum or sums, and at such time or times, as they shall deem necesasry to provide for her suitable comfort, support and maintenance. 

2. Subject to the provisions hereinabove made for my wife and after her decease, or in event she does not survive me, I direct my trustees to hold the trust estate then remaining for my lawful children or child, if any, who being sons or a son have attained or shall attain the age of twenty-one (21) years, or being daughters or a daughter shall have attained or shall attain that age, or previously marry, and if more than one, in equal shares; provided, however, that in event any lawful child of mine shall have died before distribution to such one shall have been completed, the share of such one or the portion thereof remaining shall vest in and be paid and distributed to his or her then surviving issue, and in default of issue the share of such one shall be equally added to the shares held for my other lawful children, child or issue, as the case may be. 

3. I direct my trustees during the minority, or minority or spinsterhood as the case may be of any lawful child of mine, or issue of a deceased child, to use and apply the whole or any part of the income or principal of the share of such one as they shall deem necessary or proper to provide for his or her suitable support, comfort, maintenance and education, or to pay the same, or any part thereof, to such one's parent or guardian for the purposes aforesaid without being required to see to the application thereof, the voucher or receipt of the parent or guardian being sufficient evidence of payment for the purposes hereof. 

4. Should my wife survive all of our children and all surviving issue thereof, then upon her decease one-half (2) of the trust estate then remaining shall be paid and distributed by my trustees to such persons, in such proportions, and subject to such conditions as she may appoint by her last will and testament, and should she fail to exercise such power of testamentary appointment, then to her next of kin, under the laws and statutes of the State of---in force and effect at the time of her decease, the other half of the trust estate then remaining shall be paid and distributed to my brother-in-law, and my sister, share and share alike, or to their issue per stirpes, and in default of issue, to the survivor of them. 

5. My trustees are authorized and empowered to make any distribution required hereunder in kind, that is to say in real estate, stocks, bonds, mortgages or other securities belonging to the trust estate according to their absolute discretion, and at such valuations as they may establish therefor. 

6. My trustees are authorized and empowered to determine whether money or property coming into their possession shall be treated as principal or income, and to charge or apportion expenses and losses to principal or income as it may deem just and equitable, and to bind the beneficiaries herein by their judgments.

ITEM VI. I direct that all interest, dividends and other income accrued and accruing and not collected from my property passing to the trustees at the time the trusts are established shall be treated as income when collected by it. [ 123 ]

ITEM VII. I do hereby declare that the foregoing provisions in favor of my wife are intended to be in lieu of any and all rights of dower, distributive share, year's allowance, or other right or interest in my estate.
