Living Trust Agreeements REVOCABLE LIVING TRUST 

This Agreement of Trust made this.

._of___ --day of___. A. D. 193----by and between (hereinafter sometimes referred to as the Donor) and the---- (hereinafter sometimes referred to as trustee) WITNESSETH: 

That, whereas, the Donor desires to assign, transfer and deliver to the trustee certain property and securities hereinafter described for the purpose of creating a voluntary living trust, and Whereas, the trustee has agreed to accept said property and securities and to hold the same and the proceeds and income thereof upon the trusts hereby created, Now, therefore, in consideration of the mutual covenants herein contained and the sum of One Dollar ($1.00) by the trustee to the Donor in hand paid, receipt whereof is hereby acknowledged, the Donor has granted, conveyed, assigned, set over and delivered and by these presents does grant, convey, assign, set over and deliver unto the said trustee, its successors and assigns, all his right, title and interest in and to the property and securities, described in Schedule "A," which initialed by the Donor, is attached hereto and made a part hereof, together with the appurtenances and all the estates and rights of the Donor thereto. 

To Have and to Hold all and singular the above granted and described property and securities, together with any property and securities which may be added to and included by the Donor, in Schedule "A" or any other schedules attached hereto and made a part hereof, unto the said Trustee, its successors and assigns, in trust nevertheless, for the following purposes and uses, and subject to the terms, conditions, powers and agreements hereinafter set forth, namely:

First. To receive, hold and manage all the property conveyed by this indenture, together with any property by the Donor hereinafter made a part of this trust; to sell, transfer, or lease forever or a day, and to exchange all or any part of said property as though the absolute owner thereof and to collect, receive and recover the rent, issues, interest, income and profits thereof, and after deducting the commission of the Trustee as hereinafter provided, and the proper and necessary expenses in connection with the administration of this trust, to pay and apply the principal and the income thereof in the manner and upon the terms and conditions as may or shall be hereinafter provided under this agreement. 

Second. To invest and re-invest money, property and securities belonging to the trust estate, subject to any limitations or conditions hereinafter specifically imposed, in such loans, stocks, bonds, securities and real estate as it may deem proper or suitable for the investment of trust funds, without being restricted to a class of investments which a trustee is or may hereafter be permitted by law to make. 

Third. To retain by way of investment, any real or personal property at any time belonging to the trust estate, without liability for its depreciation in value and in accepting title to real estate the trustee shall not be held to assume the payment of any encumbrances thereon nor any responsibility for the validity of the title conveyed to or held by it and all conveyances executed and delivered by it shall be without covenants of warranty except as against its own acts. 

Fourth. To compound, compromise and adjust any and all claims in favor of or against the trust estate, on such terms and conditions as it may deem just and equitable and to enter into agreements from time to time as it may deem expedient in reference to the reorganization of any corporation the stocks, bonds or other obligations of which shall form a part of the trust estate. 

Fifth. To renew notes, make advances or borrow money upon such terms and conditions at any time or times for such purpose or purposes as it may deem necessary or proper for the improvement, protection or preservation of the trust estate, and as security therefore to pledge, mortgage or encumber all or any part of said estate.

Sixth. The trustee shall have a first and prior lien upon the trust estate, both as to principal and income, as security for the prompt payment of charges of the trustee as, herein provided, including any advances or loans made by it with interest at prevailing rates, together with any and all expenses incurred or paid by it in connection with this trust; and to secure the payment and discharge as and when due, the principal and interest respectively of any lien, encumbrance or other charges against the trust property, and further, as security for any and all losses or expenses, including attorney fees and liabilities of every kind and nature which it may at any time expend, incur or suffer by reason of its ownership of the trust property, by reason of the terms of this trust, or by reason of any of its acts or commissions performed by it in good faith as such trustee, and this provision shall remain in full force and effect, notwithstanding this agreement be otherwise terminated. 

Seventh. To employ suitable counsel and agents in the discharge of its duties and to determine and pay them reasonable compenastion and expenses. However, the trustee shall not be liable for any negligence [ 125 ]

or omission or wrongdoing of such counsel or agents provided reasonable care shall have been exercised in their selection, nor save for its own negligent or willful default for any loss or damage. 

Eighth. The trustee shall not be required to cause stocks or registered bonds belonging to the trust to be registered in its name as trustee hereunder, but it may cause such stocks or registered bonds to be registered in its own name without describing the trust or in the name of its nominee, but such registration shall neither increase nor decrease the liability of the trustee. 

Ninth. To determine whether or not money or property coming into its possession shall be treated as principal or income and to charge or apportion expenses or losses to principal or income in such manner as it may deem just and equitable. Tenth. To furnish to the Donor during his lifetime and each of the beneficiaries after his death, whenever requested, a statement of the receipts and disbursements of the trust estate and at the end of each year to furnish to the Donor and after his death, his beneficiaries, a full and complete schedule of all securities and other property comprising the trust estate. 

Eleventh. The trustee is authorized at its discretion to purchase as an investment for the trust estate, regardless of its character, and even though not of a type herein approved for the investment of trust funds, any property real, personal or mixed belonging to Donor's general estate and to continue to hold the same as suitable for investment hereunder without liability for its depreciation in value. 

Twelfth. The trustee shall receive compensation for its services, fees as follows: (a) A fee of 3% of the gross income derived from the trust estate, during the lifetime of the Donor. (b) A fee of 5% of the gross income derived from the trust estate, after the death of the Donor and until final distribution of the trust estate. (c) In event of the revocation of this trust agreement, in whole or in part, during the lifetime of the Donor, the trustee shall receive a fee of 1/10th of 1% of the then value of the trust estate withdrawn. (d) The trustee shall be entitled to a fee upon any other termination of the trust, in whole or in part, of 1% of the reasonable value of the principal so distributed. 

Thirteenth. The trustee may resign from the trusts hereby created at any time upon giving thirty (30) days written notice of its intention to resign, duly signed and acknowledged by one of its officers and delivered personally or by registered mail to the Donor herein, or to the beneficiaries, if the Donor is deceased, and upon payment and delivery to the Donor, or any successor Trustee, as hereinafter provided, if the Donor is deceased, all of the property and assets of the trust estate, and after full settlement of its accounts the trusts herein created shall terminate without any application to or further action by any court. 

Fourteenth. In event the trustee hereunder shall resign, become disqualified, or cease to act as such trustee, the Donor, during his lifetime, and after his death a majority of the adult beneficiaries named hereunder, are hereby authorized and empowered by instrument in writing, duly executed and acknowledged, to select as successor to the trustee, any bank or trust company duly incorporated under the laws of the 

State of. authorized to perform the duties of such trustee and having its principal place of business in the city of----------and in such event the bank or trust company so selected is hereby appointed trustee hereunder, with all powers and duties conferred upon the said trustee. 

Fifteenth. Any corporation having trust powers, whether a State or National institution, into which the trustee may be converted or merged or with which it may be consolidated or to which it may sell or transfer its business or assets, as a whole or substantially as a whole, or any corporation resulting from such merger, conversion, consolidation, sale or transfer to which the Trustee shall be a party, shall without any affirmative act, stipulation or agreement of the parties hereto, become the successor of the Trustee hereunder and shall succeed to and be vested with all of the title of the trustee in and to the whole of the trust estate, and shall be vested with all of the powers, discretions, immunities and privileges, and shall be charged with all of the duties and obligations of the trustee, with like effect as if such merged or resulting or substituted corporation were herein named as original trustee. 

RIGHTS RESERVED BY DONOR IT IS MUTUALLY AGREED BY AND BETWEEN THE PARTIES: 

First. That the entire net income, after deducting commissions of the trustee as herein provided and the proper and necessary expenses in connection with the administration of the trust, shall be paid in quarterly installments or oftener to the donor during his lifetime, and the trustee shall pay and deliver to him in addition thereto from time to time such further amounts from the principal of the trust estate as said donor shall deem proper and necessary for his maintenance, support, comfort and enjoyment. 

Second. That for and during the life of the Donor the Trustee shall not lease, sell, transfer, invest, re-invest, mortgage, or encumber the funds or property heretofore or hereafter conveyed to it by virtue of this deed of conveyance and trust without the written consent of the donor; provided, however, that as to [126]

any specific property or for any specified period of time as the Donor may from time to time elect, he may in writing waive the requirement of such written consent and authorize the trustee to exercise full power as to such matters without any written direction or consent from the Donor. Nevertheless, it shall not be required of any firm, person or corporation dealing with the trustee to ascertain whether such consent shall have been given, and every such firm, person or corporation shall be protected in relying solely upon the deed, transfer or assurance of the trustee.

Third. That the donor reserves the right to be exercised by himself, at any time to alter, amend or revoke in whole or in part the trusts hereby created and all schedules hereto attached with full power to direct the trustee as to the disposition of the then trust fund. 

Fourth. That the donor may from time to time, sell, assign, transfer or deed over to the trustee other and additional property which shall be held and discharged in the same manner as the property now impressed upon said trust and upon the delivery of such additional property or evidence thereof to the trustee and subject to its acceptance, a supplemental schedule describing such property shall be prepared, which, when identified by the signature of the donor and the trustee herein, shall be attached to and be made a part of this trust indenture. 

Fifth. For and during the lifetime of the donor, or until the donor shall otherwise direct, the powers herein enumerated as powers of the trustee, shall be, at the election of the donor, performed and exercised by the donor as the agent of the trustee and as such agent the donor shall be authorized to perform and exercise the same in the donor's own name, but shall hold the trustee harmless from all personal or individual liability of the trustee for any act done by the donor as such agent, any such liability to be payable out of the principal of the trust estate. 

DISPOSITION OF TRUST ESTATE AFTER DECEASE OF DONOR 

-19 The trustee is authorized and empowered, upon the death of the donor, to pay the net income derived from the trust estate to his wife, during the term of her natural life and in addition to the payments of income above directed to be made to her, the trustee, in the exercise of its discretion is authorized and empowered to pay to her from the principal of the trust estate, such sum or sums at any time or times as it shall deem necessary or proper to provide for her suitable support, comfort and maintenance or for the education of any of donor's children that may be dependent upon her, should her income from this or from other sources, prove in the judgment of the trustee, inadequate for such purposes. 

Subject to the provision heretofore made for donor's wife and after her decease, or in event she shall not survive him, the trust estate shall be treated as composed of as many equal shares as there shall be children of donor him surviving, including in such division the surviving issue per stirpes of any child who may predecease him, and one such share shall be held for each of his children or the issue of a deceased child on terms and conditions as follows:

Until any beneficiary herein named shall attain the age of twenty-one (21) years, the Trustee is authorized and empowered to pay to or for the benefit of such one from the income or principal of the share held for such one, such sum or sums as it shall deem necessary or proper to provide for the suitable support, education and maintenance of such beneficiary, and after any such beneficiary shall attain said age of twenty-one (21) years, the Trustee shall thereupon pay to such one the entire net income derived from his or her share in quarterly installments or oftener during the lifetime of such beneficiary, except as otherwise hereinafter provided.

The Trustee is directed to distribute to any male beneficiary if and when he shall attain the age of thirty (30) years one-third (%) of the share held for such one, paying income upon the balance of the share retained by the Trustee as hereinabove provided, and if and when any male beneficiary shall attain the age of thirty-five (35) years the Trustee shall distribute to such one attaining said age one-half (%) of the share then held for such one, paying income upon the balance of his share retained by the Trustee as hereinabove provided, and if and when he shall attain the age of forty (40) years the Trustee shall distribute to such one attaining said age the balance of his share, and the trust estate as to such share shall thereupon cease and determine.

In addition to the payments of income hereinabove directed to be made to any of his female beneficiaries herein named, the Trustee is authorized and empowered to distribute to such one from the principal of her share such sum or sums as it shall deem necessary or proper to provide for her suitable support, comfort and maintenance and for the education of her children, if any, provided her income from this and from other sources proves inadequate for such purposes. 

In event of the decease of any beneficiary before distribution to such one shall be completed, his or her share or the portion thereof then remaining in the hands of the Trustee shall be held for the issue of such beneficiary or some one or more of them in such proportions and subject to such terms and conditions as such beneficiary may direct by his or her last will and testament, provided, however, that if any such beneficiary shall leave spouse him or her surviving, such beneficiary may by last will and testament direct the net income from the whole or part of such share to be paid to such spouse if alive at donor's 

---

death until his or her death or re-marriage, whichever shall first occur. In default of the exercise of such power of testamentary disposition or in so far as the same shall be void, shall not extend or take effect, such share or portion thereof then remaining in the hands of the Trustee shall vest in and be paid and distributed to the issue of such beneficiary, or in default of issue, such share or portion thereof then remaining in the hands of the Trustee shall be equally added to the shares held or intended to be held for donor's surviving children, their testamentary appointees or issue as the case may be, and in event there be no such children, testamentary appointees or issue then surviving, the trust estate shall vest in and be paid and distributed to donor's next of kin under the laws and statutes of the State of.. and distribution in force and effect at the time of his decease. relating to descent

Any division or distribution of the trust estate required under the provisions of this agreement may be made by the trustee in kind-that is to say, in real estate, stocks, bonds, mortgages or other securities belonging to the trust estate but any securities or other property so distributed in this manner shall be taken at their fair market value or if same have not a market value, then at such value as the trustee in its absolute discretion shall consider proper.

Any inheritance or succession tax that may be levied or assessed by any state or federal government against the distributable interest of any beneficiary named hereunder shall be paid by the trustee out of the principal of the trust estate. All other taxes shall be paid out of income and if none, or if an insufficient amount thereof from the principal of the share of the trust estate from whence such tax or taxes arise. 

Notwithstanding anything hereinbefore contained, the Donor directs that the income which by the terms hereof is or shall be payable to any person shall only be so payable until such person shall by specific or general assignment, transfer, or otherwise alienate or dispose of or attempt to alienate or dispose of his or her right to such income or any part thereof or interest therein or until by reason of or in consequence of or under any attachment, sequestration, execution, writ, judgment, order or other proceeding, or by any other means whatsoever, the said income or any part thereof or interest therein can no longer be personally enjoyed by him or her, or would but for the terms of this agreement belong to or become vested in or subject to the control of some other person, persons, or corporation, courts or officials, and from and after the happening of any such event the right of such person to receive and be paid the said income shall cease, but the trustee, in its absolute and uncontrolled discretion may pay over to or expend for the benefit of his wife and children, or her husband and children, as the case may be, the whole or any part of such income as it may think fit during its pleasure; the balance (if any) of such income to be paid or applied by the trustee for the benefit of such child, grandchild or grandchildren of the Donor as the trustee, in its absolute and uncontrolled discretion sees fit; provided always that if and as soon as (but not before) the reason and cause whereby the said person can no longer personally enjoy the said income as above mentioned, shall come to or be at an end, then in such case such income shall again be paid over to him or her as before the happening of any of the events above mentioned, until the again happening of any such event, and so often as the same shall happen the right of the said person to be paid such income shall cease and the trustee shall again deal with such income in its absolute and uncontrolled discretion and otherwise as above provided for until the reason or cause whereby such person can no longer personally enjoy the said income as above mentioned, has again come to or is at an end as above mentioned and then shall again pay over to him or her the said income as before, and so on from time to time as the case may be when and so often as any such event may happen and the reason or cause as aforesaid has come to or is at an end as above mentioned. 

The trustee by joining in the execution of this instrument signifies its acceptance of the trust and acknowledges that it has received this day from the donor all and singular the securities and property described in Schedule "A" attached hereto and made a part hereof, and agrees that it will hold the same and use and dispose of the proceeds thereof upon and subject to all and singular the trusts, terms and provisions set forth in this indenture. 

It is mutually agreed that these presents shall extend to and be obligatory upon the executors, administrators, legal representatives and successors and assigns, respectively of the parties hereto. 

This instrument is made in duplicate, each of which is an original, but both together shall be deemed one and the same instrument.

REVOCABLE TRUST AGREEMENT BY MARRIED WOMAN 

611193, is to evidence: That I, have this day sold, assigned, transferred, conveyed, delivered and set of____ as Trustee, the property described in "Schedule part hereof, to be held, managed and controlled and for the uses and purposes hereinafter set THIS INSTRUMENT, of the--------day of----of_. over unto the__ A," which initialed by me is hereto attached and made by the___ as Trustee, upon the trusts forth, to-wit: 

THE TRUSTEE SHALL HAVE POWER: 

(1) To sell, lease, transfer or exchange all or any part of said property and all property that may hereafter from time to time be substituted therefor or added thereto, at such prices and upon such terms and conditions and in such manner as it may deem best, including the right to lease for any terms irrespective of the period of the trust; to execute and deliver any proxies, powers of attorney or agreements that the Trustee may deem necessary or advisable in administering this trust; to invest and re-invest money coming into its possession in such loans, stocks, securities or real estate as it may deem proper and suitable, irrespective of any statutes or rules or practices of Chancery Courts now or hereafter in force limiting the investments of trust companies or trustees generally, with power to vary or transpose investments so made into others of like or similar nature, it being my intent that the Trustee shall have unrestricted power to manage all property held by it hereunder as if the absolute owner thereof, subject, however, to the provisions hereinafter made. (2) To retain by way of investment any property or securities transferred to it, without liability for depreciation; and in accepting title to real estate, the Trustee shall not be held to have assumed the payment of any incumbrances thereon, nor any responsibilities as to the validity of the title conveyed to or held by it. All conveyances executed and delivered by it shall be without covenants of warranty except as against its own acts. [129]

(3) To determine whether money or property coming into its possession shall be treated as principal or income, and to charge or apportion gains, expenses and losses to principal or income, according as it may deem just and equitable; to employ suitable agents and attorneys, and to pay their reasonable compensation and expenses. The Trustee shall not be liable for any neglect, omission or wrong doing of such agents or attorneys, provided reasonable care shall have been exercised in their selection; nor, save for its own neglect or wilful default, for any loss or damage; and in event it shall acquire title to any real estate, shall be entitled to reimburse itself from the property held by it for any loss or liability it may incur by reason of the use, occupation or condition of said real estate. (4) To make advances or borrow money upon such terms and conditions, at any time or times during my life, for such purpose as to it may seem desirable or proper, upon obtaining my written consent thereto, and after my death, at any time or times for the improvement, protection or preservation of the trust estate. For the repayment of such advances, with interest, the Trustee shall have a lien upon the trust estate, and for sums so borrowed may issue its promissory notes as Trustee, and secure the repayment thereof by mortgaging or pledging any part or all of the trust estate. (5) To compromise, compound and adjust claims in favor of or against the trust estate, upon such terms and conditions as it may deem best, provided that during my lifetime it shall secure my written approval thereto. (6) To purchase, in event of revocation, any notes and mortgages representing investments made by it, for amount of principal, and accrued interest to date of purchase. (7) To determine by a majority vote of its Board of Directors whose decision shall be conclusive, as to the competency of the donor whenever the question of her competency to act hereunder shall become material. (8) To allow and pay interest on uninvested funds at the rate and in accordance with the rules then governing deposits in its Savings Department, and to render to me a quarterly statement of its receipts and disbursements, and also, upon my request, a statement of assets. 

The entire net income derived from the trust estate shall be paid, in quarterly installments or oftener, to me during life, and the Trustee shall pay and deliver to me, in addition thereto, from time to time, such further amounts from the principal of the trust as it may deem proper or necessary for my maintenance, support, comfort and enjoyment, absolute discretion being vested in the Trustee to determine what may be necessary or proper for such purposes.

The Trustee is further authorized to make, or to procure to be made, advances to me pending accumulation of income or disposition of any part of principal, and for any such advances shall have a lien upon the trust estate.

During my life the Trustee, whenever practicable, shall secure my written approval to all sales or purchases of securities which it may propose to make. No person, firm or corporation shall be required to ascertain whether or not such approval shall have been given, but every such one shall be protected in relying solely upon the deed, transfer or assurance of the Trustee, and all sales, purchases, investments or re-investments made with such approval shall be deemed to be proper sales, purchases, investments or re-investments, as the case may be, and no claim shall be asserted against the Trustee should loss result therefrom.

Unless requested so to do in writing by me, the Trustee shall not return for taxation any personal property held by it in this trust during my life, all obligations with respect thereto being assumed by me, and for any liability incurred by the Trustee for any reason whatsoever in connection with the subject matter of this paragraph, it shall be entitled to reimburse itself from the trust estate. 

The Trustee shall allow me the free use and enjoyment of all real estate conveyed to it hereunder during my life, and during such time shall not be required to see to the payment of taxes or maintenance of insurance thereon unless requested so to do in writing. 

I reserve the right at any time during my life or so long as I am competent to act in the matter, to revoke the settlement hereby evidenced, either in whole or in part, as well as the right to modify in any respect the terms of this settlement, any such revocation or modification to be evidenced by written instrument signed by me and delivered to the Trustee. To whatever extent this settlement shall be so revoked, the Trustee shall thereupon transfer and deliver to me such part or all of the property comprising the trust estate as may have been withdrawn under such revocation, conditioned, however, upon my repaying any advances made by the Trustee and satisfactorily indemnifying it against any liabilities incurred by it in the execution of this trust. [130]

I direct that any and all property acquired by theunder any will executed by me shall be held, managed, controlled and disposed of by it as Trustee, under the powers and for all the purposes set forth in this trust, unless and in so far as the terms of the will otherwise specifically provide. 

The execution of this instrument by the.--- __shall constitute its agreement to administer all property so received by it in trust for such purposes. 

I reserve the right to add other properties to the trust estate, by conveyance, transfer or delivery to the Trustee, but only with its consent, to be treated as part of the trust estate as herein provided. -" Subject to the rights hereinbefore reserved to me during my lifetime, and after my death the Trustee is directed to pay the entire net income derived from the trust estate, in quarterly installments or oftener, to my husband, during his life. In addition to the payments of income hereinabove directed to be made to my husband, the Trustee is authorized and empowered to pay to my husband from the principal, at any time or times, such sum or sums as it shall deem necessary or proper to provide for his suitable support, comfort and maintenance, should his income from this and other sources prove, in the judgment of the Trustee, insufficient for such purposes. 

Subject to the provisions hereinbefore made, and after the decease of the survivor of myself and my said husband, the trust estate shall be treated as composed of four (4) equal shares. 

One such share shall be distributed to my daughter, One such share shall be distributed to my son, One such share shall be distributed to my daughter, One such share shall be distributed to my son, now residing at now residing in now residing in_-_now residing at--- In event of the decease of any of my said children before distribution to such one shall have been completed, the share of such one shall be distributed to the nominees and appointees by last will and testament of such child; and in default of the exercise of such power of testamentary appointment, shall be distributed to the lineal descendants, per stirpes, of such child; and failing any such lineal descendant, shall be distributed to the person or persons who shall then constitute the next of kin of such child, under the laws and statutes of the State of…. relating to descent and distribution at such time in force and effect.

Any division or distribution of the trust estate required under the provisions of this instrument may be made by the Trustee, in kind,-that is to say, in real estate, stocks, bonds, mortgages or other securities belonging to the trust estate, according to the absolute discretion of the Trustee, and at such valuations as the Trustee may establish therefor. 

Any succession or inheritance taxes that may be levied or assessed by reason of any gifts of principal or income hereunder shall be paid by the Trustee out of the principal of the trust estate. 

The gift of income hereinbefore made to the beneficiary hereunder shall not be alienated, disposed of, or in any manner incumbered by him while so in possession and control of the Trustee; and if he shall at any time or times alienate, charge or dispose of his said income, or any part thereof, or any interest therein, before the same shall have been delivered to him under the provision of this instrument; or if, by reason of his bankruptcy or other event happening at any time, said income otherwise intended for him shall wholly or in part cease to be enjoyed by him, as above provided, and the same, or any part thereof, or interest therein, shall, or but for this proviso would, become vested in some other person or persons, then the trust hereinbefore expressed concerning said income shall thereupon cease and determine as to him, and all income otherwise hereinbefore provided for him shall thereafter be held and distributed by the Trustee during the residue of his life according to the absolute discretion of the Trustee, but the Trustee may pay to him, or for his maintenance and support, or to his child or children, thereafter from such income, such sums, and such sums only, as it, in its absolute discretion, shall think fit and proper, retaining any unexpended sums as part of the principal of the trust estate. 

The Trustee shall receive for its services hereunder during my life such reasonable compensation as may be agreed upon, and after my deecase shall receive five per cent (5%) of the gross income received by it, and one per cent (1%) of any distributions of principal.

ART. I. Whereas, the Donor has assigned, transferred and delivered to the Trustee, the property described in the Schedule hereto attached, made a part hereof and entitled Schedule of the Property of the __Trust, Now, Therefore, in consideration of the sum of One Dollar ($1.00) by each of the parties to the other herewith given and in further consideration of the covenants herein contained, it is hereby agreed that the Trustee shall hold and administer the said property and all other property that may from time to time be added to this trust (all of which property is hereinafter sometimes referred to as the Trust Estate), in trust nevertheless for the uses and purposes and upon the terms and conditions hereinafter set forth, i. e., ART. II. Out of the income derived by the Trustee from the trust estate it shall first pay all the necessary costs and expenses of the trust including the reasonable compensation of the trustee, the balance of the income (hereinafter called net income) shall be paid to the Donor in quarterly installments or oftener during the term of his natural life and upon his death to the persons and in amounts hereinafter provided. 

The Trustee shall at least semi-annually render to the Donor, during his lifetime and after his death to the beneficiary or beneficiaries receiving income under this agreement, statements of account showing the true condition of the trust estate.

ART. III. (a) Upon the death of the Donor, the Trustee shall pay the net income derived from the trust estate then in its possession to his wife, during the remainder of her lifetime in quarterly installments or oftener per annum. In case such net income is at any time or times insufficient in the opinion of the trustee for her suitable comfort, welfare and support, or for the comfort, support and education of Donor's children, the Trustee is authorized and empowered from time to time, at its absolute discretion, to pay such sum or sums out of the principal of the trust estate as it may deem necessary or proper for such purposes. (b) Upon the death of Donor's wife or if she shall predecease him, then upon his death, the Trustee shall divide the trust estate then in its possession into---------equal shares and shall hold and administer such shares for the benefit of each of the children of Donor i. e., The Trustee shall pay the net income derived from each child's share to such child until the time hereinafter specified for the final distribution thereof, provided, however, that if any child shall be a minor, the Trustee instead is directed to expend so much of the net income derived from such child's share as in its sole discretion may be necessary for the comfort, maintenance and education of such child until such child shall attain his or her majority and any unexpended income thereof shall be accumulated and added to the principal of such share. (c) When each child of the Donor shall attain the age of---------years, the Trustee shall transfer, pay over and deliver to such child the principal of his or her share of the trust estate. (d) Upon the death of any child before he or she shall be entitled to receive all of the principal of the share allotted for such child's benefit, or if any child shall predecease either the Donor or his wife, then upon the death of the survivor of them the Trustee shall thereafter hold and administer the share allotted for the benefit of such child, to or for the benefit of his or her lawful issue then surviving, per stirpes and not per capita and is directed to expend so much of the net income derived thereupon as the trustee in its sole discretion may deem necessary or proper for their comfort, maintenance and education, until they shall attain their majority respectively, at which time the Trustee shall transfer, pay over and deliver to each such beneficiary the share or portion thereof so allotted for his or her benefit and in default of issue such share shall be divided equally between the shares allotted for the living children of the Donor and the lawful issue of deceased children and be held and/or distributed in the same manner as is provided for such shares.

ART. IV. In case the net income from the trust estate allotted for the benefit of any beneficiary hereunder is at any time or times insufficient in the opinion of the Trustee for the comfort, maintenance and/or education of such beneficiary, the Trustee is directed to pay out from to time such sum or sums from the principal of such share as in its sole discretion may be necessary for such purposes. 

ART. V. (a) The Trustee shall invest and reinvest the funds of the trust estate in any bonds or other interest bearing obligations of the United States of America or in any of its political subdivisions as follows: 

In any territory, state or county, or any city having a population at the last federal census of 100,000 or more inhabitants, provided that any such political subdivision has not defaulted in the payment of interest or principal on any of its bonds or other interest bearing obligations within ten (10) years prior to the time of making such investment. [132]

In any bonds or other obligations issued by any bank organized under the provisions of the Act of 

Congress, known as the Federal Farm Loan Act, approved July 17th, 1916, and amendments thereto.

In any notes or bonds, and/or in certificates of interest or participation, or in promises to pay issued by a reputable bank or trust company, provided the same be secured by a first mortgage or mortgages on improved real estate held in fee of at least double its appraised value. 

Upon the written request of Donor's wife, and if she be not living, upon the written request of a majority of the adult beneficiaries herein named, the trustee may at its discretion, but is not required to, invest and reinvest the funds of the trust estate in the fee simple title to improved real estate located within a radius of one hundred (100) miles of the City of--. --and/or in the stocks, bonds and other obligations of any reputable corporation listed on the New York Stock or Curb Exchanges, or on any Exchange located in a city within the United States of America having a population at the last federal census of 500,000 or more inhabitants. (b) The Trustee shall have full power and authority to enter into agreements from time to time, as it may deem necessary or expedient, with reference to the reorganization of any corporation, the stocks, bonds, or other obligations of which shall form a part of the trust estate. The Trustee is further authorized and empowered to manage, care for, improve, protect, control, deal with, mortgage, pledge, sell or otherwise dispose of the trust estate, or any part thereof, in its discretion, in any and every way in which any owner could manage, care for, improve, protect, control, deal with, mortgage, pledge, sell or otherwise dispose of the same. (c) The Trustee may determine the mode in which expenses are to be borne as between principal and income and may determine which moneys or property are to be treated as principal or income, which right shall include the power to determine whether any part of the actual income of any investment purchased at a premium or a discount shall be treated as capital or income. Each such determination, whether made upon a question actually raised or implied in the acts or accounts of the Trustee, shall be conclusive and binding upon all persons. (d) In dividing the Trust Estate into shares, the Trustee may allot undivided interests in any of the assets of the trust estate, to the several shares or funds, and may also make investments in common for any or all of such shares or funds insofar as the same may advantageously be done. (e) For the purpose of making any distribution of the principal of the trust estate, the Trustee is authorized and empowered to value and appraise any and all of said trust estate, and to distribute the same in kind or in cash, or partly in kind and partly in cash, as it may deem advisable; and any such valuation or appraisal made by said Trustee shall be valid and binding upon every beneficiary hereunder. (f) If any estate, inheritance or other succession taxes or duties or transfer charges are assessed in connection with any trust or gift under this agreement, they shall be paid by the Trustee out of the principal of the trust estate. (g) In case the Trustee shall be compelled at any time during the existence of this trust or after its termination to pay any tax or any penalty with respect to any succession or transfer hereby effected or directed, or on account of or because of property held under this agreement or income arising therefrom, it shall be entitled to be reimbursed therefor from the trust estate; or, if the trust estate be then insufficient or the trust be then terminated, then by the Donor and by the person or persons to whom the trust estate or the income therefrom shall have been distributed, or either or any of them, to the extent of the amount received by each distributee. (h) Each and every payment, transfer, delivery or conveyance of property to be made hereunder by the Trustee to any beneficiary in person or upon his or her personal receipt; and no such payment, transfer, delivery or conveyance shall be made upon any order of or by any such beneficiary, or upon any assignment or transfer by such beneficiary or by operation of law. (i) The Trustee is authorized and empowered upon the death of the Donor provided this agreement is still in force to purchase securities or other property from the executor or administrator of said Donor's estate and the Trustee is authorized and empowered to make loans to the executor of said Donor's estate either secured or unsecured at such rate of interest as to the trustee shall seem reasonable. The Trustee shall not be responsible or liable for any loss resulting to the trust estate by reason of any such purchase and/or loan.

ART. VI. The Donor reserves the right to revoke, alter or amend this agreement, in whole or in part, at any time and from time to time, by a memorandum in writing delivered to the Trustee (provided that the duties, powers and liabilities of the Trustee shall not be materially or substantially changed by such alteration or amendment without its consent thereto in writing) to authorize and direct the Trustee to assign, transfer, pay over and deliver to his order all or any part of the trust estate in such form as it may then exist. Such direction to the Trustee, upon the making of such assignment, transfer, payment or delivery, [133]

shall operate as a revocation of this agreement and of the trust hereby created, as to the property so assigned, transferred, paid over and delivered by the Trustee.

POWERS AND DUTIES OF TRUSTEE 

The Trustor may, from time to time, with the consent of the Trustee, add further property, real, personal or mixed, to the Trust Estate, or any part thereof, which shall thereupon be subject to all the terms of this Trust.

At any time or times, by written notice to the Trustee and upon payment of all sums due it, the Trustor may change any beneficiary; amend any provision hereof to such extent as may be acceptable to the Trustee; and/or revoke this Trust, in whole or in part, or withdraw all or any part of the Trust Estate upon indemnifying the Trustee to its satisfaction. any part

The Trustee may hold, maintain or continue any securities, properties or investments received by it hereunder, whether the same be of the character permitted by law for investment of trust funds or otherwise, or in its sole, absolute and uncontrolled discretion may grant, bargain, sell, convey, exchange, convert, lease for terms either within or beyond the duration of this Trust, assign, partition, divide, subdivide, improve, loan, re-loan, invest and re-invest the Trust Estate in securities, properties, or investments, either of the character permitted by law for the investment of trust funds or otherwise, borrow money for any Trust purpose, hypothecate the Trust Estate, or thereof, and/or replace, renew and/or extend any encumbrance thereon, upon such terms and conditions and by such means of security as may be determined upon by the Trustee, including the conveyance of any real property or the assignment, transfer and/or delivery of any personal property, to such person as it shall select, for the purpose of executing and delivering the note, mortgage, deed of trust or other instrument to evidence or secure any such debt, and of re-conveying or re-transferring such property to the Trustee subject thereto, without causing any suspension or interruption of the trusteeship hereunder, and generally in all respects manage the Trust Estate in such manner and upon such terms and conditions as to said Trustee, in its absolute and uncontrolled discretion, may seem best, and may do all of such other things and exercise and execute each and every right, power and privilege in connection with or with relation to the Trust Estate, as could be done, exercised and/or executed by an individual holding and owning said property in absolute and unconditional [134]

ownership. The Trustee is authorized to disclose the provisions of this Declaration of Trust whenever, in its discretion, such disclosure will facilitate the operation of this Trust. (Here insert paragraphs relating to distribution of Trust Estate as may best conform to desires of Trustor.)

If the Trustee deems the net income payable hereunder not sufficient to provide for the proper support, maintenance, comfort and education of any beneficiary, it may as often as it deems necessary, pay to or apply for the use and benefit of such beneficiary such additional part, up to and including the whole thereof, of the principal of the Trust Estate, or if the Trust Estate has been divided into shares or departments, of the principal of the respective share or department, all as the Trustee deems adequate in its absolute discretion. Such payment in the discretion of the Trustee may be treated as an advance on the share of the particular beneficiary.

No interest of any beneficiary in this Trust, nor any part of such interest, shall in any event be subject to sale, assignment, hypothecation, or transfer by any beneficiary, nor shall the principal of the Trust Estate hereunder, or the income arising therefrom be liable for any debt of any beneficiary, or subject to any judgment rendered against any beneficiary, or to the process of any court in aid of execution of any judgment so rendered, and all of the income and/or principal under this Trust shall be transferable, payable and deliverable only to the beneficiaries designated hereunder at the times entitled to take same under the terms of this Trust, and the personal receipts of said designated beneficiaries shall be conditions precedent to the payment or delivery of the same by said Trustee to such beneficiaries. This provision shall not restrict any authority of the Trustee to use and disburse funds for the maintenance and education of a beneficiary or to disburse funds to a guardian as herein provided. 

In the event the Trustee is required to distribute any moneys hereunder to, or use or expend such moneys for the benefit, support or maintenance of a minor, or an incompetent person, each such distribution or expenditure may, at the sole discretion of the Trustee, be made without the intervention of any guardian or any court, or the Trustee may distribute to the guardian having the legal custody of such minor or incompetent and the voucher of such guardian shall be full acquittance to the Trustee for any sums so distributed; but the Trustee may, in its discretion, require such reports and take such steps as it may deem requisite to assure and enforce the due application of such money to the purposes aforesaid. 

The Trustee may at its option, at any time, in connection with its management of the Trust Estate or the collection of any moneys due or payable to it as Trustee hereunder, compromise any claims existing in favor of or against the Trust Estate, and may loan or advance its own funds for any Trust purpose to this Trust upon the security of the entire Trust Estate, said loans to bear interest at the then current rate from date of advancement until repaid; but the Trustee shall in no event be required to make any such loan or advancement to this Trust.

If the whole or any part of the Trust Estate, or the proceeds or avails thereof, shall become liable for the payment of any estate, inheritance, income or other tax, charge or assessment which said Trustee shall be required to pay, said Trustee shall have the full power and authority, without previous notice to or demand upon any person, to pay such tax, charge or assessment out of any moneys in its hands for the account of the person whose interests hereunder are so liable. Estate and inheritance taxes so paid by the 

Trustee shall be charged to principal. All other taxes shall be charged to income. 

The Trustee shall not amortize premiums or accumulate discounts. Stock dividends and rights to subscribe to stock shall be considered as principal and all cash dividends as income, each being added to principal or income as of the date of its distribution. Unless otherwise specifically provided in this Declaration, the Trustee shall have absolute discretion in determining what is principal or income and what shall be charged or credited to either, and its judgment therein shall bind everyone beneficially interested hereunder.

Any instrument properly executed hereunder by the Trustee shall be satisfactory to it in form and substance and its terms shall be binding on all parties hereto and all beneficiaries hereunder. No person paying money to the Trustee need see to the application of the money so paid. 

Upon any division or distribution of the Trust Estate, in whole or in part, the Trustee may assign, transfer or deliver to the person, share or department, then entitled thereto, any part of the Trust Estate or an undivided interest in the Trust Estate, or any portion thereof, at such valuation as the Trustee may establish as the then fair market value, or may, within a reasonable time, convert the Trust Estate, or any portion thereof, into cash, distributing the net proceeds to such person, share or department, all in the absolute discretion of the Trustee.

The Trustee, or any successor, may resign and discharge itself of this Trust by giving written notice to the Trustor__ -----days before such resignation shall take effect, or such resignation may be accepted by the said Trustor to take effect prior to the expiration of such period. SECTION I. I nominate, constitute and appoint my son, 

_and_. as advisors to the Trustee, hereinafter described as the Advisory Board, with the powers and responsibilities and subject to the restrictions, limitations and conditions hereinafter described. Said appointments and each of them is subject to the express condition that no commissions or compensation shall be claimed by or allowed to any of them for their services as members of said Advisory Board, except as hereinafter provided, and if any of them shall decline to serve on that condition his appointment as a member of such Advisory Board shall become inoperative and void. In case persons other than those above named shall be appointed members of said Advisory Board, as hereinafter provided, the persons so appointed shall receive no commisison or compensation except such, if any, as is provided for in the instrument appointing them. 

All actions by my Advisory Board shall be by resolution in writing signed by a majority of the members who shall at the time be acting, but such resolutions may be in one or more duplicate originals. In case there shall be an even division between the members of the Advisory Board, then my son, if acting, shall have an additional vote. If there shall still be an even division, then that member of the Advisory 

Board other than my son who shall have served longest as a member of such Advisory Board shall have an additional vote, and if two or more shall have served for the same period and longer than any others, then that one of them who shall be the oldest shall have such additional vote. 

In case any member of the Advisory Board named by me shall refuse to act, resign, die, or shall for any reason be incapacitated to discharge his duties as such advisor, I direct those willing to act surviving or remaining to fill such vacancy or vacancies by nominating and appointing any person or persons, said nomination and appointment to be made by an instrument in writing executed and acknowledged in the form prescribed by law for the execution and acknowledgment of a deed to be recorded. The person or persons so nominated and appointed, upon accepting said appointment, subject to the terms and conditions in said instrument set forth by joining in the execution and acknowledgment of said instrument, shall become a member of the Advisory Board with those willing to act surviving or remaining, with the same powers and authorities as if he or they had been named by me in this instrument as a member or members of such Advisory Board. The said instrument of appointment and acceptance shall be filed with the Trustee aforesaid. And as often as a vacancy or vacancies shall in like manner be filled by the surviving or remaining advisors for the time being, but nothing herein contained shall be construed to require the filling of such vacancy or vacancies in order to enable the remaining advisors to act; but notwithstanding that the number of advisors shall be less than the number appointed by this instrument, the remaining advisors may exercise any or all of the powers of the Advisory Board granted by this instrument, or by any supplement hereto. 

The person or persons making such appointment may direct a reasonable compensation to be paid to any such new advisor. Any advisor appointed and accepting the appointment, as herein provided, shall have and possess all and singular the same powers, rights, privileges, exemptions, elections and discretions, including the power to appoint successors, and shall be subject to all and singular the same duties and obligations as are in and by this instrument conferred or imposed upon the advisors named herein. 

I authorize and empower each of the advisors, by proper power of attorney, to delegate in case of sickness, absence from the City of‒‒‒‒or for other reasons, to any other member of the Advisory Board, his powers as advisor, and to revoke the same at will. 

No member of the Advisory Board shall be answerable for losses to the trust which shall occur for any reason other than as the result of his own wilful and intentional dishonesty. 

I authorize and empower the Trustee, with the consent of the Advisory Board, to incur and pay out of the income or principal of the trust fund any and all expenses in connection with the discharge of its [137]

duties which the Advisory Board may approve. The Trustee shall keep regular books of account, and make a report to the Advisory Board once in each year and as often as the Advisory Board shall direct. 

I authorize the Trustee, with the consent of the Advisory Board, to sell, invest, re-invest, exchange. manage, control, and in any way use and deal with any and all of the property forming the subject matter of the trust, and to make investments of all or any part of the trust fund, not only in such investments as are or may be permissible for trust investments by the general rules of law or by statutory provision existing at the time, but likewise in the purchase of or loan upon the security of any securities or items of personal property whatsoever and wheresoever located. 

The Trustee, with the consent of the Advisory Board, shall have power to determine all questions, whether any moneys, securities, properties, stock dividends, rights or other things, are to be treated as capital or income, and of determining the mode in which the expenses of management and other expenses incidental to or connected with the administration of the trust shall be borne as between capital and income, or otherwise, and as between the various trust funds in this instrument provided for, which power shall include the power of determining, in case the Trustee shall make any investment in any bond or security for money at a premium, whether and to what extent and in what manner any part of the income of such bond or security shall be dealt with as capital, with the view to prevent the diminution of capital by reason of such premium.

The Trustee, with the consent of the Advisory Board, may consent to the reorganization or consolidation of any corporation or to the sale to any corporation or person of the property of any corporation `any of the stocks, bonds, notes or other securities of which are held by it hereunder, and may do any act with reference to such stocks, bonds, notes or other securities necessary or proper to enable it to obtain the benefit of any such reorganization, consolidation or sale. And in case any of the stocks, bonds, notes or other securities so held shall at any time contain an option or options to the holder thereof, or or the ownership thereof shall entitle the owner thereof to convert the same into other stocks, bonds, notes or other securities, or in case the right shall be given to the holders of such stocks, bonds, notes or other securities so held to subscribe for additional stocks, bonds, notes or other securities, the Trustee, with the consent of the Advisory Board, may exercise such option or options, make such conversions and subscriptions, and make any necessary payments therefor, and thereafter hold, manage and dispose of such stocks, bonds, notes or other securities so acquired, as a part or a whole of the trust fund; or may sell said rights or take such other action with reference thereto and may apply the proceeds as between capital and income as it, with the consent of the Advisory Board, may determine. Any advances or payments which it may see fit to make in connection with the exercise of any of the powers conferred upon it as aforesaid it is hereby authorized, with the consent of the Advisory Board, to make from the capital fund held by it as an investment of the same or from income, as it and the Advisory Board may determine. 

Where in the judgment of the Trustee it is necessary to make advances for the protection of any of the securities held by the Trustee in case of foreclosure of realty or other mortgages, or otherwise, the Trustee, with the consent of the Advisory Board, is hereby authorized to make such advances from the capital fund held by it as an investment of the same, and to pay from capital or from income any premium which may be required to be paid in the purchase of securities. 

In case any tax, assessment or other charge, ordinary or extraordinary, shall be levied or made upon the trust fund or upon the Trustee by reason of this trust, or upon any beneficiary, in connection with the fund or the income thereof, said Trustee, with the consent of the Advisory Board, may pay and discharge such tax or assessment or other charge out of the income or principal of such fund or of the share of the beneficiary against whom said tax or assessment or other charge is made, and if paid out of principal may sell so much of the securities held by it in trust as shall be necessary to provide the funds with which to make such payments.

The Trustee, or any trustee hereinafter appointed, may resign or be discharged of the trusts created by this instrument by giving notice of such intended resignation and of the date set for the same to take effect to the said Advisory Board at least thirty days before the date set for such resignation to take effect, unless the Advisory Board shall accept shorter notice. 

The Advisory Board, by unanimous vote of all its members for the time acting, may remove the Trustee above named, or any trustee hereinafter appointed, by a written notice served upon said Trustee, signed by all the members of the Advisory Board as aforesaid. 

In case of either the resignation or removal of any trustee as aforesaid, the Advisory Board shall have power from time to time by an instrument in writing, executed and acknowledged in the same manner as a deed is required by law to be acknowledged, to appoint any trust company, wherever located, having a capital and surplus of at least__. -dollars to fill such vacancy as trustee, and the compensation of said substituted trustee shall be fixed in the instrument appointing it, and any trustee so substituted, upon the acceptance by it of said appointment by an instrument in writing, executed and acknowledged as aforesaid shall be vested with the title to the trust funds on the same trusts as are prescribed with reference to the original Trustee herein named, with all its powers and discretions and subject to all its liabilities and restrictions. [138]

In case any trustee shall be removed by the Advisory Board as above provided, the Trustee from the time of the service of notice of removal upon it, as above provided, shall cease to have any powers or control over the said fund, except such as are necessary to properly care for the same pending the transfer to a new trustee, and upon receipt of notice from the Advisory Board of the appointment of a new trustee the Trustee which has been removed shall forthwith transfer and deliver to the substituted trustee all the property in its hands forming the subject matter of the trust. 

In addition to the powers herein specifically enumerated the Advisory Board may exercise any other powers necessary or desirable in carrying out the general purposes of this instrument, and may authorize and direct the Trustee to do whatever is necessary or desirable to that end. 

SECTION II. In interpreting this instrument any words of the masculine gender shall be held to include the feminine, and words of the singular number shall be held to include the plural, and words in the plural shall be held to include the singular, except where such construction is expressly, excluded by the context.

SECTION III. I direct the said Trustee to pay from the income of said fund to my son-in-law, husband of my daughter, an annuity of__ ___dollars a year, in quarterly payments, so long as he shall live; and I make this annuity a charge upon the income of said trust fund during the life of my daughter and proportionately upon the income of the trust funds into which the said fund is to be subdivided upon the death of my daughter, as hereinafter provided, but nothing herein contained shall be construed to limit any of said trust funds upon the life of the said...

SECTION IV. During the life of my daughter, the Trustee shall apply to the use of the said…-wife of the said__ so long as she shall live, the net income as aforesaid. of said trust fund after deducting the annuity payable to the said. 

SECTION V. Upon the death of my said daughter I direct the said Trustee to pay, transfer and deliver over the trust fund, as the same shall then be, subject to the charge of my son-in-law's annuity as hereinafter provided, to such of the issue of my said daughter, with the right wholly to exclude any of them, or to such charitable corporation, or to or among such charitable corporations, as she may select, for such estates, in such manner, in such proportion, upon such terms and conditions, and subject to such limitations as my said daughter, by her last will and testament, duly executed in writing, shall have directed or appointed. But any sums so disposed of by my said daughter shall be charged with their proportionate part of the annuity payable to my said sonin-law, as provided in Section III of this instrument, the amount so charged upon the sum appointed to any beneficiary being the proportion of the total annuity which the principal of such sum bears to the trust fund as originally constituted. And I earnestly hope that my daughter, in exercising the above power of appointment, will bear in mind my belief that it would be prejudicial to the welfare of her children and their descendants for them to come into possession and enjoyment of large amounts of property before and unless they have attained sufficient wisdom and character to use the same wisely, and that in order to prevent such injury to them I am more concerned lest they receive too much rather than too little; and I trust that my daughter in the exercise of her discretion will not refrain from withholding from her children and their descendants whatever amount, however large, she may think wise, and in order to enable her to do so, I have given her the power to appoint the whole or any part of said fund to charity. 

SECTION VI. If my said daughter shall fail to exercise the power of appointment given to her as aforesaid as to all or any part of said trust fund, then upon the death of my said daughter, if she shall leave her surviving any child or any issue of a deceased child, I order and direct the said Trustee to divide said trust fund, or the amount not so disposed of by my said daughter, in the manner hereinafter provided, into a series of trust funds, one of which is to be held in trust for the benefit of each such child, and one of which is to be held in trust for the benefit of each such issue of a deceased child, on the terms and subject to the limitations hereinafter provided. The fund to be held in trust for the benefit of each such child shall be an amount equal to the principal of the trust estate as then constituted, or so much thereof as shall remain after deducting such amounts, if any, as my daughter shall have disposed of by her power of appointment, divided by the num; ber of children of my said daughter who shall then be living and of the children of my said daughter who shall have died before their mother leaving lawful issue who shall then be living. 

The fund to be held in trust for the benefit of each such issue of a deceased child shall be an amount equal to the fund to be held in trust for the benefit of a child, as above provided, divided by the number of the issue then living of the deceased child of whom he is the issue per stirpes and not per capita.

Each of such sums directed to be held in trust for the benefit of any such child or issue of a deceased child of my said daughter is hereinafter called a share. The person for whose benefit a share is directed to be held in trust is hereinafter called a beneficiary, and for convenience of desig- [139]

nation only, and not as indicating the vesting of title therein, the share which is held in trust for the benefit of such beneficiary is hereinafter described as his share. Each share is to constitute a separate fund, and the trust with reference to each share is to constitute and to be held as a separate and distinct trust, but for the purposes of investment and administration the said Trustee may treat the aggregate of such shares as a single fund except as actual payments shall be made therefrom to the respective beneficiaries of parts of the principal, as is hereinafter provided for, and may hold, manage, invest and re-invest the same, all losses from, additions to, and expenses of administration of the fund to be apportioned pro rata among such shares. The said Trustee shall pay from the income of each of such shares to the said__ --its proportion of the annuity provided for in Section III of this instrument, the proportion payable from the income of each share being the proportion of the total annuity which the principal of such share bears to the original trust fund created by this instrument. The Trustee shall apply to the use of each of such beneficiaries so much and so much only of the net income of his share, after deducting its proportion of the annuity aforesaid, as the Advisory Board shall from time to time in its absolute and unrestricted discretion deem wise, and said Advisory Board may in its absolute and unrestricted discretion direct the withholding of the whole or any part of such net income during the whole or any part of the lifetime of such beneficiary, except as hereinafter provided with reference to beneficiaries who shall not have been in being at the date of this instrument. Any balance of the income of any such share over and above the amount which shall be applied to the use of such beneficiary, which is hereinafter described as surplus income, shall not be accumulated, but shall from time to time and as it is received by the said Trustee be disposed of as is provided in Section XI of this instrument.

After each such beneficiary shall have arrived at the age of thirty years, but not before, the said Trustee shall from time to time pay to each such beneficiary such portion of the principal of his share at such times and in such amounts as the Advisory Board shall direct, but the Advisory Board may in its absolute and unrestricted discretion direct the withholding of the whole or any part of the principal of any such share during the whole or any part of the lifetime of such beneficiary except as hereinafter provided with reference to beneficiaries who shall not have been in being at the date of this instrument. The several amounts which the Trustee shall pay to each beneficiary shall from the time of such payments respectively be and remain the absolute property of the beneficiary to whom they are paid, and shall thereafter be free from the terms of this trust. 

If any of said beneficiaries shall not have been in being at the date of this instrument, the share of each such last named beneficiary shall be held in trust only during the lifetime of the youngest of my descendants who shall be living at the time of the death of my said daughter and who shall have then attained the age of five years and who shall have been in being at the date of this instrument, and if such person upon whose life the trust estate in such share is limited shall die before the final distribution of the principal of such share as above provided, then upon the death of such person upon whose life such trust estate is limited such particular trust shall terminate, and the Trustee shall forthwith pay and deliver to each such beneficiary his share as then constituted, free from any trust.

In case at the death of any beneficiary any portion of the principal of his share shall then remain in the hands of the Trustee, such portion shall upon the death of such beneficiary be paid to his issue, if any, per stirpes and not per capita, and if there shall be no such issue of such beneficiary so dying, then and in that event such portion shall be disposed of as provided for in Section 

XI of this instrument.

SECTION VII. For the guidance of the Advisory Board in the exercise of its discretion as to the amounts which it is to allow to my grandchildren and their descendants out of the several trust funds created pursuant to this instrument, I declare that it is my belief that it would be prejudicial to the welfare of my grandchildren and their descendants for them to come into the possession and enjoyment of large amounts of property before and unless they have attained sufficient wisdom and character to use the same wisely; and in order to prevent such injury to them I have vested in said Advisory Board broad powers of control over said fund in the hope that they will have in mind these considerations in exercising such powers and will realize that I am more concerned lest my grandchildren and their descendants may receive too much rather than too little, and I trust that the Advisory Board will not refrain from withholding whatever amount, however large, it deems wise. 

SECTION VIII. The Trustee in making distribution of the trust fund, as is herein provided, may, with the consent of the Advisory Board, distribute the securities in which said trust fund is invested or any part of them in kind.

SECTION IX. Wherever in this instrument the Trustee is directed to distribute the principal or the income of any trust fund among the issue of any person, such distribution shall be made per stirpes and not per capita, except as otherwise expressly directed. [140]

SECTION X. Wherever in this instrument the Trustee is directed to apply the income of any fund in its hands to the use of any person, such application shall be made to his or her actual, personal and beneficial use, and if the person to whom such income is payable is a married woman such income shall be separate and apart from and free from the debts, control or interference of any husband she may at any time have; and such application shall be when and as such income, after it shall have accrued, shall be on hand for application and not by way of anticipation, and no disposition, charge or incumbrance of such income or any part thereof by way of anticipation by the person to whom such income is payable shall be of any validity, nor be in any wise regarded by said Trustee, nor shall such income or any part thereof be in any wise liable to any claim of any creditor of the person to whom such income is payable. 

Section XI. The several amounts of income and of principal which are in this instrument directed to be disposed of as provided in this Section shall be paid as the Advisory Board shall direct to---or to such other charitable corporation or corporations as the Advisory Board may select, and in such sums respectively as it may deem proper. The Advisory Board may give to each of said institutions so much of the property as it shall see fit, and it shall have the right to exclude any one or more of such institutions absolutely. It shall have the power to specify as to each institution to which the property shall be given whether it shall be used for current expenses, for buildings, for endowment, or for any other of the charitable corporate purposes of such institution.
