Whereas the Settlor is desirous of providing for the education of---brother__ by creating a trust for said purpose, Now Therefore, in consideration of the premises and the mutual covenants herein contained, the party of the First Part has granted, conveyed, assigned, set over and delivered, and by these presents does grant, convey, assign, set over and deliver unto said Trustee, its corporate successors and/or successor, the property and securities set forth in the schedule attached and marked Schedule "A" which has been signed by both of the parties hereto and made a part hereof.

To Have and To Hold all and singular the above granted and described property and securities unto said Trustee, its corporate successors and/or successor, In Trust Nevertheless for the uses and purposes and subject to the terms and conditions hereinafter set forth.

POWER AND DUTIES OF TRUSTEE 

First: To receive, hold and manage all of the property of the trust estate and to collect, receive and recover the interest, income and profits thereof; to sell, transfer or lease forever or a day and to exchange all or any part of said estate as though the absolute owner thereof and after deducting the proper and necessary expenses in connection with the administration of the trust and the commissions of the trustee, to pay and apply the principal and the income thereof in the manner and upon terms and conditions as hereinafter provided under this agreement. 

Second: To retain by way of investment any property belonging to the trust estate although it may not be of a character suitable for the investment of trust funds and the Trustee is to be relieved from any and all liability for the depreciation of any property which may arise or from which the estate may suffer by reason of such retention.

Third: To invest and re-invest the property and securities belonging to the trust estate in such loans, stocks, bonds, securities and real estate as it may deem proper or suitable for the investment of trust funds without being restricted to a class of investments which a trustee is or may hereafter be permitted by law to make.

Fourth: To vote in person or by proxy upon all the stocks or other securities held by it; to exchange the securities of any corporation or company for the securities issued by the same or by any other corporation or company at such times and upon such terms and conditions as the Trustee shall deem proper; to consent to the reorganization, consolidation or merger of any corporation or company or to the sale or lease of its property or any portion thereof, to any person, corporation or company, or to the lease by any person, corporation or company of his or its property or any portion thereof, to such corporation or company and upon such reorganization, consolidation, merger, sale or lease, to exchange the securities issued in connection therewith; to pay all assessments, subscriptions and other sums of money as the Trustee may deem expedient for the protection of its interests as holder of any stocks, bonds or other securities of any corporation or company, and to exercise any option contained in any stocks, bonds, or other securities for the conversion of the same into other securities, or take advantage of any rights to subscribe for additional stocks, bonds or other securities, and to make any and all necessary payments therefor, and generally to exercise in respect of all stocks, bonds or other investments held by the Trustee hereunder all rights, powers and privileges as are or may be lawfully exercised by any person owning similar property in his own right. 

To make advances or borrow money for the trust estate upon such terms and conditions at any time or times and for such purpose or purposes as it may deem necessary or proper for the improvement or protection of the trust estate. For the payment of such advances with interest, the Trustee shall have a lien on the trust estate and for sums so borrowed may issue its promissory notes as Trustee and secure the payment thereof by mortgage or pledge all or any part of the property belonging to the trust estate. 

To make any distribution directed hereunder in kind, that is to say, in real estate, stocks, bonds, mortgages or other securities belonging to the trust estate but any such property or securities so distributed in kind shall be taken at their fair market value or if same have not a market value, then at such value as the 

Trustee shall consider just and equitable.

DISPOSITION OF THE TRUST ESTATE 

If and when the said___ --(hereinafter called the Beneficiary) enters a preparatory school or other private school, military academy, or college of secondary education requiring the payment of tuition, the Trustee shall pay to or for his benefit during each academic year, and so long as he shall remain a student in good standing in said institution or institutions, but not exceeding four years, the sum of in nine installments spread over the months of the said academic year. 

The aforesaid provision for the secondary education of the Beneficiary shall cease and determine if he does not enter any such institution before he shall attain the age of fifteen years. [142]

If and when the Beneficiary enters a recognized college for academic training recognized by the American Association of Universities, the Trustee shall pay to or for his benefit during each academic year, not exceeding four years, the sum of $-payable in nine installments spread over the months of said academic year.

The trust herein created shall cease and determine if the Beneficiary does not qualify and enter a college recognized by the American Association of Universities at or before the age of nineteen years. (The said Beneficiary was born on the_. -----day of--- A. D. 19-----.) 

The trust herein created shall cease and determine if the Beneficiary during his college course shall be expelled from college for any cause, or shall leave the institution which he enters without first obtaining from said institution a credential indicating that he is in good standing and is leaving voluntarily and shall within a period of one month of leaving said institution enter another college of similarly recognized standing to continue his college course.

The trust herein created shall cease and determine at the end of four years from the time the Beneficiary enters a college for academic training recognized by the American Association of Universities, except as hereinafter provided.

If the Beneficiary graduates from college within four years, or at the end of four years, from the time of first entrance, he shall receive at the time of graduation all of the remaining assets of the trust fund after all expenses and fees have been deducted and paid by the Trustee. 

The Trustee shall have sole discretion in the case of illness of the Beneficiary to extend the period during which the college course shall run as provided herein, not exceeding one year, and may omit monthly payments if the Beneficiary is absent from college on account of illness, and make the payments during the extended period not exceeding one year. It is the general purpose to provide for the Beneficiary during his college course in monthly payments during the academic year, but to require that graduation take place within five years if there is illness, and four years if there is no illness. The Trustee is to take into consideration that the Settlor's affection for the father of the Beneficiary is such that he is desirous of helping the Beneficiary unless the Beneficiary is incompetent, or guilty of misconduct or becomes so incapacitated that the continuance of the college course would be futile. 

If this trust should terminate because of the death of the Beneficiary, or for any other reason, and under the terms hereof, there should be any assets left in the fund after paying expenses and fees of the Trustee, the remaining assets shall revert to the Settlor; if he should be dead, they shall be turned over to the Settlor's wife, ; if both shall be dead, they shall be turned over to the Settlor's son, ; if the three shall be dead, they shall be turned over to the Settlor's brother, If all shall be dead, they shall revert to the estate of the Settlor. 

All payments of income and principal herein provided for shall be paid by said Trustee into the hands of the Beneficiary or Beneficiaries, and only upon the receipt of the respective beneficiaries subscribed with their own proper hand and not upon any written or verbal order, nor upon any assignment or transfer by any such beneficiary, and the same shall not be grantable, transferable or otherwise assignable by anticipation, either by voluntary or involuntary act of any of such beneficiaries or by operation of law, and no person shall acquire any right, title or interest of any beneficiary in said trust estate or any part thereof by reason of any sale, assignment by anticipation, transfer, claim, debt or judgment against such beneficiary, whether voluntary or involuntary.

It is the Settlor's purpose, through this trust deed to express to the Beneficiary his desire that the Beneficiary prepare himself for entrance into the practice of law, and if circumstances are suitable, for entrance into the law firm of_____ 

of which the Settlor is now a 11111 partner. The Settlor, however, does not make this a condition of the acceptance of any of the funds to be paid under this trust by the Beneficiary. The Settlor merely admonishes the Beneficiary to prepare himself thoroughly for some life work and expresses the hope that in preparing himself to be a lawyer the Beneficiary will find himself well prepared for any business. The Settlor wishes the Beneficiary to understand for himself that he is the sole judge of what pursuit in life he will follow; that the Beneficiary is absolutely free to do as he pleases; that in expressing the hope that the Beneficiary will enter the practice of law, the 

Settlor has merely made a suggestion that may be helpful to the Beneficiary. The Settlor will not be disappointed if the Beneficiary decides to enter some other profession or business. 

All doubts arising hereunder regarding the exercise of discretion by the Trustee or in the construction of the terms of the trust or otherwise shall in each case be resolved in favor of the Beneficiary. 

The Trustee shall be entitled to receive as compensation for its services in the administration of this trust an annual commission of‒‒‒‒‒‒‒‒per cent per annum upon all income received and a like amount on all sums paid out by it, as well as all proper and necessary expenses incurred, and it is hereby authorized to deduct all such commissions before making payments to any of the beneficiaries hereunder. 

The party of the first part covenants and agrees to make, execute and deliver in due form of law such other and further assignments, conveyances or other instruments, which the Trustee may deem requisite or necessary to effectuate the purposes hereof. [143]

The Trustee by joining in the execution of this instrument signifies its acceptance of the trust herein created, and covenants and agrees with the party of the first part that it will faithfully perform the duties thereof according to the best of its skill, knowledge and ability, and the Trustee shall not be responsible for any action taken by it in the administration of the trust or for any mistake in judgment or decrease in value or loss of the trust estate, nor for any cause whatsoever except from its own bad faith.

CONCERNING THE TRUSTEE 

First. To receive, hold and manage all the property conveyed by this indenture, together with any property hereinafter made a part of this trust; to sell, transfer or lease forever or a day, and to exchange all or any part of said property as though the absolute owner thereof, and to collect, receive and recover the rent, issues, interest, income and profits thereof, and after deducting the commission of the Trustee as hereafter provided, and the proper and necessary expenses in connection with the administration of this trust, to pay and apply the principal and the income of this trust in the manner and upon the terms and conditions as may or shall be provided in Schedule "B" attached hereto and made a part hereof. 

Second. To invest and reinvest money coming into its possession, subject to any limitations or conditions hereinafter specifically imposed, in such loans, stocks, bonds, securities and real estate as it may deem proper or suitable for the investment of trust funds, without being limited or restricted to a class of investments as may be prescribed by statute or otherwise for the investment of trust funds by trust companies or [144]

trustees generally. The Trustee may purchase for investment purposes, securities, real estate and other property from its bond, mortgage or other departments, paying therefor not more than the price at which said securities, real estate or other property are offered for sale to the general public. Third. To retain by way of investment any property or securities transferred to it, without liability for their depreciation in value; and in accepting title to real estate, the Trustee shall not be held to have assumed the payment of any encumbrances thereon, nor any responsibility as to the validity of, the title conveyed to or held by it. All conveyances executed and delivered by it shall be without covenants of warranty except as against its own acts. 

Fourth. To determine whether or not money or property coming into its possession shall be treated as principal or income, and to charge or apportion expenses or losses to principal or income, according as it may deem just and equitable.

Fifth. To make advances or borrow money upon such terms and conditions, at any time or times and for such purpose as it may deem desirable or proper for the improvement, protection or preservation of the trust estate. For the repayment of such advances, with interest, the Trustee shall have a lien upon the trust estate, and for such sums so borrowed may issue its promissory notes as Trustee,. and secure the repayment thereof by mortgaging or pledging any part or all of the trust estate. 

Sixth. To compromise, compound and adjust claims in favor of or against the trust estate, upon such terms and conditions as it may deem best; and the doing of all such things for the preservation in and about the management of said trust property, as it may consider necessary or proper and for the best interest of said estate.

Seventh. To join in any reorganization, and to deposit said securities subject to the terms of any protective reorganization agreement approved by it, and to pay from the principal of the trust any moneys required to be paid according to such plan and without applying to any court for permission to do so. 

Eighth. The Trustee may make any distribution directed hereunder in kind according to its discretion, but any securities or other property so distributed shall be taken at their fair market value, or if same have not a market value, then at such value as the Trustee shall deem proper. 

Ninth. The Trustee shall keep accurate books of account in respect to the trust estate which at all reasonable times shall be open to inspection by the Settlors during their lifetime and after their death by any adult beneficiary entitled to receive income or principal hereunder, and shall render not less than four reports per annum to Settlors, or their beneficiaries, showing receipts and disbursements made in behalf of the trust estate and at least once in each year to furnish the Settlors, and after their decease all adult beneficiaries, a full and complete Schedule of property comprising the subject matter of this trust. 

Tenth. As compensation for its ordinary services hereunder, the Trustee is instructed to pay to itself out of the funds of the trust estate fees not to exceed.. --per cent (-----%) per annum upon the gross income received and paid out, provided only that if the trust created herein shall continue until the death of the Settlors, then thereafter so long as the trust estate remains it shall be entitled to receive for its services as Trustee, fees not to exceed‒‒‒ --per cent (-----%) per annum upon the gross income received and paid out; and upon distribution of the trust estate the Trustee shall be entitled to a fee of --per cent (-----%) of the principal so distributed. 

Eleventh. The Trustee may at any time resign from the trusts hereby created, and in such event the 

Settlors, if living, shall appoint a Bank or Trust Company to succeed the Trustee, or if the Settlors shall not be living, such of the beneficiaries as shall then be of age and of sound mind are authorized and directed to appoint a bank or trust company as successor, and upon being so appointed, any such bank or trust company shall be clothed with the same powers, rights, and privileges heretofore granted to the original trustee named herein.

Twelfth. In event the Trustee shall resign, become disqualified, cease to act as such Trustee or its doors be closed as a banking or financial institution for voluntary liquidation or by operation of law, the Beneficiary during her lifetime or a majority of the adult beneficiaries hereunder, after her decease, are hereby authorized and empowered, at her or their discretion, by instrument in writing duly executed and acknowledged, to select as successor Trustee any bank or trust company duly incorporated under the laws of the State of --authorized to perform the duties as such Trustee and having its principal place of business in the City of--- -----and the bank or trust company so selected is hereby appointed to receive hereunder with all powers, duties and obligations herein conferred upon the original Trustee.

First. Second. During the lifetime of the Settlors, the Trustee shall not sell, lease, invest, reinvest, mortgage or encumber the funds or property belonging to the trust estate without the written consent of the Settlors. 

Neverheless it shall not be required of any firm, person or corporation dealing with the Trustee to ascertain whether such consent shall have been given and every such firm, person or corporation shall be protected in relying solely upon the deed, transfer or assurance of the Trustee.

Notwithstanding anything hereinbefore contained, the Settlors will and direct that the income which by the terms hereof is or shall be payable to any person shall only be so payable until such person shall by specific or general assignment, transfer, or otherwise alienate or dispose of or attempt to alienate or dispose of his or her right to such income or any part thereof or interest therein or until by reason of or in consequence of or under any attachment, sequestration, execution, writ, judgment, order or other proceeding, or by any other means whatsoever, the said income or any part thereof or interest therein can no longer be personally enjoyed by him or her, or would but for the terms of this agreement belong to or become vested [147]

in or subject to the control of some other person, persons or corporation, courts or officials, and from and after the happening of any such event the right of such person to receive and be paid the said income shall cease, but the Trustee, in its absolute and uncontrolled discretion may pay over to or expend for the benefit of his wife and children, or her husband and children, as the case may be, the whole or any part of such income as it may think fit during its pleasure; the balance (if any) of such income to be paid or applied by the Trustee for the benefit of such child; grandchild or grandchildren of the Settlors as the Trustee, in its absolute and uncontrolled discretion sees fit; provided always that if and so soon as (but not before) the reason and cause whereby the said person can no longer personally enjoy the said income as above mentioned, shall come to or be at an end, then in such case such income shall again be paid over to him or her as before the happening of any of the events above mentioned, until the again happening of any such event, and so often as the same shall happen the right of the said person to be paid such income shall cease and the Trustee shall again deal with such income in its absolute and uncontrolled discretion and otherwise as above provided for until the reason or cause whereby such person can no longer personally enjoy the said income as above mentioned, has again come to or is at an end as above mentioned, and then shall again pay over to him or her the said income as before, and so on from time to time as the case may be when and so often as any such event may happen and the reason or cause as aforesaid has come to or is at an end as above mentioned.

POWERS AND DUTIES OF TRUSTEE 

First. To receive, hold and manage all of the property and securities conveyed to the trustee by the terms of this indenture, together, with any additional property or securities that may hereinafter be made a part of this trust; to sell, transfer or lease forever or a day and to exchange all or any part of said property or securities as though the absolute owner thereof; to collect, receive and recover the rent, issues, interest, income and profits thereof, and after deducting the proper and necessary expenses in connection with the administration of the trust and the commission of the Trustee, as hereinafter provided, to pay and apply the principal and the income thereof in the manner and upon the terms and conditions as hereinafter provided under this agreement.

[148]

To compound, compromise and adjust any and all claims in favor of or against the trust estate on such terms and conditions as it may deem just and equitable and to enter into agreements as it may deem expedient in reference to the reorganization of any corporate stocks, bonds or other obligations which shall form a part of the trust estate. 

Fifth. To make advances or borrow money for the trust estate upon such terms and conditions at any time or times and for such purpose or purposes as it may deem necessary or proper for the improvement, protection or preservation of the trust estate; for the repayment of such advances, with interest, the Trustee shall have a lien on the trust estate and for such sums so borrowed may issue its promissory notes as trustee and secure the payment thereof by mortgaging or pledging all or any part of the property and securities belonging to the trust estate.

Sixth. To determine whether or not money, property or securities coming into its possession by virtue of this agreement shall be treated as principal or income and to charge or apportion expenses or losses to principal or income in such manner as it may deem just and equitable. 

Seventh. The Trustee is authorized and empowered to make any distribution directed hereunder in kind, i. e., in real estate, stocks, bonds, mortgages or other securities belonging to the trust estate but any such property or securities so distributed in kind shall be taken at their fair market value or if same have not a market value then at such value as the trustee shall consider just and equitable. 

Eighth. Estate, succession and inheritance taxes so paid by the Trustee shall be charged to principal. 

All other taxes shall be charged to income.

Party of the Second Part, hereinafter called the "Trustee," Witnesseth:

Whereas, the Grantor has caused his life to be insured, and said insurance is represented by insurance policies deposited with the Trustee as set forth in schedule hereto annexed and made part hereof, which said policies are, or will hereafter be made payable to the Trustee as beneficiary. 

Now, therefore, in consideration of the mutual covenants and agreements herein contained, it is agreed by and between the parties hereto as follows: 

ARTICLE I. It is agreed that the Grantor from time to time may make additional insurance policies subject to the terms of this agreement by causing the same to be made payable to the Trustee as beneficiary. 

ARTICLE II. The Grantor directs the Trustee to disburse the income and principal of the trust estate in the manner hereinafter set forth. 

ARTICLE III. It is expressly understood and agreed that the Trustee is not obligated to pay any premiums or assessments on said policies and to be under no obligation in respect of the policies other than for the safekeeping thereof, except to the extent expressly agreed to herein. 

ARTICLE IV. The Grantor reserves to himself, during his life, all payments, dividends, surrender value privileges and benefits of any kind which may accrue on account of any of the aforesaid policies, and the right at any time to assign, pledge or use said policies, or any of them, or to change the beneficiary thereof, or to borrow money thereon, without the consent, approval or joinder of the Trustee, or any beneficiary hereunder. It is the intent that the trust shall be operative only in respect of the proceeds of such of the policies as in terms may be due and payable to the Trustee at the time of the death of the Grantor or thereafter, after deduction of all charges against the policies by way of advances, loans, premiums, or otherwise, and the receipt of the Trustee for such proceeds shall release the insurance companies from liability on the policies.

ARTICLE V. It is agreed that the Grantor may by instrument in writing, delivered to the Trustee, (a) modify or alter this agreement, in whole or in part, provided that the duties, powers, compensation and liability of the Trustee shall not be changed without its written consent; (b) revoke this agreement in whole or in part.

ARTICLE VI. The Grantor agrees and directs that upon his death, the proceeds of said policies or any thereof which may then be subject to the terms of this agreement shall be paid to the Trustee. The Trustee may institute any proceeding at law or in equity in order to enforce the payment thereof, and may do and perform any and all other acts and things which may be necessary, for the purpose of collecting any sums which may be due and payable under the terms of said policies; it being distinctly understood, however, that the Trustee shall not, except at its option, enter into or maintain any litigation to enforce the payment of said policies until it shall have been indemnified to its satisfaction against all expenses and liabilities to which it may, in its judgment, be subjected by any such action on its part. 

The Trustee is authorized to compromise and adjust claims arising out of the insurance policies or any of them, upon such terms and conditions as it may deem just, and the decision of the Trustee shall be binding and conclusive upon all persons and corporations interested therein. 

ARTICLE VII. The Trustee shall hold and manage the fund, when so collected, together with such other property as may be acquired, during the existence of this trust, all of which said property, will hereinafter be referred to as the "trust estate," upon the terms and conditions, and for the uses and purposes, and with the duties and powers that is to say: 

To hold, possess, manage and control the said trust estate and every part thereof, with full power to sell, transfer, convey and dispose of the same, upon such terms and in such manner and for such prices as to the said Trustee shall seem meet and proper. 

The Trustee is hereby given full power and authority to invest and reinvest all or any part of the said trust estate which may come into its hands, in such a manner and in such securities or other property, personal or real, and upon such terms and for such length of time, as to the Trustee shall seem meet and proper; it being intended hereby to give unto the said Trustee full and complete authority to hold, possess, manage, control, sell, convey, encumber, lease, invest and reinvest the whole and every part of the said trust estate, according to its sole judgment and discretion, without any limitation upon its powers and authority so to do, either by statute or otherwise. [ 151 ]

ARTICLE VIII. The Trustee may purchase securities or other property from the executor or the ad ministrator of the Grantor's estate, and the Trustee may or shall have power to make loans to the executor of the Grantor's estate, either secured or unsecured, at such rate of interest as the Trustee shall determine. 

The Trustee shall not be responsible or liable for any loss resulting to this trust estate by reason of any such purchase and/or loan.

ARTICLE IX. The Trustee is directed to charge all premiums on investments against principal, and to credit all discounts on investments to principal. Any and all cash dividends, whether ordinary or extraordinary, shall be considered as income and any and all stock dividends or other things of value shall be considered as corpus and added. to the capital of the trust, as far as permitted by law. The Trustee, in its discretion, may apportion between principal and income any expenditure which in its opinion should be apportioned, notwithstanding any legal rule to the contrary. 

ARTICLE X. In any case in which the Trustee is required to divide the principal of the trust estate in parts or shares, or to distribute the same, it is authorized and empowered in its sole discretion to make division or distribution in kind, or partly in kind and partly in money. The judgment of the Trustee concerning the values for the purpose of such division or distribution of the property or securities shall be binding and conclusive on all parties interested therein. 

ARTICLE XI. The Trustee shall render periodic statements of the receipts and disbursements of the income and principal to the beneficiaries of the trust estate.

T Whereas, the Donor desires to establish a trust of certain policies of insurance on his life, and of the proceeds thereof, and of certain other property and securities, Now, therefore, in consideration of the premises, the mutual covenants herein contained, and of other good and valuable consideration, and of the sum of One Dollar ($1) to him in hand paid by the Trustee at or before the time of the ensealing and delivery of these presents, the receipt whereof is hereby acknowledged, the Donor has delivered and does hereby covenant and agree forthwith to assign or cause to be made payable to the Trustee the policies of insurance so as to vest title to the policies in the Trustee, and has delivered and does hereby grant, convey, assign and set over unto the Trustee the other property and securities, listed in Schedule A hereto annexed. 

To have and to hold said policies, the proceeds thereof, and said property and securities unto said Trustee, its successors and assigns, in trust nevertheless, and subject to the terms hereof. 

First: During the lifetime of the Donor, the Trustee shall pay the premiums which may become due and payable on the policies or any of them, from the income of the trust or from cash seasonably furnished from time to time for that particular purpose by the Donor. In order to afford the Donor an opportunity to furnish cash seasonably for the payment of premiums, should the income of the trust be insufficient for that purpose, the Trustee, if in its opinion it will or may not have in hand sufficient income to pay a premium about to fall due, shall give to the Donor notice in writing to that effect. To assure the continued payment of the premiums on the policies, the Trustee is authorized, in its discretion, to retain in its hands an undistributed balance of cash income in such amount as may seem to it necessary. Should the income of the trust be insufficient or should the Donor fail to furnish cash seasonably to pay the premiums on all the policies, then the Trustee, at its sole and unrestricted option, and for the purpose of obtaining_funds with which to pay premiums on any policy, may sell at public or private sale, without notice to the Donor, or to any other person, property and securities (other than the policies) constituting the principal of the trust, may borrow upon the policies, or any of them, or as the assignee of any policy may exercise options for the automatic application of loan provisions to the payment of future premiums, it being understood that said methods of obtaining funds are permissive only and not mandatory upon the Trustee, and the Donor hereby expressly releases the Trustee from all liability for failure to avail itself of said methods, or any of them, and from the consequences of such failure. The Trustee is authorized in its sole discretion to select from time to time the policies to the payment of premiums on which moneys actually available shall be applied, in the event that premiums on all the policies may not be provided for. Unless the income of the trust, or unless cash seasonably furnished by the Donor shall be sufficient to enable the Trustee to pay such premiums, the Trustee shall be under no obligation to pay them, nor to see that payment is made by the Donor or otherwise, and the Trustee shall be under no liability to anyone in case such premiums are not paid, nor for any result of the failure to make such payments. The Trustee shall be responsible for the proceeds of the policies only when, as and if collected by or paid to it, and the Trustee shall not be liable to anyone if for any reason whatsoever the policies, or any of them, shall lapse or be otherwise uncollectible, unless such lapse or uncollectibility results from the negligence of the Trustee in failing to pay premiums out of the income of the trust actually in hand or out of cash actually in hand and seasonably furnished for that particular purpose by the Donor. 

Second: Upon the actual assignment or making payable of the policies to the Trustee, the Trustee is thereupon invested with all right, title and interest in and to the policies, and is authorized to exercise and enjoy all options, rights and privileges therein and beneficial interests thereunder, as fully and effectually as the Donor might have done. The insurance companies are hereby authorized and directed to recognize the Trustee as fully entitled to all options, rights, privileges and interests under the policies and any receipts, releases and other instruments executed by the Trustee in connection with said policies shall be binding and conclusive upon the insurance companies and upon all persons and corporations interested in the trust. 

Third: Without impairing the absolute right of the Trustee to determine the manner in which dividends distributed on the policies or any of them shall be applied or disposed of, it is agreed that the Trustee may follow the written instructions given by the Donor from time to time with reference thereto, but should it elect so to do it is hereby released from all liability in connection with such application or disposal, and neither the insurance companies nor any person or corporation interested in the trust shall be bound to ascertain whether or not such instructions have been given or are being complied with, nor shall they or any of them have any right to enforce compliance or question compliance or non-compliance. 

Fourth: The Trustee shall receive and hold the proceeds of the policies, as well as the proceeds of any other policies which may hereafter be made subject to the terms hereof, upon the uses and trusts and for the purposes herein set forth. Except at its option, the Trustee shall not enter into or maintain any litigation to enforce payment of the policies until it shall have been indemnified to its sole satisfaction against all expenses and liabilities to which it may in its sole judgment be subjected by such action on its part, but the Trustee may utilize any part of the principal of the trust to meet expenses incurred in connection with enforcing payment of any policy. The Trustee is authorized to compromise and adjust claims arising out of the insurance policies or any of them, upon such terms and conditions as it may deem just, and the decision of the Trustee shall be binding and conclusive upon all persons and corporations interested in the trust. [153]

Fifth: The Trustee shall hold, manage, invest and reinvest the proceeds which come into its hands and the securities and other property constituting the trust fund, in the manner hereinafter specified and shall collect, recover and receive the issues, interest and income thereof, hereinafter called "income," and after deducting the commissions of the Trustee as hereinafter provided, the premiums on the policies, and reservations therefor, and the proper and necessary expenses in connection with the administration of the trust, shall pay the income in quarterly installments of equal amount, or as nearly equal as possible, unto (Here insert such dispositions of income and ultimate distribution of principal as is hereby expressly may suit donor's desires.)

Sixth: Notwithstanding anything to the contrary herein contained, authorized and empowered, at any time or times within---. (----) days after the date of death of the Donor, provided the trust shall not have terminated, to withdraw from the principal of the trust fund such sum or sums as he (or she) may request by instrument in writing executed and acknowledged and delivered to the Trustee, provided, however, that the aggregate amount so withdrawn from the principal of the trust fund shall not exceed in any event the sum of. 

Seventh: The Trustee is authorized and empowered to retain, subject to the provisions hereof, any and all of the property and securities hereinbefore described in their then existing form, and also such additional property and securities as the Donor may, from time to time, add to the trust fund, without liability for decrease in the value of such property or securities. 

The Trustee is hereby given full power of sale and exchange in connection with the property and securities from time to time comprising the trust fund and is authorized and empowered, from time to time, to invest and reinvest the proceeds in such property and securities as it may deem suitable for the trust and shall not be restricted to those of the character authorized by the laws of the State of___ for trust investment.

No purchaser upon a sale by the Trustee shall be bound to see to the application of the purchase money arising therefrom or to inquire into the validity, expediency or propriety of any such sale. 

Eighth: In any case in which the Trustee is required, pursuant to the provisions of this Indenture, to divide the principal of the trust fund into parts or shares, or to distribute such parts or shares, it is authorized and empowered in its sole discretion to make such division or distribution in kind, or partly in kind and partly in money, and for the purpose of such allotment the judgment of the Trustee concerning the propriety thereof, and the relative value for the purpose of division or distribution of the property and securities so allotted, shall be binding and conclusive on all persons and corporations interested therein. 

Ninth: The Trustee may in its sole discretion cause the securities and property which may, from time to time, comprise the trust fund, or any part thereof, to be registered in its name as Trustee hereunder, or in its own name, or in the name of its nominee, or to take and keep them unregistered, and to retain them or any part thereof, in such a condition that they will pass by delivery. 

Tenth: The Trustee shall be entitled to receive as compensation for its services in the administration of a trust a commission of-------% upon income, and is hereby authorized to deduct such commission before paying the income to the beneficiary or beneficiaries hereunder. 

The Trustee also shall be entitled to a commission of--------% upon the market value of the property and securities, exclusive of insurance policies, comprising the original trust fund, payable upon the execution of this Indenture, or at the time of the delivery of additional property and securities to the Trustee; to a commission of--------% upon the proceeds of the policies payable therefrom at the time of the receipt thereof by the Trustee; and to a commission of--------% upon the market value of the trust fund when and as the trust shall terminate in whole or in part, such market value to be computed as of each respective date.

If the Donor, prior to his death, shall revoke this Indenture as to any particular policy so that the face amount thereof is no longer payable to the Trustee, the Donor shall pay forthwith to the Trustee a carrying charge of one-tenth of 1% upon the face amount of such policy. 

The Trustee is also authorized in the discharge of its duties to employ counsel and agents and to determine and pay to them reasonable compensation and it shall be entitled to reimbursement there for and for such other expenses and charges as it may be necessary and proper to incur. 

Eleventh: The Donor hereby expressly reserves to himself the privilege, from time to time, of assigning or making payable to the Trustee so that title will vest in it, further policies of insurance on his life, and other property and securities, all of which and the proceeds whereof, when paid to it, the Trustee shall receive, hold, manage, invest and reinvest in the manner herein specified. 

Twelfth: Any and all income, including dividends declared but unpaid, which has accrued on securities at the time of the delivery thereof by the Donor to the Trustee hereunder, whether such delivery be made at this time or at some future date, shall be considered by the Trustee as income, and distributed hereunder accordingly. [154]

Thirteenth: Notwithstanding anything to the contrary herein contained, the Donor may from time to time during his life, by instrument in writing executed and acknowledged in the manner required for a deed of real property situated in the State of.. and delivered to the Trustee, modify or alter in any manner, or revoke in whole or in part, this Indenture, and the trusts then existing, and the estates and interests in property hereby created, and in case of such revocation said instrument shall direct the disposition to be made of the property, securities, or policies, or of the portion thereof affected by such revocation, and the Trustee shall make, execute and deliver such instruments, if any, as may be necessary or proper in order to carry the same into effect, and no one shall have any right, interest or estate under this Indenture, except subject to the proper modification, alteration or revocation thereof, provided, however, that no such modification, alteration or revocation shall be effective to terminate this Indenture as to any particular policy or to withdraw any particular policy from the operation hereof, until, with the consent of the insurance company if need be, the assignment of the policy or change of beneficiary so as to remove title from the trust has been completed so that the Trustee is no longer entitled to be paid the proceeds. 

Fourteenth: The Donor covenants and agrees to make, execute and deliver in due form of law such other and further instruments as the Trustee may deem requisite or necessary to effectuate the purposes hereof. trust.

Such conversion on the part of the Trustee shall be purely discretionary and may not only vary from said schedule but may or may not be made in absolute disregard thereof. 

Fifth: The Trustees shall collect the proceeds of any and all policies now or hereafter subject hereto as and when the same shall become payable and such proceeds shall be held by it for the uses and trusts and for the purposes herein set forth. The Trustee is authorized to adjust, compromise and satisfy any and all claims arising out of the insurance policies, or any of them, upon such terms as to it may seem just and proper and its decision shall in all respects be binding and conclusive. It may institute, maintain and enter into any litigation to enforce the payment of any policy held by it hereunder, but in no event shall it be required to institute, maintain or enter into any litigation until it first shall have been indemnified to its satisfaction against any and all expenses or liabilities incurred or which may be incurred by it in connection with any such litigation.

to exercise any rights to subscribe for stocks and bonds or other allotments received by reason of securities held in the Trust Fund and to sell such rights for such prices as to it may seem best; to join in any plan of reorganization, consolidation, merger, foreclosure or other proceedings in respect to any corporation in which the Trustee, as such, may hold stocks, bonds or other securities and to deposit, surrender or exchange such stocks, bonds, or other securities and pay any amounts assessed thereon under any such plan; to determine and fix from time to time the value of any of the securities in the Trust Fund and any such value so fixed shall in all respects and for all purposes be binding upon any person in any way interested in the Trust Fund; to determine the amount of and pay reasonable compensation to any counsel and agents employed by it in the discharge of its duties hereunder, for which payments, as well as for such other expenses and charges as may be necessarily and properly incurred, it shall be entitled to reimbursement.

It shall incur no liability to anyone in acting upon any notice, request, consent, certificate, note or other paper or document believed by it to be genuine and to have been signed by the proper person or persons; it may consult with counsel and the opinion of such counsel shall be full and complete authorization and protection in respect to any action taken or suffered by it in good faith and in accordance with the opinion of such counsel; it may select and employ in and about the execution of this trust suitable attorneys, agents and employees and shall not be answerable for their default or misconduct if such attorneys, agents or employees shall have been selected with reasonable care, nor shall the Trustee in any other manner be answerable or accountable under any circumstances whatever, except for its own gross negligence or bad faith.

commissions as well as any expenses incurred by it before making any payments for any purpose hereunder.

Company, hereinafter acquired by him. 

DISTRIBUTION OF TRUST 

Upon receiving written notice of the death of any of the Insurers hereunder, the Trustee shall proceed with all convenient diligence to prepare, serve and file such notices and proofs of death, and to take any and all such other steps as in its discretion, may be necessary to collect the amount due and owing upon the policies of insurance held under the trust, and receipt of the Trustee for any amounts paid to it under said policies of insurance shall be full and complete acquittance to the Insurer or Insurers, who shall be under no obligation to see to the application of the insurance money by the Trustee; in order to effect the collection of the amounts owing upon said policies, the Trustee may, in its discretion, institute and maintain any and all actions at law or in equity as it may deem necessary or proper, and it shall also have full authority in its discretion, without the order or approval of any court, to make and effect any compromise, settlement or adjustment in dispute, upon such terms as it may consider just and equitable. 

With the proceeds of such life insurance on the life of any of said Insurers so collected, the Trustee shall purchase the stock belonging to such decedent held hereunder for the equal proportionate benefit of the surviving Insurers at a price determined by the book value of said stock at the time of the last annual inventory preceding the death of such decedent, paying the purchase price to the executors, administrators or assigns of such decedent. (There are several different ways of determining the price to be paid for decedent's stock. A flat price may be fixed in the agreement, or by letter sent by Insurers to Trustee. The price may be further modified by providing that it shall reflect operating losses or profits occurring since the price was last fixed, as well as any insurance proceeds which may have been paid the corporation.) 

If the net proceeds of said insurance policies on the life of such decedent shall be insufficient to purchase all of his stock held hereunder at the price so fixed, it is expressly understood and agreed that each of the survivors of the Insurers or his assigns, shall have the right to purchase the balance of said stock in equal proportions at said price within six (6) months after the death of such decedent, upon giving his promissory note or notes therefor payable in five (5) equal installments on or before one to five years respectively after the death of such decedent, with interest from the date of death at six per cent (6%) per annum. If said proceeds shall be more than sufficient to purchase all of said stock of such decedent, the excess shall be paid to the survivors of the Insurers in equal shares. The proceeds of the sale of said stock including said promissory notes, if any, shall be by the Trustee turned over to the executor or administrator of the decedent. (These terms are varied to suit the individual case. In order to prevent undue hardship, the Trustee may be authorized to extend the time of payment, if thought desirable for the benefit of all concerned, as in a period of very poor business, etc.)

TERMINATION OF THE CONTRACT 

This agreement may be terminated at any time upon written notice to the Trustee, signed by all the Insurers, or the survivors of them, or by similar notice signed by the Trustee and addressed to all of the Insurers, or the survivors of them, and upon payment and delivery to the Insurers or to the survivors of them, all of the property and assets of the trust estate, the trusts herein created shall terminate without any application to or further action by any court.

decedent shall file with the Trustee a statement setting forth the book value of the interest of the said decedent in the co-partnership and stating that such book value has been determined as hereinabove provided. If the survivor and the executor or administrator of the decedent shall not file such statement with the Trustee within sixty days of the appointment of such executor or administrator, the Trustee at any time thereafter upon the request of either the survivor or the said executor or administrator shall cause the book value of the interest of the decedent to be determined in pursuance of the foregoing provisions by an accountant to be selected by the Trustee, and the signed determination of such accountant as to the book value of the interest of said decedent in the said co-partnership, stating that the book value of such interest has been determined by him in pursuance of the foregoing provisions, shall be absolute, conclusive and binding upon all persons interested hereunder.

The said survivor shall be credited at the time when each such payment is made. with the amount of the payment, and each such payment shall be regarded for all of the purposes of this agreement as a payment by the said survivor on account or in full, as the case may be, of the said purchase price. In no event, however, shall the purchase price of the interest of the decedent in the said co-partnership be deemed to be less than the amount of the insurance moneys less the Trustee's lawful commissions, disbursements and counsel fees. 

If the said aggregate amount so paid by the Trustee is less than the said purchase price, the said survivor shall, as soon as the last payment of insurance moneys has been made by the Trustee to the said executor or administrator (unless he elects to pay the balance of the said purchase price in cash) make and deliver to the said executor or administrator six notes of equal amount which notes taken together shall aggregate the unpaid balance of the said purchase price, and which notes shall be payable respectively, two, four, six, eight, ten and twelve months from its date, with interest at the rate of six per centum per annum, the interest on each note being payable at the maturity thereof, and each of which notes shall contain a provision that in case of default in payment of principal all notes subsequently due shall become immediately due and payable. When the insurance moneys have been made over to the said executor or administrator as aforesaid, and the foregoing notes have been executed and delivered, or cash paid in lieu of such notes, or if the aggregate amount paid by the Trustee in pursuance of the provisions of this paragraph 4, to such executor or administrator is in full payment of such purchase price, then upon such payment of the purchase price the said executor or administrator shall execute and deliver to the survivor an instrument in writing, acknowledging the receipt of such cash, or of such cash and notes as the case may be, and waiving any and all rights to an accounting by such survivor, and stating that such survivor has purchased, and that he has sold to such survivor, all right, title and interest in the said co-partnership, and in the property belonging to the said co-partnership, owned by the party so dying at the time of his death. 

5. All right, title and interest of any kind of any person in any policy of life insurace upon the life of the survivor of the parties of the first and second parts in which the Trustee hereunder is named as beneficiary shall forthwith, upon the death of that one of the parties of the first and second parts who is the first to die, pass to the executor or administrator of the decedent, but the survivor shall take such policy or policies and shall pay to such executor or administrator the surrender value thereof, and upon payment being made the Trustee and such executor or administrator shall execute papers efficient to transfer all rights therein to the survivor; provided, however, that if such payment is not made within thirty days of the appointment of such executor or administrator, such policy or policies shall be made over to such executor or administrator and all parties shall sign the papers required to bring that about.

7. The Trustee may, out of the proceeds of such policy or policies, repay any advances made to it or reimburse itself for any advances made by it for expenses incurred in collecting or attempting to collect any sum from an insurance company by suit or otherwise.

If this trust agreement is revoked or terminated in any manner other than by the death of either of the said parties of the first and second parts, the Trustee shall be entitled to a fee equal to one-fourth of one per centum of the face value of all insurance policies in which the Trustee has been named the beneficiary, as Trustee hereunder.

PURPOSE 

2. The purpose of the agreement is to protect the Stockholders in the event of the death of any one of them by providing for the sale of his stock in The----- ---Corporation to the surviving Stockholders, such stock being described in the Schedule attached hereto and made a part hereof. It is also the purpose of this agreement to provide for any sale of the said stock during the lifetime of a stockholder upon the conditions as hereinafter set forth.

THE STOCKHOLDERS 

3. Each of the Stockholders has made application for insurance on his life to be made payable to the 

Trustee under this agreement as set forth in the Schedule. The rights under any such policies of insurance are reserved to the Stockholders and shall be exercised only upon the joint consent of all of them or the survivor or survivors of them except as specifically provided herein. 

4. Each of the stockholders has executed an assignment in blank of the stock of The_---- .__Corporation standing in his name and has deposited the certificate with the Trustee as set forth in the Schedule. 

Such assignment and deposit, however, shall in no way affect the rights of the Stockholders to vote such stock and to collect dividends thereon as heretofore until such time as a sale has been completed under the terms of this agreement. 

5. No certificate of stock or insurance policy subject to this agreement shall be assigned or otherwise disposed of during the continuance of the agreement except as specifically provided herein. 

6. The Stockholders agree to pay all premiums on the insurance policies subject to this agreement promptly as they become due and agree that the insurance proceeds shall be used for the purpose of purchasing the aforesaid stock as herein provided. The proportion of the total premiums on the foregoing policies to be charged to each Stockholder shall be equal to the ratio of the shares standing in his name to the total shares standing in the names of all of the Stockholders, as set forth in the Schedule. The Stockholders hereby authorize and direct The__ --Corporation to pay any such amounts as they become due and deduct the sums so paid from the compensation or dividends payable to them. If any amounts are paid to a Stockholder or to the Trustee under any insurance policy during the life of the insured as dividends, refunds, cash values, disability income or otherwise, such amounts shall be received for the benefit of and shall be paid over to all of the Stockholders in the same proportions in which they are charged with premiums. 

THE TRUSTEE 

7. The Trustee agrees to receive the aforesaid certificates of stock and policies of insurance and any other certificates or policies of insurance and any other certificates or policies added hereto and agrees to hold them subject to the conditions of this agreement. Until the death of a Stockholder, the Trustee shall be under no duty or obligation with respect to this agreement except to hold the said policies of insurance, certificates of stock and other documents given over to its possession. Upon receipt of due notice of the death of any Stockholder, the Trustee shall collect the proceeds of the insurance on the life of the deceased Stockholder and apply the same as hereinafter provided. The Trustee shall not be bound to institute any legal proceedings for the collection of such proceeds unless the expenses of the legal action shall be first advanced. 

8. As soon as possible after the death of a Stockholder and collection of the insurance, the Trustee shall transfer to the surviving Stockholders the stock owned by the deceased as set forth in the Schedule. 

For that purpose the Trustee shall cause new certificates of stock to be issued in place of the certificate deposited by the deceased Stockholder as set forth in the Schedule. Each surviving Stockholder shall receive an amount of stock equal to the ratio of the shares standing in his name to the total shares standing in the names of the surviving Stockholders as set forth in the Schedule. The insurance proceeds collected [165]

by the Trustee at the death of the Stockholder shall be paid over to the executor or administrator of the deceased as the purchase price or a part thereof for the said stock. If the amount of such proceeds is less than the value of the stock transferred to the surviving Stockholders, the deficiency in the purchase price shall be paid to the executor or administrator of the deceased Stockholder in cash, each surviving Stockholder contributing his pro rata share, or such deficiency shall be made up as follows: Each of the surviving stockholders shall give five (5) notes of equal amount to the executor or administrator of the deceased Stockholder to cover his pro rata share of such deficiency, such notes to mature one (1), two (2), three (3), four (4) and five (5) years respectively after the death of the Stockholder. The notes shall bear interest at the rate of six per cent (6%). annually until paid but payment may be made at any time before maturity. Any unpaid notes or amounts due hereunder shall be secured by stock of The______ --Corporation to the extent of their face value and they shall be payable to the executor or administrator of the deceased Stockholder or his order. The Stockholders hereby authorize and direct The------- ..Corporation to pay any such notes given by them as they become due, and deduct the sums so paid from the compensation or dividends payable to them. 

9. The executor or administrator of the deceased Stockholder shall accept the insurance proceeds paid over by the Trustee and any cash and/or notes given by the surviving Stockholders as above provided in full payment for the stock of the deceased and shall have no right, title or interest in the said stock except the stock held as collateral security. 10. For the purpose of this agreement the purchase price of the stock set forth in the Schedule shall be the value stipulated by the Stockholders each year as of‒‒‒and the respective Stockholders hereby agree to sell their said stock at the said value or as otherwise provided herein. Such value shall be promptly filed with the Trustee and kept with the original of the agreement. In the event that more than a year has elapsed between the date of the last valuation filed with the Trustee and the date of the death of a Stockholder, the executor or administrator of the deceased Stockholder and the surviving Stockholders shall agree upon the value as of the date of death and the purchase shall be made on that basis. If such value cannot be agreed upon as aforesaid then the value of the stock to be purchased shall be fixed by a Board of Arbitration consisting of one member appointed by the executor or administrator of the deceased, one member appointed by the surviving Stockholders and the third member appointed by the two so appointed. 

The decision of such Board shall be binding upon all parties to this agreement. The value of the stock of a deceased Stockholder, however, shall in no case be less than the amount of the insurance proceeds payable at his death, less the amount of any compensation or charges deductible therefrom. TERMINATION 

12. This agreement shall terminate upon: (a) The written agreement of the Stockholders; (b) The lapse or cancellation of the policy or policies on the life of any stockholder; 

(c) The dissolution, bankruptcy or insolvency of The... 

Corporation; (d) The transfer of the stock of a deceased Stockholder to the surviving Stockholders as herein provided; (e) The sale by a Stockholder during his lifetime of all or any of his shares of stock in The Corporation; (f) The simultaneous deaths of all of the Stockholders so that no survivor or survivors of them can be determined. 

13. If any Stockholder elects to sell any of his said stock during his lifetime, he shall give the Trustee and the remaining Stockholders written notice thereof, and the said Stockholders shall have the exclusive right at any time within.... days from the date of such notice to purchase such stock at a value not greater than the value on file with the Trustee at that time. If the date of such valuation is more than [166]

a year prior to the date of such notice of sale, then the seller and purchasers shall agree upon the valuation at which the purchase shall be made. If such value cannot be agreed upon, then the value shall be fixed by a Board of Arbitration consisting of one member appointed by the Seller, one member appointed by the purchaser or purchasers and the third member appointed by the two so appointed. The decision of such 

Board shall be binding upon all parties to this agreement. Each Stockholder shall have the right to purchase a proportion of such stock equal to the ratio of the shares standing in his name to the total shares standing in the names of all of the Stockholders who exercise their right to purchase hereunder. 

14. In the event of the termination of this agreement under subdivisions (a), (b), (c) and (e) of paragraph 12, the insurance policies subject to this agreement shall be disposed of as all of the Stockholders who are parties hereto shall jointly direct and in the event of termination under sub-division (d) the policies remaining on the lives of the surviving Stockholders shall be disposed of as the said surviving Stockholders shall jointly direct. In no event, however, shall a Stockholder who survives the termination of this agreement be deprived of his pro rata share in the cash values of the said insurance policies, such share being equal to the ratio of the premium payments charged to him to the total premium payments of the surviving Stockholders. In the event of the termination of this agreement under subdivision (f) of paragraph 12, the insurance proceeds collected by the Trustee shall be paid over pro rata to the executors or administrators of the deceased Stockholders, each share being equal to the ratio of the premium payments charged to the deceased to the total premium payments hereunder. Any stock certificates held by the Trustee at the termination of this agreement and not purchased in accordance with its terms shall be assigned and transferred to the Stockholders who deposited the same, their lawful successors or assigns. 

GENERAL PROVISIONS 

15. This agreement may be amended or altered in any of its provisions and any such change shall become effective when reduced to writing and signed by the parties hereto. This agreement shall include any common stock of The John Doe Corporation hereafter acquired by the Stockholders and shall also include any additional insurance policies assigned to the Trustee or naming the Trustee beneficiary under this agreement. 

16. Any successor of the Trustee herein named shall be substituted in its stead and shall have the same rights, powers and duties hereunder as the said Trustee. 

17. This agreement shall be binding not only upon the parties hereto, but also upon their heirs, executors, administrators, successors and assigns and the parties hereto agree to execute any instruments in writing which may be necessary or proper in the carrying out of the purposes and intent of this agreement.

In Witness Whereof, the parties hereto have executed this agreement the day and year first above written.

LAST WILL AND TESTAMENT OF FRANK CRANE 

I, FRANK CRANE, a resident of the City of Los Angeles, County of Los Angeles, State of California, and residing therein at 546 North McCadden Place, being over the age of twenty-one (21) years and of sound and disposing mind and memory, and not acting under duress, menace, fraud or undue influence of any person whomsoever, do make, publish and declare this my Last Will and Testament, in the manner following, to-wit: 

FIRST: I direct that all my debts, including my funeral expenses, expense of my last illness and the expenses of the administration of my estate, be paid by my Executor, hereinafter named, out of the first moneys coming into its hands and available therefor. 

SECOND: I hereby declare that I am married; that my wife's name is ELLA S. CRANE; and that I have but two (2) children, a son, JAMES L. CRANE, and a daughter, DAPHNE DRAKE.

THIRD: I give, devise and bequeath all of the rest and residue of my property, after the payment of the debts and expenses provided for in Paragraph "FIRST" hereof, whether such property be real, personal or mixed, of whatsoever kind or character and wheresoever situated, to my wife, ELLA S. CRAÑE. 

FOURTH I hereby nominate and appoint BANK OF ITALY NATIONAL TRUST AND SAVINGS ASSOCIA- 

TION the Executor of this, my Last Will and Testament.

LASTLY: I hereby revoke all former Wills and Codicils to Wills heretofore by me made. 

IN WITNESS WHEREOF, I have hereunto set my hand and seal this 3d day of Aug., 1927.

FRANK CRANE (Seal) 

The foregoing instrument, consisting of one (1) page besides this, was, at the date hereof, by said FRANK CRANE, signed, sealed and published as and declared to be his Last Will and Testament, in the presence of us, who, at his request and in his presence and in the presence of each other, have signed our names as witnesses hereto. 

MAY STANHOPE, 

Residing at 328 So. Benton Way, Los Angeles, Calif.

R. F. STEWART, 

Residing at 5126½ Clinton St., Los Angeles, Calif.

WILL OF HENRY P. DAVISON 

I, HENRY P. DAVISON, of Locust Valley, Nassau County, New York, do hereby make, publish and declare this instrument as and for my last will and testament, hereby revoking all other and former wills and codicils by me at any time heretofore made.

FIRST. I direct that all my debts, funeral and testamentary charges shall be paid as soon after my decease as may conveniently be done.

I also direct that all succession or inheritance taxes which may be levied or assessed against my estate and the legacies, life estate and devises hereinafter set forth shall be paid out of my residuary estate. 

SECOND. I give and bequeath to my wife, Kate Trubee Davison, all of my personal effects and jewelry, wherever located, and also the household furniture and equipment, pictures, works of art, silver, ornaments, bric-a-brac and supplies, which are located on the premises known as No. 690 Park Avenue, Borough of Manhattan, City of New York, for her own use, absolutely and forever.

I also give and bequeath to my wife, Kate Trubee Davison, my automobiles and all equipment relating thereto, and my yachts, together with all their contents and equipment. 

THIRD. In token of my regard and affection for my daughter-in-law, Dorothy Peabody Davison, and my son-in-law, Artemus L. Gates, I give and bequeath to each of them the sum of $5,000. 

FOURTH. Section 1. I give and bequeath to my Secretary, William C. Heinkel, the sum of $20,000.

Section 2. I agree and bequeath to my wife's secretary, Levina E. Oliphant, the sum of $10,000. 

FIFTH. All the rest, residue and remainder of my estate, both real and personal, of whatsoever nature, and wheresoever situated, of which I shall be seized and possessed, or to which I shall in any way be entitled at the time of my death, including any lapsed legacies, I give, devise and bequeath to Bankers Trust Company of the City of New York, in trust, nevertheless, to invest, reinvest, and keep the same invested and to collect, recover and receive the rents, issues, income, interest and profits thereof, and, after deducting all proper charges incidental to the administration of the trust, to pay the same in quarterly installments of equal amount or as nearly equal as possible to my wife, Kate Trubee Davison, for and during the term of her natural life.

Upon the death of said Kate Trubee Davison, I direct that said trust shall thereupon terminate and the trustees shall assign, transfer, deliver and pay over the principal of said trust as follows: 

1. To my son, Frederick Trubee Davison, the sum of $4,500,000 (which at his option may be paid either in cash or in securities at their market valuation on the date of delivery) together with all shares of stock of Peacock Point Corporation, a New York corporation, which were held in said trust, and I give and bequeath the same to him accordingly. In the event that said Frederick Trubee Davison shall then be deceased, I give and bequeath said legacies of $4,500,000 and said shares of stock of Peacock Point Corporation to his executors or administrators in their representative capacity as such. 

2. All the rest, residue and remainder of the said trust fund in equal shares to my three children, Henry P. Davison, Jr., Alice Davison Gates and Frances Pomeroy Davison, or to such of them as shall then be living and to the issue of such of them as shall then be deceased, such issue taking per stirpes and not per capita, and I give, devise and bequeath the same to them accordingly.

SIXTH. Inasmuch as I have otherwise provided for my sister, Mary Davison Le Braz, and for my wife's sister, Alice 

Trubee Sallmon, I make no provision for either of them under this my Will.. 

SEVENTH. I have made no provision in this my will for my household servants and employes inasmuch as I have expressed to my wife and to my children my wishes in regard to the matter of showing my appreciation of their loyalty and faithful service to us. [ 171 ]

EIGHTH. The provisions of this will in favor of my wife, Kate Trubee Davison, are in lieu of dowers and all other rights or interest in and to my estate.

NINTH. I authorize and empower my executrix and executors, or the survivors or successors of them, and my trustee as follows: 

Section 1. To sell, mortgage, lease and convey all or any part of my estate, both real and personal (except the stock of Peacock Point Corporation) at such times and upon such terms and conditions as they may deem best in order to carry out the provisions of this my last will and testament, giving and granting to said executrix, executors and trustees full power and authority to make proper conveyances and transfers of my estate, both real and personal. 

Section 2. At the risk of my estate and without responsibility to my executrix, executors and trustees, to continue and in their or its discretion to turn over as part of the shares of my estate hereinbefore given, devised and bequeathed and in the erection of the trust herein create, any stock, bonds or other securities in which at the time of my death any portion of my estate shall be invested.

Section 3. In any such case in which my executrix, executors and trustees are required, pursuant to the provisions of this will, to divide any portion of my estate into parts or shares or to distribute the same, I authorize them or it, in their or its discretion, to make such division or distribution in kind, or partly in kind and partly money, and for the purpose of such allotment the judgment of my executrix, executors and trustees concerning the property thereof and the relative value for the purpose of distribution of the securities so allotted, shall be binding and conclusive on all persons interested in my estate.

Section 4. At the risk of the trust estate, and without responsibility to my trustee, to continue to hold any stock, bonds or other securities in which at the time of my death any portion of my estate shall be invested, although the same shall not be of the character authorized by the laws of the State of New York for trust investment, and likewise to have full power and authority to dispose of, call in and change any and all investments (except the stock of Peacock Point Corporation) and to invest and reinvest in any stock, bonds or other securities which in its discretion may be deemed safe and for the best interest of my estate, and said trustee shall not be restricted to securities of the character authorized by the laws of the State of New York for trust investments.

Section 5. I hereby expressly direct my executrix and executors to assign and transfer to my trustee hereunder as part of the principal of the trust created under Article Fifth, all shares of stock of Peacock Point Corporation which I may own at the time of my death and I expressly direct any trustee to hold such shares of stock during the trust period and not in any event to sell or otherwise dispose of said shares of stock, or any part thereof, and upon the termination of the trust to assign, transfer and deliver the stock of said corporation or the stock of any successor corporation to my son, Frederick Trubee Davison, or to his executors or administrators in their respective capacities as such, as provided for in Subdivision 

1 of Article Fifth hereof. 

Section 6. I expressly authorize my executrix and executors, in their absolute and uncontrolled discretion, to settle, compromise and adjust any and all claims in favor of or against my estate. 

TENTH. My executrix, executors and trustee are expressly authorized in their or its discretion to allot or appropriate any funds or money belonging to my estate which may be allowed or permitted to remain in the business of J. P. Morgan & Company under agreements by and between the partners thereof, or otherwise, to the trust created under this will. 

ELEVENTH. Section 1. In case of securities, taken or purchased for the trust fund at a premium, by executrix, executors and trustee shall not be bound to set aside any part of the income thereof as sinking fund to retire or absorb such premiums.

Section 2. I further authorize my said executrix and executors, or my trustee, in their or its discretion, to vote in person or by proxy upon all stocks or other securities held by them; to exchange the securities of any corporation or company for other securities issued by the same, or by any other corporation or company at such times and upon such terms and conditions as my executrix and executors or my trustee shall deem proper, to consent to the reorganization, consolidation or merger of any corporation or company, or to the sale or lease of its property, or any portion thereof, to any person, corporation or company, or to the lease by any person, corporation or company of his or its property or any portion thereof, to such corporation or company and upon such reorganization, consolidation, merger, sale or lease, to exchange the securities held by it for the security issued in connection therewith; to pay all assessments, subscriptions and other sums of money as the executrix, executors or the trustees may deem expedient for the protection of their or its interests as holders of any stocks, bonds or other securities of any corporation or company, and to exercise any option contained in any stocks, bonds or other securities, for the conversion of the same into other securities, or to take advantage of any rights to subscribe for additional stocks, bonds, or other securities, and to make any and all necessary payments there for and generally to exercise with respect of all stocks, bonds or other investments, held by the executrix, executors or trustee hereunder, all rights, powers and privileges as are or may be lawfully exercised by any person owning similar property in his own right. 

TWELFTH. I direct my executrix and executors to accept any statement which may be furnished or submitted by the firm of J. P. Morgan & Company, of which I am a member as to any interest which I may have at the time of my death in said firm of J. P. Morgan & Company or as to any sums of money, if any, that may be due me from said firm. Such statement shall be final, conclusive and binding upon my said executrix and executors, and my estate and any person or party interested therein, and shall be accepted without question. Such statement may be of such character as may be determined upon by the said firm of J. P. Morgan & Co., and may in their discretion cover and refer to any amounts which may be due me either as a member of said firm or otherwise.

THIRTEENTH. The words "executrix" and "executors" whenever used in this will shall be construed to mean the executrix and executors hereinafter named, or the one or more of them who shall qualify or the survivors or survivor of them, and their successors and successor. 

FOURTEENTH. I hereby nominate, constitute and appoint my wife, Kate Trubee Davison, and my sons Frederick Trubee

Davison and Harry P. Davison, Jr., as executrix and executors to this, my last will and testament. 

I hereby nominate, constitute and appoint Bankers Trust Company of the city of New York, trustee of the trust herein created.
