I hereby give and grant unto my said executrix, executors and trustee the amplest and fullest powers to enable them to carry into effect and to accomplish the purposes under this, my will, and I expressly order and direct that no bonds or other security shall be required in any jurisdiction of my said executrix, executors or trustee for the faithful performance of their respective duties hereunder. 

IN TESTIMONY WHEREOF, I have hereunto set my hand and seal this twenty-seventh day of April, in the year one thousand, nine hundred and twenty-two.

HENRY P. DAVISON. 

The foregoing instrument, consisting of seven typewritten pages, was on the day of the date thereof signed, sealed, published and declared by Henry P. Davison, the testator therein named, as and for his last will and testament in the pres- [172]

ence of us, the undersigned, who at his request, and in his presence, and in the presence of each other, have hereunto set our names as witnesses; this clause having been first read to us, and we now intending to certify that the matters herein specified took place in fact and in order herein stated. 

GEORGE B. CASE, Englewood, N. J. 

LEONARD HULL SMITH, 8420 Eleventh Avenue, Brooklyn, N. Y. 

JAMES J. MCDERMOTT, 1030 Hoe Avenue, New York City. 

LAST WILL AND TESTAMENT OF ELBERT H. GARY 

I, ELBERT H. GARY, of Jericho, County of Nassau and State of New York, do make, publish and declare this to be my last will and testament, hereby revoking all other and former wills by me at any time made. 

ARTICLE I. I direct that all my just debts and my funeral and testamentary expenses be paid as soon as practicable after my death. There are no claims or obligations of any kind against me except such as are public and well known, including principally an indebtedness of one hundred thousand dollars ($100,000.00) to the Chatham Phenix National Bank and Trust Company duly secured. 

ARTICLE II. As a first claim and charge upon my estate, I make the following gifts and provisions to and for the benefit of my children and grandchildren and without preference or priority of payment of one over any other:— 

SECTION 1. I give and bequeath unto my Executor and Trustee hereinafter named the sum of eight hundred thousand dollars ($800,000.00) in trust, to invest and reinvest the same as hereinafter provided, to collect and receive the income thereof and to pay the net income to my daughter, Gertrude Gary Sutcliffe, during her natural life, in equal quarterly payments as far as may be, and upon her death to pay the net income thereof to her son, Elbert Gary Sutcliffe, during his natural life, in equal quarterly payments as far as may be, and upon his death to pay over, assign, transfer and deliver the principal of said trust to his lawful issue him surviving, per stirpes and not per capita. If the said Elbert Gary Sutcliffe shall leave no lawful issue, then the principal of said trust shall be paid over, assigned, transferred and delivered to those persons then living who would have been my lawful heirs according to the laws of the State of New York governing the descent of real estate in case of intestacy if I had died at the date of the death of said Elbert Gary Sutcliffe and not notwithstanding the principal of said trust may be invested in personal property. 

SECTION 2. I give and bequeath unto my said Executor and Trustee the sum of eight hundred thousand dollars ($800,000.00) in trust, to invest and reinvest the same as hereinafter provided, to collect and receive the income thereof and to pay the net income to my daughter, Bertha Gary Campbell, during her natural life, in equal quarterly payments as far as may be, and upon her death to pay the net income thereof to her daughter, Julia Elizabeth Campbell, during her natural life, in equal quarterly payments as far as may be, and upon her death to pay over, assign, transfer and deliver the principal of said trust to her lawful issue her surviving, per stirpes and not per capita. If the said Julia Elizabeth Campbell shall leave no lawful issue, then the principal of said trust shall be paid over, assigned, transferred and delivered to those persons then living who would have been my lawful heirs according to the laws of the State of New York governing the descent of real estate in case of intestacy if I had died at the date of the death of said Julia Elizabeth Campbell and notwithstanding the principal of said trust may be invested in personal property. 

SECTION 3. I give and bequeath unto my said Executor and Trustee the sum of three hundred thousand dollars ($300,000.00) in trust, to invest and reinvest the same as hereinafter provided, to collect and receive the income thereof and to pay the net income to my grandson, Elbert Gary Sutcliffe, during his natural life, in equal quarterly payments as far as may be, and upon his death to pay over, assign, transfer and deliver the principal of said trust to his lawful issue him surviving, per stirpes and not per capita. If the said Elbert Gary Sutcliffe shall leave no lawful issue, then the principal of said trust shall be paid over, assigned, transferred and delivered to those persons then living who would have been my lawful heirs according to the laws of the State of New York governing the descent of real estate in case of intestacy if I had died at the date of the death of said Elbert Gary Sutcliffe and notwithstanding the principal of said trust may be invested in personal property.

SECTION 4. I give and bequeath unto my said Executor and Trustee the sum of three hundred thousand dollars ($300,000.00) in trust, to invest and reinvest the same as hereinafter provided, to collect and receive the income thereof and to pay the net income to my granddaughter, Julia Elizabeth Campbell, during her natural life, in equal quarterly payments as far as may be, and upon her death to pay over, assign, transfer and deliver the principal of said trust to her lawful issue her surviving, per stirpes and not per capita. If the said Julia Elizabeth Campbell shall leave no lawful issue, then the principal of said trust shall be paid over, assigned, transferred and delivered to those persons then living who would have been my lawful heirs according to the laws of the State of New York governing the descent of real estate in case of intestacy if I had died at the date of the death of said Julia Elizabeth Campbell and notwithstanding the principal of said trust may be invested in personal property. 

ARTICLE III. I give and bequeath unto my daughters, Gertrude Gary Sutcliffe and Bertha Gary Campbell, share and share alike, all my personal wearing apparel, jewelry and personal belongings, including diplomas, decorations, memorials and gifts connected with my business career or otherwise which they may wish and decide to retain on account of such articles having a sentimental value to them or their children. If either one does not survive me, then her child shall take the mother's place under this provision. 

ARTICLE IV. I devise in fee simple unto my two daughters, Gertrude Gary Sutcliffe and Bertha Gary Campbell, or their heirs, the lot owned by me in the cemetery at Wheaton, Illinois, on which I have erected a granite mausoleum, subject to and upon the following conditions and limitations: I direct that my body after death be placed and kept in one of the crypts in said mausoleum, or in any other that may take its place, and that the body of my first wife be retained and kept in the crypt adjoining, and that the body of my second wife after death be placed in another crypt adjoining, unless she by will or in some manner otherwise directs. I consent and direct that the bodies of my father and mother be removed and placed in crypts in said mausoleum, provided my brother, my sister, or either of my daughters requests and none of them objects. I consent and direct that the bodies of my daughters and their husbands, if any, and all descendants and their husbands and wives, respectively, after death be placed in said mausoleum, unless by will or otherwise they respectively object. It is my will and direction that the body of no other person be placed in said mausoleum or any built to take its place. The Merchants Loan and Trust Company of Chicago, Illinois, Trustee under deed of trust dated July 

29, 1918, and its duly authorized agents, shall be given access to said lot and mausoleum for the purpose of caring for, maintaining, repairing and replacing the mausoleum thereon erected, as in said deed of trust provided. 

ARTICLE V. I give and bequeath unto my said Executor and Trustee the sum of fifty thousand dollars ($50,000.00) in trust, to invest and reinvest the same as hereinafter provided, to collect and receive the income thereof and, so far as necessary after using the income from the principal of the funds heretofore given by me to the Trustees of the Church by letters duly recorded in their minutes, to apply the net income to the care and maintenance of the First Methodist Epis- [173]

copal Church edifice of Wheaton, Illinois, commonly known as the Gary Memorial Church, and to keep the same in good condition and repair. Any net income not used in any year for the purpose above stated shall be set aside as a depreciation fund to be used in making extraordinary repairs to or in replacement of the present Church and any church built to take its place if and when necessary by reason of its decay or destruction. Whenever such depreciation fund reaches the sum of one hundred thousand dollars ($100,000.00), the net income over and above the amounts necessary for the care and maintenance aforesaid may be used for the general purposes of said Church, but if and when such depreciation fund for any cause falls beblow one hundred thousand dollars ($100,000.00) the net income over and above the amounts necessary for the care and maintenance aforesaid shall be set aside for such depreciation fund until it again equals one hundred thousand dollars ($100,000.00). It is my intention to provide for, and I direct that, in the manner above specified, there shall always be maintained, as a memorial to my father and mother, a church edifice as good as or of better quality, value and character than the present edifice of said Church costing not less than one hundred thousand dollars ($100,000.00). 

ARTICLE VI. SECTION 1. I give, devise and bequeath unto my wife, Emma T. Gary, for her own use and occupation during the term of her natural life, unless sooner forfeited or surrendered as hereinafter provided, and without impeachment of waste my real estate known as "Ivy Hall," comprising about one hundred and nine and seven hundred and seventyfive thousandths (109.775) acres situated at or near Jericho in the Town of Oyster Bay, County of Nassau and State of 

New York, together with all buildings and structures thereon and all furniture, clothing, pictures, works of art, silver, plate, ornaments, bric-a-brac, household goods or supplies, books, linen, china, glass, automobiles and all implements, plants and tools which may be in or upon my said real estate at the time of my death or shall then be customarily used by me in connection therewith; excepting, however, any and all personal property bequeathed by Article III of this my will and also any and all live stock. I direct that no inventory or security shall ever be required of my said wife in respect of any of the articles of personal property by this Section of my will bequeathed to her for life, and neither she nor her estate shall ever be held liable for any consumption, use, damage, loss or injury in respect to any of said articles. 

SECTION 2. The cost of all necessary repairs to said premises and all taxes, assessments, water rates and other governmental charges thereon shall be paid by my said wife while she has the use of the same. My said Executor and 

Trustee, or its duly authorized agents, shall have access to said premises at reasonable times for the purpose of ascertaining whether all necessary repairs have been made, and all receipted bills for taxes, assessments, water rates and other governmental charges thereon shall be placed in the custody of said Executor and Trustee. Neither my said wife, the remaindermen nor said Executor and Trustee shall be required to insure the improvements on said premises or their contents against loss or damage by fire or otherwise, leaving it to my said wife and the remaindermen, at their own cost and expense and without right of reimbursement for insurance premiums one as against the other, to insure their respective interests if and when they desire so to do. 

SECTION 3. In case my said wife does not herself use and occupy said premises or in case she fails or neglects to repair the same or promptly to pay the cost of such repairs and all taxes, assessments, water rates and other governmental charges, or any of them, or in case she notifies my said Executor and Trustee that she desires to surrender her life estate in said premises because the maintenance thereof will be or has become burdensome to her, or upon the death of my said wife in case her life estate in said premises has not been sooner forfeited or surrendered, my said Executor and Trustee shall sell said premises, provided it shall be convinced that the price which can be obtained therefor is fair and reasonable and the proceeds shall thereupon be added to and become part of the principal of the trust created by Section 3 of Article X of this my will and thereafter be held in trust for the uses and purposes therein stated. 

ARTICLE VII. I give and bequeath unto McKendree College, Lebanon, Illinois; University of Pittsburgh, Pittsburgh, 

Pennsylvania; Lafayette College, Easton, Pennsylvania; Trinity College, Hartford, Connecticut; Lincoln Memorial University, Harrogate, Tennessee; Syracuse University, Syracuse, New York; Northwestern University, Chicago, Illinois, and New York University, New York City, New York, and their respective lawful successors, if there shall ever be any, the sum of fifty thousand dollars ($50,000.00) each, in trust, for the purposes and subject to the conditions hereinafter stated. Each said sum with its accretions shall perpetually be called and known as the Elbert H. Gary Scholarship Fund. 

It shall be paid by my Executor hereinafter named in securities left by me at their actual value at time of payment to be selected by my Executor in its discretion. It may be retained by the legatee in the securities delivered to it by my Executor or they may be sold and the proceeds invested and reinvested in other safe and first-class securities. The net income of such fund shall from time to time be used toward the payment of the regular tuition fees of students, male or female, to be selected from time to time by the vote of a majority of the Board of Trustees of the legatee. By such Trustees is meant the Board governing the affairs of the legatee by whatever name it may be called. It is my desire that in making such selections the Board in its discretion will choose persons who are least pecuniarly able to pay their tuition, and who are morally, mentally and physically worthy and competent. If at any time the total net income from such fund is not necessarily used for such tuitions, then the surplus may be used by said Board of Trustees for such other university or college expenses as a majority of the Board may determine. The principal shall always be kept and maintained intact and undiminished. If the legatee should ever cease to exist and function as an eduactional institution and should have no lawful successor functioning as an educational institution, then the said fund shall revert to those persons then living who would have been my lawful heirs according to the laws of the State of New York governing the descent of real estate in case of intestacy if I had died at the date of the happening of such contingency and notwithstanding the principal of such fund may be invested in personal property. If there shall be a successor or successors entitled to receive said fund under the provisions of this will, the said fund shall be taken and held by such successor or successors subject to the conditions and terms hereinabove expressed.

ARTICLE VIII. I have heretofore given to servants of myself and wife at the end of ten years service one thousand dollars ($1,000.00) each in securities. If such servants, or any of them, should continue ten years longer in the service of myself and wife, it is my intention to repeat such donations but, if I fail to do so prior to my death, I direct my said Executor to pay to each such servant completing his or her second ten years of service during my lifetime who has not received such donation, the sum of one thousand dollars ($1,000.00) in securities at their market or actual value at time of payment to be selected by my said Executor out of the securities owned by me at the time of my death. If I do not live until the expiration of the second ten years of service of such servants, or any of them, I direct my said Executor to pay to each of such servants the sum of one hundred dollars ($100.00) for each full year of service from the expiration of such first ten years of service to the date of my death, and to Alfred George Fletcher I direct my said Executor to pay, in addition to any amount to which he may be entitled under the foregoing provisions, one hundred dollars ($100.00) for each full year which he was in my employ prior to the commencement of the first ten year period of service for which he has received a donation as aforesaid.

ARTICLE IX. I shall leave in a separate paper directions for distributing certain articles of no considerable value and it is my wish and desire that such directions be carried into effect, if at the time of my death I have not already disposed of such articles.

ARTICLE X. All the rest, residue and remainder of my property and estate, real and personal, of whatsoever nature and wheresoever situated and whether acquired before or after the execution of this will, including the property herein- [174]

before attempted to be disposed of, the disposition whereof by reason of lapse or other cause shall fail to take effect, and all property over which at the time of my death I shall have any power of testamentary disposition, I now dispose of as follows, that is to say: 

SECTION 1. I give, devise and bequeath one-third (3) thereof unto my said Executor and Trustee, in trust, to hold or, in its discretion, to convert the same and to invest and reinvest the proceeds thereof as hereinafter provided, to receive and collect the income thereof and to pay the net income to my daughgter, Gertrude Gary Sutcliffe, during her natural life, in equal quarterly payments as far as may be, and upon her death to pay the net income thereof to her son, Elbert Gary Sutcliffe, during his natural life, in equal quarterly payments as far as may be, and upon his death to pay over, assign, transfer and deliver the principal of said trust to his lawful issue him surviving, per stirpes and not per capita, and if the said Elbert Gary Sutcliffe shall leave no lawful issue, then to those persons then living who would have been my lawful heirs according to the laws of the State of New York governing the descent of real estate in case of intestacy if I had died at the date of the death of said Elbert Gary Sutcliffe and notwithstanding the principal of said trust may be invested in personal property. 

SECTION 2. I give, devise and bequeath one-third (3) thereof unto my said Executor and Trustee in trust, to hold or, in its discretion, to convert the same and to invest and reinvest the proceeds thereof as hereinafter provided, to receive and collect the income thereof and to pay the net income to my daughter, Bertha Gary Campbell, during her natural life, in equal quarterly payments as far as may be, and upon her death to pay the net income thereof to her daughter, Julia Elizabeth Campbell, during her natural life, in equal quarterly payments as far as may be, and upon her death to pay over, assign, transfer and deliver the principal of said trust to her lawful issue her surviving, per stirpes and not per capita, and if the said Julia Elizabeth Campbell shall leave no lawful issue, then to those persons then living who would have been my lawful heirs according to the laws of the State of New York governing the descent of real estate in case of intestacy if I had died at the date of the death of said Julia Elizabeth Campbell and notwithstanding the principal of said trust may be invested in personal property. 

SECTION 3. I give, devise and bequeath one-third (3) thereof unto my said Executor and Trustee in trust, to hold or, in its discretion, to convert the same and to invest and reinvest_the_proceeds thereof as hereinafter provided, to receive and collect the income thereof and to pay the net income to my wife, Emma T. Gary, during her natural life, in equal quarterly payments as far as may be, and upon the death of my said wife, or if she shall die in my lifetime, then on my death, to divide said one-third (3) part into two equal parts or shares and to hold one of such parts for my daughter, Gertrude Gary Sutcliffe, or in its discretion, to convert the same and to invest and reinvest the proceeds thereof as hereinafter provided, to receive and collect the income thereof and to pay the net income to my daughter, Gertrude Gary Sutcliffe, during her natural life, in equal quarterly payments as far as may be, and upon her death to pay over, assign, transfer and deliver such part to her son, Elbert Gary Sutcliffe, or, if he be dead, to his lawful issue him surviving, per stirpes and not per capita, and if the said Elbert Gary Sutcliffe shall leave no lawful issue, then to those persons then living who would have been my lawful heirs according to the laws of the State of New York governing the descent of real estate in case of intestacy if I had died at the date of the death of said Elbert Gary Sutcliffe and notwithstanding the principal of such part may be invested in personal property, and to hold the other part for my daughter, Bertha Gary Campbell, or, in its discretion, to convert the same and to invest and reinvest the proceeds thereof as hereinafter provided, to receive and collect the income thereof and to pay the net income to my daughter, Bertha Gary Campbell, during her natural life, in equal quarterly payments as far as may be, and upon her death to pay over, assign, transfer and deliver such part to her daughter, Julia Elizabeth Campbell, or, if she be dead, to her lawful issue her surviving, per stirpes and not per capita, and if the said Julia Elizabeth Campbell shall leave no lawful issue, then to those persons then living who would have been my lawful heirs according to the laws of the State of New York governing the descent of real estate in case of intestacy if I had died at the date of the death of said Julia Elizabeth Campbell and notwithstanding the principal of such part may be invested in personal property. 

ARTICLE XI. The provisions herein made for the benefit of my wife, Emma T. Gary, together with the marriage settlement, a copy of which will be found with my papers and securities, are in lieu of dower or any other interest in my estate.

ARTICLE XII. It is my wish and desire that my funeral services shall be held and conducted in accordance with directions which I shall leave in a separate paper. 

ARTICLE XIII. SECTION 1. I earnestly request my wife and my children and descendants that they steadfastly decline to sign any bonds or obligations of any kind as surety for any other person or persons; that they refrain from anticipating their income in any respect; that they refuse to make any loans except on the basis of first-class, well-known securities and that they invariably decline to invest in any untried or doubtful securities or property or enterprise or business. They should reject any representations or opinions of others if involved in any doubt. They will be approached frequently with suggestions for investment that are not entitled to be relied upon from a business standpoint. 

SECTION 2. I hereby direct that no legatee, devisee or beneficiary of a trust under this will shall ever, under any condition, be permitted to anticipate, convey, assign, mortgage, encumber or charge any legacy, devise or income payable to him or her under this will, or any part thereof, before it is due and payable; and that if any legatee, devisee or beneficiary of a trust anticipates, conveys, assigns, mortgages, encumbers or charges his or her legacy, devise or income, or any part thereof, or attempts so to do, then such legacy, devise or income, or part thereof, so anticipated, conveyed, assigned, mortgaged, encumbered or charged, or attempted to be anticipated, conveyed, assigned, mortgaged, encumbered or charged, shall thereby and thereupon be forfeited and I hereby give, devise and bequeath such legacy, devise or income, or part thereof, share and share alike, to such of my residuary legatees, or their descendants, as shall not have violated this provision of my will, per stirpes and not per capita. 

SECTION 3. If any objection shall be made to the probate of this will or any attempt shall be made to revoke the probate thereof by any of my heirs, next of kin, legatees, devisees, or any beneficiary under any provision of this will, or in case any contest shall arise as to the carrying into effect of any article thereof, it is my will that any heir, next of kin, legatee, devisee or beneficiary under any provision of this will who shall make or offer or permit to be made or offered any such objection or attempt or who shall inauguarate or raise or abet any such contest shall by reason thereof forfeit any and all right or interest which he or she might otherwise have under this my will or in my estate, and shall be excluded from any share or interest in my estate as legateę, devisee, heir at law, next of kin or otherwise, and I hereby give, devise and bequeath the property, interest, articles or money constituting such devise, legacy or share in my estate to which he or she or they might otherwise become entitled to such of my residuary legatees as shall not have violated this provision of my will.

ARTICLE XIV. SECTION 1. I nominate and appoint The New York Trust Company, of New York, Executor of this my will and Trustee thereunder, if and on condition that it file in the office of the Surrogate's Court of the County in which I may legally reside at the time of my death, prior to the issuance of letters testamentary, a written consent to accept in lieu of all compensation or commissions to which it would otherwise be entitled as such Executor or Trustee, at the times [ 175 J

and in the manner therein provided, the compensation named in a written agreement between_said The New York Trust Company and myself, dated the sixth day of June, 1923. In case said The New York Trust Company fails or refuses to file said consent as hereinbefore provided or fails or refuses to qualify as Executor or Trustee or in the event that it should resign or go out of business, then and in that case I authorize the aforesaid Surrogate's Court, or its successor, if any, to appoint a trust company of New York City, New York, of good standing and credit who will accept the appointment on the terms and conditions provided for the appointment and service and compensation of the said The New York Trust Company, to act as such Executor and Trustee, with all the powers and authority conferred upon said The New York Trust Company by the terms of this will. 

SECTION 2. I direct that no bond or other security shall be required from my said Executor for the faithful performance of its duty as such, or as Trustee under this will. 

SECTION 3. I authorize my said Executor and Trustee in respect to any part of my estate which under any of the provisions of this will may be held in trust by it, to retain any investment in personal property in which my estate shall be at the time of my death, to take over any such investment, to sell and change investments of and to invest, and from time to time reinvest, the principal of the trust estates held by it in the bonds of any state or government within or without the United States, or in any bonds, notes or other obligations secured by mortgage on real estate within or without the State of New York, or in the stocks or bonds of any railroad or other company in any part of the United States or Canada which at the time of such investment are in good credit and standing, provided such investments are considered by my Executor and Trustee in its discretion to be first-class. 

SECTION 4. I authorize my said Executor in its discretion to pay any pecuniary legacy given by this will, whether in trust or otherwise, either in cash or in securities to be selected by it out of the personal property which I may own at the time of my decease, and at their market or actual value at the time such payment is made, or partly in cash and partly in such securities as it shall think best. In case my said Executor elects to pay the principal of the trusts created by Sections 

1 and 3 of Article II of this will, either wholly or partly in securities, the securities shall be selected after consultation with my daughter, Gertrude Gary Sutcliffe; and in like case securities turned over as principal of the trusts created by Sections 

2 and 4 of Article II of this will shall be selected after consultation with my daughter, Bertha Gary Campbell. 

SECTION 5. I direct my said Executor and Trustee as soon as practicable after my death to sell upon the terms and conditions stated in Section 6 of this Article XIV all of my real estate, excepting such as is devised by Article VI of this my will, with the right and privilege to my wife and children, or any of them, of becoming purchasers at any such sale on equal terms with others. In case more than one of my said wife and children desire to purchase the same piece of real estate individually, it shall be sold to the highest bidder, provided the price bid is in the judgment of my said Executor and Trustee fair and reasonable and is not less than that offered by third parties. I do not specifically devise or bequeath unto my said wife and children any real or personal property, except as provided in Articles III and VI of this my will, because I think it better to furnish them with money and thus give them the opportunity to buy what they may deem suitable to their respective tastes, convenience and happiness. 

SECTION 6. I hereby authorize and empower my said Executor and Trustee to sell, in its discretion, at public or private sale, all or any part of my estate, real or personal, at such price or prices and upon such terms as it may deem best and most expedient and to execute, acknowledge and deliver to any purchaser or purchasers thereof good and sufficient deeds of conveyance and instruments of transfer. 

SECTION 7. I direct that all inheritance, legacy, succession or similar duties or taxes which shall become payable in respect to any property or interest passing under my will or any codicil which I may hereafter execute shall be paid out of the capital of my residuary estate. 

ARTICLE XV. In this my will I have made no bequests, except as provided in Articles V, VII and VIII, to religious, charitable or educational institutions or to individuals outside of my immediate family, for the reason that heretofore, at different times and in various ways, I have made donations, gifts and provisions which seem to me liberal in view of the limits of my fortune. If later it seems to me appropriate to increase the amounts of gifts already made or to make other gifts or provisions, I will take such action during my lifetime. 

IN WITNESS WHEREOF, I have hereunto set my hand and seal at the City of New York this 24th day of October, A. D. 1925.

ELBERT H. GARY (Seal) 

The preceding instrument, consisting of this and eighteen (18) other typewritten pages, each signed in the margin by the testator, was on the day of the date thereof subscribed by Elbert H. Gary, the testator therein named, in our presence, who thereupon declared the same to be his Last Will and Testament and requested us to become witnesses thereto, whereupon we, in his presence and in the presence of each other, subscribed our names thereto as such witnesses: 

NAME ADDRESS 

K. B. Halstead, 360 Washington Avenue, Brooklyn, N. Y. 

E. J. Buffington, 1140 Forest Ave., Evanston, Ill. 

F. R. Sites, Shelter Rock Road, Manhasset, N. Y.

WILL OF CONRAD HUBERT 

I, Conrad Hubert, a resident of Mountain Lakes, Lake Wales, Florida, do hereby make, publish and declare the following to be my Last Will and Testament, to-wit:

FIRST. I direct that all my just and lawful debts and funeral expenses be paid as soon after my demise as may be practicable.

SECOND. I direct my executors hereinafter named to carry out and perform on my behalf the provisions of a certain contract which I entered into with Yale Electric Corporation and C. Bertram Plante, dated May 18, 1927, and direct that the voting trust agreement therein specified be executed by my executors and that the stock of Yale Electric Corporation therein specified be deposited with the Bankers Trust Company as Voting Trustee named in the said Agreement and to be named in the voting trust agreement, and to be held pending the termination of the said agreement with said Yale Electric Corporation and said C. Bertram Plante. I further direct the voting trust certificates to be held and disposed of as part of my estate in lieu, place and stead of certificates of stock of Yale Electric Corporation. 

THIRD. I give to my friend, Mrs. Lydia Utgoff, of 144 West 57th Street, Manhattan, New York City, the sum of five thousand dollars ($5000.00) absolutely and forever.

FOURTH. I give unto my friend, Mrs. John Constable of Constableville, New York, the like sum of five thousand dollars ($5000.00), absolutely and forever.

FIFTH. I give and bequeath unto my Executors hereinafter named as Trustees, the sum of thirty thousand dollars [176]

($30,000.00) in trust, for the benefit of BENJAMIN SCOTT, now employed by Yale Electric Corporation, and to be held in and upon the following trusts, to-wit: (a) To invest the same in and upon such investments as trustees are by the laws of the state of New York permitted to make investments of trust funds; to collect and receive the income, interest or profits thereof and to pay over the net income of said trusts from time to time unto said BENJAMIN SCOTT, and in equal weekly payments, if possible, for and during the natural term of his natural life. (b) Upon his death the capital of said trust fund shall fall into my residuary estate and be disposed of in accordance with the provisions of this my WILL for the disposition of my said residuary estate. 

SIXTH. I give and bequeath unto my friend and counsellor, C. BERTRAM PLANTE, two thousand (2000) shares of the common capital stock of Yale Electric Corporation to be held by him absolutely and forever. 

SEVENTH. All the rest, residue and remainder of my estate, whether real, personal, or mixed, and wheresoever the same shall be located, including the principal of the various trust estates, hereinbefore created, from and after the death of each of the beneficiaries for life respectively, and including any lapsed legacy, and subject to the carrying out of the said agreement with Yale Electric Corporation and C. Bertram Plante, hereinbefore specified, I direct shall be divided into four equal parts or shares, and I give, devise and bequeath one of such equal parts, to-wit, one-quarter of my residuary estate unto my next of kin, other than my former wife, HELEN T. HUBERT, and to be divided and distributed to and among them, in accordance with the provisions of the statute of distribution of the State of New York, in such amounts and proportions as if I had died intestate as to such one-quarter of my said residuary estate. 

EIGHTH. And the remaining three parts or shares of my said residuary estate, to-wit: the remaining three-quarters of my said residuary estate, I give, devise and bequeath to my Executors hereinafter named and the survivors of them as Trustees, for and in and upon the following trust purposes, to-wit: (a) I direct my said Trustee to pay over the same to such religious, educational, charitable or benevolent institutions that shall in other respects be valid under the laws of the State of New York and for such purpose to select a committee of three prominent citizens who shall investigate and determine the institutions to receive such gifts or bequests and the respective amounts thereof, and whose findings shall be subject to the approval of the said Trustees. Upon the approval of such findings, the said Trustees are hereby directed to pay over and distribute the said three-quarters of my said residuary estate unto the said religious, educational, charitable and benevolent institutions so selected and found and approved and to whom I give, devise and bequeath the same absolutely and forever. 

NINTH. It is my will that none of my heirs-at-law and next of kin who shall contest the probate of this Will shall share in any distribution of my estate and I therefore hereby revoke any legacy hereinbefore given to any such next of kin who shall contest my estate and direct that the share of such one-quarter which would have gone to the next of kin so contesting my said Will be added to and distributed with the three-quarters of my Estate given for charitable, educational or religious purposes, as hereinbefore provided. 

TENTH. I direct that my said Executors and Trustees may retain any investment which may be held by me at the time of my death so long as in their judgment it is advisable so to do and further direct that my said Trustees shall not be held personally liable for any loss which may occur by reason of their holding such investments unsold subsequent to my demise. 

ELEVENTH: I also direct my Executors and Trustees hereinafter named to continue any corporate enterprises which I am interested in for such time or times as in their judgment is for the best interests of my Estate and without personal liability for so retaining such investment. My reason for this is that I am interested in various corporate enterprises whose stock or assets may not be readily sold or which require time in which to liquidate the same.. 

TWELFTH. I hereby nominate, constitute and appoint my friend and counsellor, C. BERTRAM PLANTE and BANK- 

ERS TRUST COMPANY, of the City of New York, the Executors and Trustees under this my Will, hereby directing that no bond be required of either of them for the faithful performance of their duties as such Executors and/or Trustees. 

THIRTEENTH. I hereby give unto my said Executors full power and authority to sell, mortgage or lease any real estate of which I may die seized or possessed or any way entitled to or which may come into their possession as such Executors or Trustees prior to the complete administration of my Estate or the termination of the said trusts and upon such terms and conditions and for such term as to them may seem proper; permitting such portion of the purchase price as may be necessary to remain on purchase money bond and mortgage; and for any such porpose to execute any contract, deed, bond and mortgage or lease as may be suitable and proper or which may be determined necessary to carry out any such sale, mortgage or lease.

FOURTEENTH. I hereby direct that all Federal Estate Taxes or any other inheritance taxes upon any general or specific legacy given by me in this my Will to be paid out of my residuary estate so that the said general and specific legacy be relieved therefrom.

FIFTEENTH. I hereby revoke any and all prior wills by me made. 

IN WITNESS WHEREOF, I have hereunto subscribed my name and declared this instrument to be my Last Will and 

Testament this 23rd day of December, 1927.

CONRAD HUBERT (L. S.) 

In presence of: H. L. Zachman; C. T. Hutcheson; W. W. Stake.

The foregoing instrument was signed at the end thereof and sealed, published and declared by CONRAD HUBERT, the above named Testator, as and for his LAST WILL AND TESTAMENT, in our presence, and we, at his request, in his presence and in the presence of each other, have hereunto subscribed our names as witnesses. 

Dated, New York, December 23, 1927. 

H. L. Zachman, 8906 89th Avenue, Woodhaven, N. Y.

C. S. Hutcheson, Massapequa, L. I. 

W. W. Stake, 102 East 38th Street, New York City.

LAST WILL AND TESTAMENT OF JAMES A. PATTEN 

I, JAMES A. PATTEN, being of sound and disposing mind and memory, do hereby make, ordain, publish and declare this my Last Will and Testament, hereby revoking any and all former Wills and Testaments and Codicils to Wills by me heretofore made.

FIRST. I will and direct that all of my just debts and funeral expenses be paid. 

SECOND. I give and bequeath to my wife, Amanda Louisa Patten, my wearing apparel, watch and automobiles, and in case of her death before my death, I give and bequeath said articles to my son, John Lowrie Patten. The Evanston home, household furniture, pictures, plate, china, ornaments and all other household articles and family stores belong to my said wife, and I hereby confirm in her the title thereto. [ 177 ]

THIRD. I give and bequeath to the following persons and organizations the following sums respectively: 

To Ralph W. Olmstead, my secretary, the sum of ten thousand dollars ($10,000.00); 

To my chauffeur, Harold E. Greenwood, if he is in my employ at the time of my death, the sum of two thousand dollars ($2,000.00);

To my chauffeur, Charles R. Yous, if in my employ at the time of my death, the sum of two thousand dollars ($2,000.00);

If either of the above named chauffeurs are not in my employ at the time of my death the sum above bequeathed to him shall go into the residuary estate. 

To the First Presbyterian Church of Evanston, Illinois, the sum of fifty thousand dollars ($50,000.00); 

To the Young Men's Christian Association of Evanston, Illinois, the sum of fifty thousand dollars ($50,000.00); 

To the Monmouth College located at Monmouth, Illinois, the sum of fifty thousand dollars ($50,000.00); 

FOURTH. After the payment of the above debts and funeral expenses, and the costs of administration of my estate, and the payment of any inheritance taxes, State or Federal, levied against my estate, or against any of the beneficiaries herein named, and the payment of the above bequests, I give, devise and bequeath all the rest, residue and remainder of my property, real, personal and mixed, of whatever kind and wherever situated, to the Chicago Title and Trust Company, of Chicago, Illinois, in trust nevertheless, for the uses and purposes, and subject to the limitations hereinafter declared and expressed in this, my Last Will and Testament. 

I hereby authorize my executor hereinafter named in its discretion to turn over and transfer to my said trustee in kind any and all of the bonds, stocks or other securities in which the corpus of my estate may be invested at the time of my death. It is my will and wish, and I direct that the title to all of my said estate and the income thereof, and every part thereof, and any and all moneys realized from the sale of any part or all of the same, and the property in which any of said moneys may be invested or reinvested, shall be vested in and remain in my said Trustee until the actual sale, transfer, assignment, or payment thereof by it to the beneficiaries hereafter named in this, my Last Will and Testament, in the manner hereinafter indicaetd, and that the same shall not be subject to any debt or debts which may exist or hereafter be created against any or all of said beneficiaries. I will and direct that my said Trustee shall take possession of my said estate, and hold, manage and distribute the same. It shall, at all times, collect all rents, profits, interest, dividends and all income of whatever kind arising from said estate, and it may compromise debts due my estate. It shall, at all times, make all necessary repairs, pay all taxes, assessments, insurance premiums, and make any and all other payments necessary and proper in and about the said estate, and the management thereof. It shall have full power to sell and convey any or all of the real estate at any time forming a part of my trust estate, if it deems it for the best interests of my said estate, either for cash or partly for cash and the balance on deferred payments, with proper security, and to execute and deliver the necessary deeds of conveyance there for; also at any time, to lease all or any part of my real estate for such periods up to, but not exceeding, ninety-nine (99) years as it may deem best (any such term to begin either in praesenti or in futuro), and to execute and deliver the necessary leases; to amend or modify, from time to time, any lease of any part of said trust estate; also to encumber any part or all of the real estate at any time forming part of said trust estate, if, in its judgment, the best interests of my estate demand it, and to execute and deliver all instruments in, writing necessary therefor; also to sell, assign and dispose of any or all choses in action, stocks, bonds, mortgages and personal property of whatsoever name or nature, to my said trust estate belonging; except that if at the time of my death I own any stock in 

Bartlett Frazier Co., an Illinois corporation, I direct that the same be not sold for a period of five (5) years after my death unless the said corporation itself desires to buy it previous to that date or by resolution direct its sale and transfer to some other person acceptable to its Board of Directors, at a price which may be deemed fair by my said trustee; to collect or sell any securities, at any time belonging to said trust estate, and to give all proper receipts and releases therefor. It shall have full power in its discretion, to improve any portion of the real estate held by it as trustee hereunder, by the erection of buildings thereon, to add to or change, or to demolish and rebuild the improvements on such of the real estate held in trust as is already improved; to replace improvements that may be destroyed by fire, or otherwise, or which may have become obsolete, and it may, for any of the purposes above mentioned, use a part of the principal of my estate. It shall have full power to invest and reinvest the principal arising from the sale of any of the real or personal property belonging to my estate, and it may make such subsequent sales and invest and reinvest the principal derived from such sales from time to time, as in its judgment, the best interests of my estate require, such investment or reinvestment to be in good interest bearing or dividend paying securities or stocks, except that no loans shall be made on property used exclusively for dwelling house, flat or apartment building purposes, and it may also purchase, invest and reinvest in real estate (except dwelling house, flat and apartment building property) and particularly said trustee may invest in real estate encumbered by ground leases originally providing for terms not exceeding one hundred and ninety-eight (198) years. It shall have full power in its discretion to continue to hold any or all bonds, stocks, or other securities in which the corpus of my estate may be invested at the time of my death as a part of the investments of my trust estate, or it may sell the same, or any part thereof, when it deems best for my estate, and I hereby release and absolve said Trustee from any liability for any loss that may be suffered from holding such securities. It shall have power to employ a person or persons to assist it in the management of said estate, and to pay to such person or persons proper compensation for such services. It is my intention hereby to give to my said Trustee, subject to the aforesaid exceptions, full and absolute power to hold, manage, invest and distribute my said estate, and any and all powers necessary or convenient for that purpose, whether such powers are hereinbefore specifically set forth or not, are hereby given to it. I expressly will and direct that persons purchasing from my said Trustee any part or all of the real or personal property to my said trust estate belonging, or which may be acquired for said trust estate through investment or reinvestment, as herein authorized, need not look to, or be responsible for, the application of the purchase money by said purchaser or purchasers paid to my said Trustee. 

FIFTH. I direct that during the life of my wife, my said Trustee pay out and distribute the net income of my said estate as follows: 

Ten thousand dollars ($10,000.00) per annum, in quarterly installments, to my son, John Lowrie Patten. 

Ten thousand dollars ($10,000.00) per annum, in quarterly installments, to my daughter, Agnes Patten Wilder. 

Fifty dollars ($50.00) per month, in monthly installments, to Leontine Carlson, an old family servant. 

The balance of said income it shall pay to my wife, Amanda Louisa Patten, in quarterly installments. In case of the death of either or both of my said children, before the death of my wife, leaving a child or children, issue of his or her body, him or her surviving, I direct that the income hereinabove provided to be paid to any such child of mine so dying as aforesaid, be paid to the child or children of his or her body, share and share alike, and in case either or both of my said children shall die before the death of my wife, leaving no child or children of his or her body, him or her surviving, I direct that said income hereinabove provided to be paid to such deceased child of mine while living, shall be paid to my wife. [178]

SIXTH. At the death of my wife, or in case my wife shall predecease me, then upon my death, I direct that one-half (%) of my trust estate remaining in the hands of my said trustee shall be divided between and distributed to my son, John Lowrie Patten, and my daughter, Agnes Patten Wilder, share and share alike; if either or both of them shall have passed away prior to the death of my wife, leaving issue of his or her body, him or her surviving, I direct that the amount of my trust estate herein directed to be divided and distributed to the parent if living, be divided and distributed to the issue of the body of such parent per stirpes and not per capita; if either of them die before the death of my wife, leaving no issue of his or her body, him or her surviving, I hereby direct that the share hereinabove directed to be paid and distributed to the one of my children so dying be added to the amount hereinafter provided to be distributed between the charities hereinafter named, and distributed between them in the same proportions as one-half of my trust estate is to be distributed between them.

The other half of my said trust estate, at the death of my wife shall be divided by my said trustee into seven equal parts as nearly as may be, one of which parts I give, devise and bequeath and direct my trustee to distribute to the Evanston Hospital Association of Evanston, Illinois; one part to The Presbyterian Home, an Illinois corporation, for the use of the home now located at Evanston, Illinois; one part to The United Charities of Chicago; one part to the Old People's 

Home of the City of Chicago, now located at number 4724 Vincennes Avenue, in the City of Chicago; one part to The 

Visiting Nurse Association of Chicago; one part to the Glenwood Manual Training School of Chicago; and one part to the Chicago Council of the Boy Scouts of America. 

In case any one or more of said charitable organizations mentioned in this "Sixth" Section shall cease to exist or function as a charitable institution at any time before distribution is made, as above directed, I hereby authorize and direct my said Trustee, in its sole discretion, to select some other reliable and responsible incorporated charitable organization or organizations located in or near the City of Chicago, and engaged in similar activities as nearly as may be to the one or more that shall have ceased to exist or function, and to distribute and pay over to the one or more incorporated charitable organizations so selected, the share hereinabove provided for the one or more that shall have ceased to exist or function. 

While in disposing of my estate in and by this, my Last Will and Testament, outside of charitable bequests, and a provision for certain servants, I have confined, and mean to confine, my benefactions to my wife and to those only in lawful direct descending line from me and my body, I do not mean to convey or imply any disapproval whatever of the action either of my said children have taken or may hereafter take in adopting children, but I have taken into consideration the fact that both my son and daughter will have separate property of their own provided for them from other sources than my provisions for them out of which they may adequately provide for such adopted children. 

SEVENTH. The provisions hereinabove made for my wife, Amanda Louisa Patten, are in lieu of homestead, dower rights, widow's award and of all other rights, interests or claims which she may or might have or claim in or to my estate or any part thereof.

EIGHTH. I direct that the income to be paid to the beneficiaries under this, my Last Will and Testament, as well as the distributive share of my estate to be paid to them, when the same is divided and distributed as hereinabove directed, shall be paid, distributed, assigned and conveyed to each of said beneficiaries in person, or to their lawfully appointed guardian or other representative, and that in no event shall any of said beneficiaries or the creditors of any of them, have the right or power to anticipate the share of said estate or its income which my Trustee is to pay over to such beneficiaries, by any order on said Trustee, assignment, conveyance or other voluntary transfer by such beneficiaries, or by operation of law, or by virtue of any attachment, garnishment, judgment, decree or other legal proceedings against such beneficiaries or my said Trustee.

NINTH. I hereby direct that the Chicago Title and Trust Company shall receive and annually account to the distributees for all moneys belonging to or coming to my estate, and shall receive a compensation not exceeding five per cent of the net income that may come into its hands from time to time, in full for its services as Trustee hereunder. 

TENTH. All inheritance taxes under the laws of the United States or of any of the States of the United States which may be in force at the time of my death and which may be levied upon or against my estate or upon or against any annuities, bequests or devises contained in this Will, shall be paid out of the corpus of my estate, and not charged against the bequest or devise to any beneficiary herein. 

ELEVENTH. I hereby direct that in dividing and distributing my estate the Trustee shall have full power to divide and distribute in kind or to sell the whole or a part of my said estate, including real estate, and divide and distribute the proceeds in cash or partly in cash and partly in kind, and that for the purposes of division and distribution, the judgment of the Trustee as to the value to be placed upon the different items of property composing my estate in its hands shall be conclusive and final upon the beneficiaries under this, my Will. 

TWELFTH. I nominate and appoint the Chicago Title and Trust Company, to be the Executor of this, my Last Will and Testament.

THIRTEENTH. In case the said Chicago Title and Trust Company shall, at any time or times hereafter, become consolidated with any other corporation or corporations, or in case at any time hereafter the said Chicago Title and Trust Company shall reorganize or reincorporate, and the corporation so formed shall acquire the assets and succeed to the business of said Chicago Title and Trust Company, then the corporation so formed by such consolidation or consolidations, or the corporation which shall so acquire the assets and succeed to the business of said Chicago Title and Trust Company, shall become the Trustee and also the executor under this, my Will, with the same force and effect and with the same duties, powers, titles, and privileges as if such consolidation or successor corporation had been specifically nominated as such trustee or executor by the express terms of this, my Will. 

FOURTEENTH. In case the said Chicago Title and Trust Company shall decline or refuse to act as such Trustee and 

Executor, or in case it refuses either trust, or in case of the inability of the said Chicago Title and Trust Company, or its successor (as provided for in the next preceding section hereof) to act, I hereby nominate and appoint the First Trust and Savings Bank, of Chicago, Illinois, as such executor and Trustee, and in case it also declines or refuses to act as such Executor or Trustee, I hereby direct, authorize and empower the then presiding judge of the Probate Court of Cook County, Illinois, to appoint some other reliable and responsible Trust Company doing business in the City of Chicago and having a capital and surplus exceeding $1,500,000.00 as Successor in Trust or Executor, and in the event that said 

First Trust and Savings Bank accepts the trust or another Trust Company is appointed as herein directed and accepts the trust, I hereby invest and confer upon such Successor in Trust, all the title to my estate and all the powers herein conveyed or conferred upon the Trustee herein, and direct that it be allowed the same compensation as is provided herein may be paid to the said Chicago Title and Trust Company of Chicago, Illinois. 

IN WITNESS WHEREOF, I have hereunto set my hand and seal this 4th day of June, in the year of Our Lord, Nineteen 

Hundred and Twenty-five.

JAMES A. PATTEN. (Seal) 

The foregoing instrument, consisting of eight (8) pages, including this page, was, on the day of the date thereof, signed, sealed, published and declared by the said James A. Patten, the testator named therein, as and for his Last Will [179]

and Testament, in the presence of us, who, at his request, and in his presence, and in the presence of each other, have hereunto subscribed our names as witnesses thereto, the said Testator having also written his name in full on the margin of each page except the last one, in our presence. 

Date of Death-December 8, 1928. 

Address: 

Frank E. Lord, 105 So. La Salle St., Chicago. 

James M. Sheldon, 120 West Adams St., Chicago, Ill. 

Joseph E. Paden, 175 W. Jackson Blvd., Chicago, Ill. 

Will Proved and Admitted to Record in Open Court, December 20, 1928.

THE LAST WILL AND TESTAMENT OF JOHN G. SHEDD 

I, JOHN G. SHEDD, of Chicago, Illinois, being of sound and disposing mind and memory, do make, publish and declare this as and for my Last Will and Testament, hereby revoking all other wills and codicils thereto by me at any time heretofore made, that is to say:

FIRST. I direct that all my just debts and all my funeral expenses shall be paid as soon after my death as may be convenient, and that all valid estate transfer and inheritance taxes, whether imposed by the Government of the United States, or by the government of any State or of any foreign country, on or in respect to any of my property or any devise or bequest herein contained, shall be treated in all respects as if they were debts for which I was liable at the time of my death, and shall be paid out of my general estate before my property is distributed as herein provided, and that all such taxes when thus paid shall not in any manner or in any event whatsoever be charged against or deducted from any such devise or bequest, nor shall the devisee or legatee in such devise or bequest be under any obligation or liability whatsoever to reimburse my Executor or Executors or any beneficiary or beneficiaries hereunder for the same or for any part thereof. 

SECOND. I give and bequeath to Ruben DeLaunty, my faithful and skilled chauffeur for many years, if he shall survive me and shall be at the time of my death still in my employ, the sum of Five Thousand Dollars ($5,000.00), but if he shall not survive me, but shall have been in my employ at the time of his death, then I give said sum to the wife of said Ruben DeLaunty, if she shall survive me; otherwise said sum shall become a part of my residuary estate. 

THIRD. I give and bequeath to Patrick Brittain, now residing at 955 South Orange Grove Avenue, Pasadena, California, and for many years my faithful employee, if he shall survive me, the sum of Five Thousand Dollars ($5,000.00), but if he shall not survive me, then I give said sum to his wife, Maggie, if she shall survive me; otherwise said sum shall become a part of my residuary estate. 

FOURTH. I give and bequeath to William Becker, if he shall be in my employ at 955 South Orange Grove Avenue, Pasadena, California, at the time of my death, the sum of One Thousand Dollars ($1,000.00).

FIFTH. I give and bequeath to each person who at the time of my death is in my employment at my house in Chicago, and who has then been in my personal employment for five years or more, the sum of One Thousand Dollars ($1,000.00). 

SIXTH. I give and bequeath to my nephew, Harrison Porter Shedd, if he shall survive me, the sum of Twenty-five 

Thousand Dollars ($25,000.00).

SEVENTH. I give and bequeath to my grandnephew, John G. Shedd II., if he shall survive me, the sum of Twentyfive Thousand Dollars ($25,000.00).

EIGHTH. I give and bequeath to my nephew, Herman G. Shedd, now residing near Boone, Iowa, to him and to his heirs forever, the sum of Ten Thousand Dollars ($10,000.00).

NINTH. I give and bequeath to my niece, Dora Shedd Bennett, now residing at Fairbury, Illinois, if she shall survive me, the sum of Ten Thousand Dollars ($10,000.00).

TENTH. I give and bequeath to my nephew, Arthur W. Shedd, now residing at or near Galion, Alabama, the sum of 

Twenty Thousand Dollars ($20,000.00), to him and to his heirs forever.

ELEVENTH. I give and bequeath to my niece, Edith Shedd Sparling, now residing at Demopolis, Alabama, if she shall survive me, the sum of Fifteen Thousand Dollars ($15,000.00), but if she shall not survive me, but shall leave lawful issue surviving me, then I give and bequeath said sum to such lawful issue me surviving; otherwise said sum shall become a part of my residuary estate.

TWELFTH. I give and bequeath to my niece, Alice Shedd Randall, now residing at Rensselaer, Indiana, to her and to her heirs forever, the sum of Fifteen Thousand Dollars ($15,000.00).

THIRTEENTH. I give and bequeath to my niece, Harriette Shedd, residing at Rensselaer, Indiana, if she shall survive me, the sum of Fifteen Thousand Dollars ($15,000.00); but if she shall not survive me, but shall leave lawful issue her and me surviving, then I give and bequeath said sum to such lawful issue; otherwise said sum shall become a part of my residuary estate.

FOURTEENTH. I give and bequeath to my grandniece, M. Emeline Graves McGuffie, now residing in Los Angeles, California, if she shall survive me, the sum of Twenty-five Hundred Dollars ($2500.00).

FIFTEENTH. I give and bequeath to my grandniece, Sadie Graves Main, if she shall survive me, the sum of Twentyfive Hundred Dollars ($2500.00).

SIXTEENTH. I give and bequeath to my grandnephew, Bertice Graves, if he shall survive me, the sum of Twenty-five 

Hundred Dollars ($2500.00); if he shall not survive me, but shall leave lawful issue me surviving, then I give and bequeath said sum of Twenty-five Hundred Dollars ($2500.00) to his lawful issue me surviving.

SEVENTEENTH. Our home in Chicago, known as No. 1550 North State Parkway, Fourth Apartment, both the owner ship of said apartment and the leasehold thereof, and all the contents, equipment, furnishings and other personal property therein and thereunto belonging and all my personal effects and all automobiles and the equipment therefor, I give, devise, and bequeath to my beloved wife, Mary R. Shedd, for her sole use and benefit during her natural life, together with full power to use, enjoy and dispose of the same; and upon her death, I give, devise and bequeath the same or so much thereof as shall then be remaining, to my beloved daughter, Helen M. Shedd Reed, to her and to her heirs forever. 

EIGHTEENTH. I give and bequeath to my son-in-law, Charles H. Schweppe, the sum of Two Hundred Thousand Dollars ($200,000.00), to him and to his heirs forever; and I give and bequeath to my son-in-law, Kersey Coates Reed, the sum of 

Two Hundred Thousand Dollars ($200,000.00), to him and to his heirs forever. 

NINETEENTH. I give and bequeath, as a loving remembrance, the sum of Twenty-five Thousand Dollars ($25,000.00) to each of my grandchildren, Mary Shedd Reed, John Shedd Reed, Jean Shedd Schweppe and John Shedd Schweppe. In case any one of my said grandchildren shall be under legal age at the time of my death, the amount herein bequeathed to him or her shall be paid over to and held by his or her father (or if he be dead, to his or her mother) as Trustee, until he or she becomes of legal age, and on his or her becoming of legal age shall be paid over to him or her. [180]

TWENTIETH. Our winter home at Pasadena, California, being the premises described as 955 South Orange Grove 

Avenue, both the real estate and the contents of the house are now owned in joint tenancy by my said wife and me. In the event that I survive my said wife, I give, devise and bequeath said real estate and the house and all other buildings and improvements thereon and all equipment, furnishings and other personal property in said house, to my beloved daughter, Laura A. Shedd Schweppe, to her and to her heirs forever. Said real estate is described as follows: 

All that portion of the 25.78 acre tract marked "Matthews" in Division "E" of the SanGabriel Orange Grove 

Association Lands, as per map recorded in Book 2, page 556, et seq., Miscellaneous Records of said County, described as follows:

Beginning at a point in the present west line of Orange Grove Avenue (as widened to 100 feet), said point being 1.25 feet north (along Orange Grove Avenue) from the intersection of the south line of said 25.78 acre tract marked "Matthews" with said west line of Orange Grove Avenue; thence west along the north line of land conveyed to Arthur H. Fleming by deed recorded in Book 2392, page 179, of Deeds, Records of said County, 3.9 feet; thence northerly parallel with Oange Grove Avenue 1.87 feet; thence west parailel with the south line of said 25.78 acre tract, 479.9 feet; thence south three feet to the southwest corner of the land conveyed to Arthur H.

Fleming hereinbefore referred to; thence westerly along the south line of said 25.78 acre tract, 1071 feet, more or less, to the east line of Grand Avenue, formerly Arroyo Drive, as located on map above referred to; thence northerly along and with the east line of said Grand Avenue, formerly Arroyo Drive, 306.24 feet, more or less, to the south line of the land conveyed to Lydia M. Baker by deed from Eurietta A. Croft and Thomas F. Croft, her husband, recorded in Book 108, page 311, of Deeds, Records of said County; thence east along the south line of the land so conveyed to Lydia M. Baker, 1574.92 feet, more or less, to the west line of Orange Grove Avenue; thence southerly along said west line of Orange Grove Avenue, 304.33 feet, more or less, to the point of beginning; 

Together with all and singular the tenements, hereditaments, easements, rights, privileges and appurtenances thereunto belonging, or in anywise appertaining, and the reversion and reversions, remainder and remainders, rents, issues and profits thereof; and together also with those portions of Grand Avenue, formerly Arroyo Drive, and 

Orange Grove Avenue immediately adjoining and adjacent to the premises hereinbefore described. 

I also give, devise and bequeath to my beloved daughter, Laura A. Shedd Schweppe, my garage known as 1732 (North) 

Park Avenue, in the City of Chicago, State of Illinois, to her and to her heirs forever. 

TWENTY-FIRST. It is my will, and I especially direct and request my Executors that they pay the foregoing cash bequests in such manner and at such times as may not embarrass the proper and economic administration of my estate. 

TWENTY-SECOND. From time to time during my life I have made what I have regarded as appropriate contributions to charitable objects. By a contract between the Shedd Aquarium Society, an Illinois corporation, and myself, I have provided for the establishment of an Aquarium in the City of Chicago, and I direct that said contract shall be binding upon my Executors.

TWENTY-THIRD. All the rest, residue and remainder of my estate, whether real, personal or mixed, of whatever kind and nature and wherever situate (including all lapsed legacies of personal property and lapsed devises of real estate), to my beloved daughters, Laura A. Shedd Schweppe and Helen M. Shedd Reed, equally, share and share alike, to them and to their heirs forever.

TWENTY-FOURTH. I hereby appoint as the Executors of this my Will, my said two daughters, Laura A. Shedd Schweppe and Helen M. Shedd Reed, and my said two sons-in-law, Charles H. Schweppe and Kersey Coates Reed, and the Illinois 

Merchants Trust Company, a corporation of Illinios, and request that no bond be required of them, or of any of them, as such Executors.

TWENTY-FIFTH. I hereby nominate the Pacific-Southwest Trust & Savings Bank, Los Angeles, California, as Executor of my California estate, and request that the Pasadena Branch of said Pacific-Southwest Trust & Savings Bank, serve as such Executor.

TWENTY-SIXTH. I authorize and empower my said Executors, to sell all or any part of my real or personal estate at public auction or by private sale, for cash or on credit, or partly for cash and partly on credit, and to execute and deliver all good and sufficient deeds, bills of sale, instruments of transfer and any and all other writings necessary or proper to convey and transfer the property so sold. I direct that the receipts of my said Executors, for the purchase money and proceeds of any property herein authorized to be sold, or for any money or for any property of any kind, paid or transferred to them, shall fully discharge the person or persons so purchasing the property so sold, or so paying said monies or so transferring said other property therefrom and from being bound to see to the application or being answerable for the loss or misapplication thereof.

IN WITNESS WHEREOF, I have set my hand and seal to this my Last Will and Testament, consisting of eight pages, including this page, on the margin of each of which (except this page) I have attached my signature this 25th day of September, in the year of our Lord One Thousand Nine Hundred and Twenty-five (A. D. 1925).

JOHN G. SHEDD (Seal) 

WE HEREBY CERTIFY that the foregoing instrument was on the day of the date thereof, signed, sealed, published and declared by John G. Shedd, the Testator, as and for his Last Will and Testament, in our sight and presence, and that we, at his request and in his sight and presence, and in the sight and presence of each of us, have subscribed our names hereto as witnesses of the execution thereof, this 25th day of September, A. D. 1925.

WILL OF FESTUS J. WADE 

Thomas H. Eddy, residing at Evanston, Ills. 

John E. MacLeish, residing at Winnetka, Ills. 

Horace H. Martin, residing at Lake Forest, Ills.

I, FESTUS J. WADE, a married man, now residing at No. 4532 Lindell Boulevard, in the City of St. Louis, Missouri, do make, publish and declare this instrument to be my last will and testament, hereby revoking all former wills and codicils by me at any time heretofore made. 

ITEM ONE: I direct the payment of all my just debts and also my funeral expenses.

ITEM Two: I leave nothing to charity or philanthropy in this my will, because for the past forty years I have distributed more than ten per cent of my income for charitable and philanthropic purposes. 

ITEM THREE: Unto my son, Festus J. Wade, Jr., I give and bequeath my library and jewelry.

ITEM FOUR: To preserve my present residence and its furnishings, or the residence in which I may be living at the time of my death, as a home for my wife, Kate V. Wade, during her life, I give, bequeath and devise my residence, No. 

4532 Lindell Boulevard, St. Louis Missouri, or the residence owned by me at the time of my death, together with the ground appurtenant thereto, and the outbuildings thereon, and also all my household and kitchen furniture, and all the [181]

appointments and furnishings of the home, either useful or ornamental, with the exception of the books constituting my library, unto the Mercantile Trust Company, a corporation of the City of St. Louis, Missouri, in trust, however, with the same powers, in reference to control, management and disposition, as are given to my Trustee in the trust created for the benefit of my wife, Kate V. Wade, under Item Five, clause "d," paragraph one of this my will. (1) It is my will and I direct that my wife, Kate V. Wade, shall be permitted to occupy said residence and to use the furniture or furnishings therein, for and during the period of her natural life, without any obligation to pay rent; the cost of maintenance of said residence to be paid by her. In the event my wife should desire to change her residence, my Trustee, upon her request, is authorized, empowered and directed to sell my residence and/or the furniture or furnishings therein, in the way and upon the terms that it shall deem best. The wishes of my wife in regard to disposing of the property and/or the contents of the house shall be evidenced by her joining in the deed to the real estate and the execution of any instrument which may be required to transfer title to the personal property. Should the said residence and/or the contents thereof be sold during the lifetime of my wife, then, and in that event, I direct my Trustee to pay over, deliver and convey the entire net proceeds of the sale of the said residence and/or the contents thereof, in equal shares, unto my wife, Kate_V. 

Wade, my daughters, Stella Wade Warren, Marie Louise Thomas and Florence J. Wade, and my son, Festus J. Wade, Jr., and/or unto the survivors or survivor of them, the descendants of a deceased child, however, to receive the parent's share per stirpes and not per capita; provided always, however, that the shares of my wife, Kate V. Wade, and my daughter, Florence J. Wade, shall pass into and become a part of the respective trusts herein created for their benefit. (2) Should my residence not be sold during the lifetime of my wife, Kate V. Wade, then, from and after her death, my daughter, Florence J. Wade, shall be permitted to occupy said residence and to use the furniture or furnishings therein, for and during the period of her natural life, without any obligation to pay rent; the cost of maintenance of said residence to be paid by her. In the event my said daughter should desire to change her residence, my Trustee, upon her request, is authorized, empowered and directed to sell my residence and/or the furniture or furnishings therein, in the way and upon the terms that it shall deem best. The wishes of my said daughter in regard to disposing of the property and/or the contents of the house shall be evidenced by her joining in the deed to the real estate and the execution of any instrument which may be required to transfer title to the personal property. Should the said residence and/or the contents thereof be sold during the lifetime of my said daughter, then, and in that event, I direct my Trustee to pay over, deliver and convey the entire proceeds of the sale of said residence and/or the contents thereof, in equal shares, unto my daughters, Stella Wade Warren, Marie Louise Thomas and Florence J. Wade, and my son, Festus J. Wade, Jr., and/or unto the survivors or survivor of them, the descendants of a deceased child, however, to receive the parent's share per stirpes and not per capita; provided always, however, that the share of my daughter, Florence J. Wade, shall pass into and become a part of the trust herein created for her benefit. (3) Should the said residence and its contents not be sold during the lives of my wife, Kate V. Wade, and my daughter, Florence J. Wade, then, and in that event, upon the death of the survivor of them, the trust herein created shall cease and terminate, and said residence and the contents thereof shall be delivered and conveyed, in equal shares, unto my children, Stella Wade Warren, Marie Louise Thomas and Festus J. Wade, Jr., and unto the descendants of my daughter,

Florence J. Wade, per stirpes, and/or unto the survivors or survivor of them, the descendants of a deceased child, however, to receive the parent's share per stirpes and not per capita. 

ITEM FIVE: All the rest, residue and remainder of my estate, of whatsoever kind, real and personal, and wherever situated at the time of my death, I give, bequeath and devise, as follows: (a) One-fifth (1/5) thereof unto my daughter, Stella Wade Warren, absolutely, or, in the event of her prior death, unto her descendants, in equal shares per stirpes and not per capita. 

Should the said Stella Wade Warren predecease me, however, leaving no descendants her surviving, then, and in that event, said one-fifth (1/5) of my estate shall pass to my wife and my surviving children, in equal shares, the descendants of a deceased child, however, to receive the parent's share per stirpes and not per capita; provided always, however, that the shares passing to my wife, Kate V. Wade, and my daughter, Florence J. Wade, shall pass into and become a part of the respective trusts herein created for them. (b) One-fifth (1/5) thereof unto my daughter, Marie Louise Thomas, absolutely, or, in the event of her prior death, unto her descendants, in equal shares per stirpes and not per capita. 

Should the said Marie Louise Thomas predecease me, however, leaving no descendants her surviving, then, and in that event, said one-fifth (1/5) of my estate shall pass to my wife and my surviving gchildren, in equal shares, the descendants of a deceased child, however, to receive the parent's share per stirpes and not per capita; provided always, however, that the shares passing to my wife, Kate V. Wade, and my daughter, Florence J. Wade, shall pass into and become a part of the respective trusts herein created for them. (c) One-fifth (1/5) thereof unto my son, Festus J. Wade, Jr., absolutely, or, in the event of his prior death, unto his discendants, in equal shares per stirpes and not per capita. 

Should the said Festus J. Wade, Jr., predecease me, however, leaving no descendants him surviving, then, and in that event, said one-fifth (1/5) of my estate shall pass to my wife and my surviving children, in equal shares, the descendants of a deceased child, however, to receive the parent's share per stirpes and not per capita; provided always, however, that the shares passing to my wife, Kate V. Wade, and my daughter, Florence J. Wade, shall pass into and become a part of the respective trusts herein created for them.
