(d) One-fifth (1/5) thereof unto the Mercantile Trust Company, a corporation of the City of St. Louis, Missouri, in trust, however, for the following uses and purposes, that is to say: (1) To manage and control the same, with full power and authority hereby conferred upon said Trustee, to sell and dispose of any and all real and personal property, stocks and bonds and other securities forming a part of said trust estate at any time, and to convey and transfer the same at such time, and upon such terms, and for such consideration as to it shall seem meet and proper, and to invest and reinvest the proceeds thereof, and all moneys forming part of the trust estate, in such property, real or personal, and in such manner, and in such securities as to it shall seem meet and proper, giving and granting unto it full discretionary power in relation to such investments. 

And I do hereby also give and grant unto it, full power and authority, from time to time, to improve and to make and grant leases of any of the real estate forming part of the trust estate, for such terms or periods, not exceeding ninetynine (99) years, and for such rent or consideration in each case, as to it shall seem proper. (2) After paying the necessary charges and expenses incurred in the management and investment of the trust estate, I direct my Trustee to pay over the net income and revenue derived therefrom, in monthly or other convenient installments, unto my wife, Kate V. Wade, for and during the period of her natural life. (3) Upon the death of my wife, Kate V. Wade, the trust herein created for her benefit shall cease and terminate, and thereupon I direct my Trustee to pay over, deliver and convey the entire balance of the trust estate held hereunder for her benefit, in equal shares, unto my children, Stella Wade Warren, Marie Louise Thomas, Florence J. Wade and Festus J. [182]

Wade, Jr., and/or unto the survivors or survivor of them, the descendants of a deceased child, however, to receive the parent's share per stirpes and not per capita; provided always, however, that the share of my daughter, Florence J. Wade, shall pass into and become a part of the trust herein created for her benefit. (e) One-fifth (1/5) thereof unto the Mercantile Trust Company, a corporation of the City of St. Louis, Missouri, in trust, however, with all of the powers in regard to control, management and disposition which I have given to my Trustee in paragraph one, clause "d" of this item (five) of my will. (1) After paying the necessary charges and expenses incurred in the management of the trust estate, I direct my Trustee to pay over the net income and revenue derived therefrom, in monthly or other convenient installments, unto my daughter, Florence J. Wade, for and during the period of her natural life. (2) Upon the death of my daughter, Florence J. Wade, the trust herein created for her benefit shall cease and teminate, and thereupon I direct my Trustee to pay over, deliver and convey the entire balance of the trust estate held hereunder for her benefit, in equal shares, per stirpes and not per capita, unto her descendants. (3) Should my daughter, Florence J. Wade, leave no descendants her surviving, then, and in that event, upon her death, I direct my Trustee to pay over, deliver and convey the entire balance of the trust estate held hereunder for her benefit, in equal shares, unto my wife, Kate V. Wade, and unto my children, Stella Wade Warren, Marie Louise Thomas and

Festus J. Wade, Jr., or unto the survivors or survivor of them, the descendants of a deceased child, however, to receive the parent's share per stirpes and not per capita; provided always, however, that the share of my wife, Kate V. Wade, shall pass into and become a part of the trust herein created for her benefit. (f) In the event of need, occasioned by sickness or misfortune, of which said need my Trustee shall be the sole judge, I authorize it to use so much of the principal, in addition to the income herein provided to be paid unto my wife, Kate V. 

Wade, and unto my daughter, Florence J. Wade, as, in its sole opinion, may be necessary for the relief of the beneficiary in need. Any advancements shall be charged to the trust held hereunder for the benefit of the beneficiary for whom the advance was made. (g) I authorize my Trustee to treat all extraordinary cash dividends as entirely income, also to treat as part of my trust estates income, dividends paid to my representatives after my death, irrespective of the date of the declaration of such dividends, in order that the beneficiaries of the trusts may enjoy the full amount of income, regardless of the fact that such dividends might possibly encroach upon the principal of the trusts. (h) My Trustee shall have the power to determine whether any money or property coming into its hands shall be considered part of the principal of said trust estates or a part of the income therefrom, and to apportion between such principal and income any loss or expenditure in connection with such trust estates, which in its opinion should be apportioned, and as to it may seem just and equitable. (i) I hereby direct that neither my wife, Kate V. Wade, nor my daughter, Florence J. Wade, nor any of her descendants, shall have any right or power to sell, assign, convey, mortgage, pledge, anticipate or hypothecate, or otherwise dispose of any right, title or interest which any of them may acquire under or by virtue of this my will, during the continuance of the trusts herein created. (j) The payments of income hereunder shall commence on the twentieth day of the second month after my death. (k) I direct that the income to be paid to my daughter, Florence J. Wade, hereunder, shall be free from the control of any husband she may have.

ITEM SIX: The provisions made for my wife, Kate V. Wade, by the terms of this my will, are intended by me to be in lieu of dower in my estate, and in lieu of any and all other rights and claims that she may have to or upon my estate, or any part thereof, under the laws of the State of Missouri. 

ITEM SEVEN: I direct that all inheritance, estate, transfer or succession taxes assessed by the United States Government, or any State of the Union, shall be paid out of my residuary estate. 

ITEM EIGHT: In the event any of my descendants are minors at the time they shall be entitled to receive a portion of my estate, then I nominate and appoint the Mercantile Trust Company, a corporation of the City of St. Louis, Missouri, the Guardian of such minors.

ITEM NINE: I hereby nominate and appoint the Mercantile Trust Company, a corporation of the City of St. Louis, Missouri, the Executor of this my last will and testament; and I give unto it as such Executor the same powers and authority, with reference to control, management and disposition of my estate, that I have given unto it as Trustee herein. 

I hereby declare that at and before the execution of this will, I received advice and counsel in relation thereto from someone not under salary from said Mercantile Trust Company. 

ITEM TEN: I have had every opportunity, over a period of years, to know the manner in which the Mercantile Trust 

Company manages estates and trusts for others. I have full confidence in the capacity and integrity of the company to manage my estate judiciously and capably, after my death. It is for that reason I have named the Mercantile Trust Čompany as my sole Executor and Trustee. I recommend that my wife and children defer to its judgment in all matters connected with the administration of my estate. 

IN WITNESS WHEREOF, I have hereunto set my hand at St. Louis, Missouri, this fifth day of August 1927, A. D. 1927.

FESTUS J. WADE. 

The foregoing instrument, consisting of nine pages, all signed by the testator, was signed, published and declared by the above named testator, FESTUS J. WADE, as and to be his last will and testament, in our presence, who, at his request, and in his presence, and in the presence of each other, have hereunto subscribed our names as attesting witnesses thereto, the day and year above written. 

State of Missouri, City of St. Louis, ss.

Geo. J. Shea 

John Joseph Johnston 

F. J. Oltman.

Be it Remembered, That on this 3rd day of October, A. D. 1927, before me, the undersigned Clerk of the Probate 

Court of the City of St. Louis, personally came John Joseph Johnston and Fred J. Oltman who being both by me duly sworn on their oaths, say: "We saw Festus J. Wade, the testator, subscribe his name to the annexed instrument in writing, bearing date the 5th day of August, 1927, and heard him declare the same to be his last Will and Testament; we subscribed our names thereto as witnesses in the presence and at the request of the said testator and at the time of the execution of said instrument as aforesaid, and of our subscribing the same as such witnesses, he, the said testator, was of sound and disposing mind, to the best of our knowledge and belief." 

Sworn to and subscribed before me, this 3rd day of October, 1927. 

John Joseph Johnston 

Fred J. Oltman. 

George Brand, Clerk. By Louis C. Kreh, Deputy Clerk. [183]
